## 3.4 `ml/model/MlOutputs.kt`

All output DTOs for M1–M13, kept in one file to avoid fragmenting the contract surface.

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.model

import android.graphics.Bitmap

// ---- M1 ----
data class DecodabilityScore(
    val score: Float,                 // [0,1], 1 = very likely decodable
    val blurEstimateSigmaPx: Float,
    val contrastEstimate: Float,
    val occlusionEstimate: Float,     // [0,1], 1 = heavily occluded
) {
    val isWorthDecoding: Boolean get() = score >= 0.55f
}

// ---- M2 ----
data class AperiodicTileDetection(
    val centroidX: Float,             // normalised [0,1] in frame
    val centroidY: Float,
    val orientationRadians: Float,
    val scaleFraction: Float,         // tile size as fraction of frame min-dim
    val confidence: Float,
    val vertexXs: FloatArray,         // Spectre tile vertices, normalised
    val vertexYs: FloatArray,
    val clusterLevel: Int,            // for hierarchical template matching
) {
    override fun equals(other: Any?): Boolean {
        if (this === other) return true
        if (other !is AperiodicTileDetection) return false
        return centroidX == other.centroidX &&
            centroidY == other.centroidY &&
            orientationRadians == other.orientationRadians &&
            scaleFraction == other.scaleFraction &&
            confidence == other.confidence &&
            vertexXs.contentEquals(other.vertexXs) &&
            vertexYs.contentEquals(other.vertexYs) &&
            clusterLevel == other.clusterLevel
    }

    override fun hashCode(): Int {
        var result = centroidX.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + centroidY.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + orientationRadians.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + scaleFraction.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + confidence.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + vertexXs.contentHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + vertexYs.contentHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + clusterLevel
        return result
    }
}

// ---- M3 ----
data class SuperResolutionResult(
    val upscaled: Bitmap,
    val scaleFactor: Int,
    val confidence: Float,
    val decodePreserved: Boolean,     // true if decode-in-the-loop loss passed
)

// ---- M5 ----
data class SoftSymbolProbabilities(
    val probabilities: FloatArray,    // length = alphabet size
    val alphabetSize: Int,
    val entropy: Float,               // normalised [0,1], 1 = uniform
    val topSymbolIndex: Int,
    val topSymbolConfidence: Float,
    val classificationTimeMicros: Long,
) {
    init {
        require(probabilities.size == alphabetSize) {
            "probabilities length ${probabilities.size} != alphabetSize $alphabetSize"
        }
    }

    override fun equals(other: Any?): Boolean {
        if (this === other) return true
        if (other !is SoftSymbolProbabilities) return false
        return probabilities.contentEquals(other.probabilities) &&
            alphabetSize == other.alphabetSize &&
            entropy == other.entropy &&
            topSymbolIndex == other.topSymbolIndex &&
            topSymbolConfidence == other.topSymbolConfidence &&
            classificationTimeMicros == other.classificationTimeMicros
    }

    override fun hashCode(): Int {
        var result = probabilities.contentHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + alphabetSize
        result = 31 * result + entropy.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + topSymbolIndex
        result = 31 * result + topSymbolConfidence.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + classificationTimeMicros.hashCode()
        return result
    }
}

// ---- M7 ----
data class FountainPolicy(
    val distributionId: Int,          // index into the firmware's degree-distribution bank
    val targetOverhead: Float,        // e.g. 1.25
    val recommendedSegmentBytes: Int,
    val confidence: Float,
)

// ---- M8 ----
data class FaceTrackState(
    val massBoundingBox: FloatArray,  // [left, top, right, bottom] normalised
    val visibleFaceIds: List<Int>,
    val visibleFaceProbabilities: FloatArray,
    val rotationPhaseRadians: Float,
    val angularVelocityRadPerSec: Float,
    val blurSigmaByFace: FloatArray,
    val trackingConfidence: Float,
) {
    override fun equals(other: Any?): Boolean {
        if (this === other) return true
        if (other !is FaceTrackState) return false
        return massBoundingBox.contentEquals(other.massBoundingBox) &&
            visibleFaceIds == other.visibleFaceIds &&
            visibleFaceProbabilities.contentEquals(other.visibleFaceProbabilities) &&
            rotationPhaseRadians == other.rotationPhaseRadians &&
            angularVelocityRadPerSec == other.angularVelocityRadPerSec &&
            blurSigmaByFace.contentEquals(other.blurSigmaByFace) &&
            trackingConfidence == other.trackingConfidence
    }

    override fun hashCode(): Int {
        var result = massBoundingBox.contentHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + visibleFaceIds.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + visibleFaceProbabilities.contentHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + rotationPhaseRadians.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + angularVelocityRadPerSec.hashCode()
        result = 31 * result + blurSigmaByFace.contentHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + trackingConfidence.hashCode()
        return result
    }
}

// ---- M9 ----
data class DeblurResult(
    val deblurred: Bitmap,
    val estimatedKernelEnergyReduction: Float,
    val confidence: Float,
)

// ---- M12 ----
data class PrintDefectReport(
    val isPrintable: Boolean,
    val defects: List<PrintDefect>,
    val overallConfidence: Float,
)

data class PrintDefect(
    val type: PrintDefectType,
    val severity: Float,              // [0,1]
    val boundingBoxNormalised: FloatArray,
)

enum class PrintDefectType {
    INK_BLEED,
    COLOR_MISREGISTRATION,
    MISSING_TILE,
    SMUDGE,
    FOLD_CREASE,
    SPECULAR_HOTSPOT,
    UNKNOWN,
}
```

## 3.5 `ml/ModelLoader.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml

import android.content.Context
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.ModelStatus
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Responsible for:
 *  1. Verifying that a model asset exists and is non-empty.
 *  2. Copying the asset to a memory-mappable cache file when required by
 *     LiteRT's [CompiledModel] (it cannot read directly from assets on all
 *     API levels without a file descriptor).
 *  3. Caching status so repeated loads are cheap.
 *  4. Exposing a ZOI asset handle for the native Rust path.
 *
 * This class deliberately does not create interpreters. Model wrappers own
 * their own interpreter lifecycle so that GPU delegate warm-up and ZOI
 * memory mapping can be tuned per model.
 */
@Singleton
class ModelLoader @Inject constructor(
    private val context: Context,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) {

    private val statusCache = ConcurrentHashMap<MlModelId, ModelStatus>()
    private val assetMutex = Mutex()
    private val cachedFiles = ConcurrentHashMap<MlModelId, File>()

    fun listMissingModels(): List<String> {
        val missing = mutableListOf<String>()
        MlModelId.entries.forEach { id ->
            if (!assetExists(id.assetName)) missing += id.assetName
        }
        return missing
    }

    fun assetExists(assetName: String): Boolean = runCatching {
        context.assets.open("models/$assetName").use { it.available() > 0 }
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    fun status(id: MlModelId): ModelStatus =
        statusCache[id] ?: ModelStatus.NotLoaded

    fun recordStatus(id: MlModelId, status: ModelStatus) {
        statusCache[id] = status
        when (status) {
            is ModelStatus.Loaded ->
                logger.i(TAG, "${id.displayName} loaded on ${status.accelerator.displayName} " +
                    "in ${status.loadTimeMillis} ms")
            is ModelStatus.Failed ->
                logger.e(TAG, "${id.displayName} failed: ${status.reason}")
            ModelStatus.NotLoaded -> Unit
        }
    }

    /**
     * Returns a stable File path for a LiteRT model. Copies from assets on
     * first use. The returned file is suitable for
     * `CompiledModel.create(file, options)` or `Interpreter(file, options)`.
     */
    suspend fun materialize(id: MlModelId): File = assetMutex.withLock {
        cachedFiles[id]?.let { if (it.exists() && it.length() > 0) return it }
        val out = File(context.cacheDir, "prism_models/${id.assetName}")
        out.parentFile?.mkdirs()
        context.assets.open("models/${id.assetName}").use { input ->
            out.outputStream().use { output -> input.copyTo(output) }
        }
        require(out.length() > 0) { "Materialised model ${id.assetName} is empty" }
        cachedFiles[id] = out
        out
    }

    /**
     * Returns the raw bytes of a zero-overhead model. ZOI models are small
     * enough (typically < 2 MB) that holding them in native memory is
     * cheap; the Rust side maps them once and reuses across calls.
     */
    suspend fun readBytes(id: MlModelId): ByteArray = assetMutex.withLock {
        context.assets.open("models/${id.assetName}").use { it.readBytes() }
    }

    /**
     * Preferred accelerator ladder. NNAPI is intentionally absent:
     * it was deprecated in Android 15 and LiteRT's GPU delegate plus
     * XNNPACK CPU fallback is the supported path.
     */
    fun acceleratorLadder(id: MlModelId): List<Accelerator> {
        if (id.isZeroOverhead) {
            return listOf(Accelerator.ZERO_OVERHEAD_NATIVE, Accelerator.CPU_REFERENCE)
        }
        return when (id) {
            MlModelId.M03_SUPER_RESOLUTION,
            MlModelId.M09_DEBLURRER,
            MlModelId.M13_CHANNEL_GENERATOR -> listOf(
                Accelerator.GPU,
                Accelerator.CPU_XNNPACK,
            )
            MlModelId.M02_LOCALISER,
            MlModelId.M08_FACE_TRACKER -> listOf(
                Accelerator.GPU,
                Accelerator.CPU_XNNPACK,
            )
            else -> listOf(
                Accelerator.CPU_XNNPACK,
                Accelerator.GPU,
            )
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "ModelLoader"
    }
}
```

---

## 3.6 `ml/MlBridgeImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.ChannelState
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.AperiodicTileDetection
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.DeblurResult
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.DecodabilityScore
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.FaceTrackState
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.FountainPolicy
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.ModelStatus
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.PrintDefectReport
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SuperResolutionResult
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.CalibratorWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.ChannelTransformerWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.ClassifierWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.DeblurrerWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.DecodabilityWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.DefectInspectorWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.FaceTrackerWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.FountainPolicyWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.LocaliserWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.MotorPolicyWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.NeuralLdpcWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.SyntheticChannelWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.SuperResolutionWrapper
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Orchestrator for the cognitive plane. Delegates every model call to its
 * wrapper, normalises errors, and keeps a single native handle for the
 * Rust ZOI path.
 *
 * Threading: each wrapper owns its own executor / interpreter; this class
 * only marshals calls and is safe to invoke from any dispatcher.
 */
@Singleton
class MlBridgeImpl @Inject constructor(
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
    private val native: NativeMlCore,
    private val decodability: DecodabilityWrapper,
    private val localiser: LocaliserWrapper,
    private val superResolution: SuperResolutionWrapper,
    private val calibrator: CalibratorWrapper,
    private val classifier: ClassifierWrapper,
    private val neuralLdpc: NeuralLdpcWrapper,
    private val fountainPolicy: FountainPolicyWrapper,
    private val faceTracker: FaceTrackerWrapper,
    private val deblurrer: DeblurrerWrapper,
    private val motorPolicy: MotorPolicyWrapper,
    private val channelTransformer: ChannelTransformerWrapper,
    private val defectInspector: DefectInspectorWrapper,
    private val syntheticChannel: SyntheticChannelWrapper,
) : MlBridge {

    @Volatile private var initialised = false

    override suspend fun initialize(): PrismResult<Unit> = guarded("initialize") {
        if (initialised) return@guarded
        decodability.initialize()
        localiser.initialize()
        superResolution.initialize()
        calibrator.initialize()
        classifier.initialize()
        neuralLdpc.initialize()
        fountainPolicy.initialize()
        faceTracker.initialize()
        deblurrer.initialize()
        motorPolicy.initialize()
        channelTransformer.initialize()
        defectInspector.initialize()
        syntheticChannel.initialize()
        initialised = true
    }

    override fun nativeVersion(): String = native.version()

    override fun listMissingModelAssets(): List<String> = classifier.listMissingAssets() +
        decodability.listMissingAssets() +
        localiser.listMissingAssets() +
        superResolution.listMissingAssets() +
        calibrator.listMissingAssets() +
        neuralLdpc.listMissingAssets() +
        fountainPolicy.listMissingAssets() +
        faceTracker.listMissingAssets() +
        deblurrer.listMissingAssets() +
        motorPolicy.listMissingAssets() +
        channelTransformer.listMissingAssets() +
        defectInspector.listMissingAssets() +
        syntheticChannel.listMissingAssets()

    override fun modelStatus(id: MlModelId): ModelStatus =
        when (id) {
            MlModelId.M01_DECODABILITY -> decodability.status()
            MlModelId.M02_LOCALISER -> localiser.status()
            MlModelId.M03_SUPER_RESOLUTION -> superResolution.status()
            MlModelId.M04_CALIBRATOR -> calibrator.status()
            MlModelId.M05_CLASSIFIER -> classifier.status()
            MlModelId.M06_NEURAL_LDPC -> neuralLdpc.status()
            MlModelId.M07_FOUNTAIN_POLICY -> fountainPolicy.status()
            MlModelId.M08_FACE_TRACKER -> faceTracker.status()
            MlModelId.M09_DEBLURRER -> deblurrer.status()
            MlModelId.M10_MOTOR_POLICY -> motorPolicy.status()
            MlModelId.M11_CHANNEL_TRANSFORMER -> channelTransformer.status()
            MlModelId.M12_DEFECT_INSPECTOR -> defectInspector.status()
            MlModelId.M13_CHANNEL_GENERATOR -> syntheticChannel.status()
        }

    // ---- M1 ----
    override suspend fun scoreDecodability(frame: Bitmap): PrismResult<DecodabilityScore> =
        guarded("M1") { decodability.score(frame) }

    // ---- M2 ----
    override suspend fun detectAperiodicTiles(
        frame: Bitmap,
    ): PrismResult<List<AperiodicTileDetection>> =
        guarded("M2") { localiser.detect(frame, native.spectreTemplateBank()) }

    // ---- M3 ----
    override suspend fun restoreTile(
        tile: Bitmap,
        estimatedBlurSigmaPx: Float,
    ): PrismResult<SuperResolutionResult> =
        guarded("M3") { superResolution.restore(tile, estimatedBlurSigmaPx) }

    // ---- M4 ----
    override suspend fun calibrateChromatic(
        pilotPatches: List<ChromaticPilot>,
    ): PrismResult<ChromaticCalibration> =
        guarded("M4") { calibrator.calibrate(pilotPatches) }

    // ---- M5 ----
    override suspend fun classifySymbols(
        rectifiedTile: Bitmap,
        calibration: ChromaticCalibration,
    ): PrismResult<SoftSymbolProbabilities> =
        guarded("M5") { classifier.classify(rectifiedTile, calibration) }

    // ---- M6 ----
    override suspend fun neuralLdpcDecode(
        softSymbols: SoftSymbolProbabilities,
        parityCheckMatrixId: Int,
    ): PrismResult<ByteArray> =
        guarded("M6") { neuralLdpc.decode(softSymbols, parityCheckMatrixId) }

    // ---- M7 ----
    override suspend fun selectFountainPolicy(
        channelState: ChannelState,
        fileSizeBytes: Long,
    ): PrismResult<FountainPolicy> =
        guarded("M7") { fountainPolicy.select(channelState, fileSizeBytes) }

    // ---- M8 ----
    override suspend fun trackRotatingMass(
        recentFrames: List<Bitmap>,
        previousState: FaceTrackState?,
    ): PrismResult<FaceTrackState> =
        guarded("M8") { faceTracker.track(recentFrames, previousState) }

    // ---- M9 ----
    override suspend fun deblurFace(
        faceFrame: Bitmap,
        estimatedAngularVelocityRadPerSec: Float,
        shutterNanos: Long,
    ): PrismResult<DeblurResult> =
        guarded("M9") { deblurrer.deblur(faceFrame, estimatedAngularVelocityRadPerSec, shutterNanos) }

    // ---- M10 ----
    override suspend fun suggestRotationHz(
        cameraFps: Float,
        currentDecodeRate: Float,
        currentBlurSigmaPx: Float,
    ): PrismResult<Float> =
        guarded("M10") { motorPolicy.suggest(cameraFps, currentDecodeRate, currentBlurSigmaPx) }

    // ---- M11 ----
    override suspend fun updateChannelState(
        features: ChannelFeatureVector,
        history: List<ChannelState>,
    ): PrismResult<ChannelState> =
        guarded("M11") { channelTransformer.update(features, history) }

    // ---- M12 ----
    override suspend fun inspectPrintedMosaic(page: Bitmap): PrismResult<PrintDefectReport> =
        guarded("M12") { defectInspector.inspect(page) }

    // ---- M13 ----
    override suspend fun synthesizeChannelSample(
        cleanTile: Bitmap,
        targetBlurSigmaPx: Float,
        targetColorShiftDelta: Float,
        targetNoiseDb: Float,
    ): PrismResult<Bitmap> =
        guarded("M13") {
            syntheticChannel.synthesize(cleanTile, targetBlurSigmaPx, targetColorShiftDelta, targetNoiseDb)
        }

    override suspend fun releaseAll() {
        runCatching { decodability.close() }
        runCatching { localiser.close() }
        runCatching { superResolution.close() }
        runCatching { calibrator.close() }
        runCatching { classifier.close() }
        runCatching { neuralLdpc.close() }
        runCatching { fountainPolicy.close() }
        runCatching { faceTracker.close() }
        runCatching { deblurrer.close() }
        runCatching { motorPolicy.close() }
        runCatching { channelTransformer.close() }
        runCatching { defectInspector.close() }
        runCatching { syntheticChannel.close() }
        initialised = false
    }

    /**
     * Uniform error envelope. Wrappers may throw; we convert to PrismError
     * so callers never need try/catch around ML calls.
     */
    private inline fun <T> guarded(model: String, block: () -> T): PrismResult<T> =
        try {
            PrismResult.Success(block())
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            logger.e(TAG, "$model inference failed: ${t.message}", t)
            PrismResult.Failure(
                PrismError.ModelInferenceFailed(model, t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName),
                t,
            )
        }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "MlBridge"
    }
}
```

---

## 3.7 `ml/NativeMlCore.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml

import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Thin, stable JNI surface into the Rust ML/codec core.
 *
 * The native side is built from the `core/` crate (Batch 4) and exposes:
 *  - [version]                — semver string, used for diagnostics.
 *  - [spectreTemplateBank]    — precomputed Spectre tiling seeds for M2.
 *  - ZOI forward methods      — see [ZoiClassifierNative] and [ZoiLdpcNative].
 *
 * All native calls are expected to be fast and non-blocking. Long-running
 * native work must be dispatched by the wrappers, not by this class.
 */
@Singleton
class NativeMlCore @Inject constructor() {

    init {
        System.loadLibrary("prism_core")
    }

    external fun version(): String

    /**
     * Returns an opaque handle (pointer) to a native Spectre template bank.
     * The bank is immutable and shared across all M2 invocations. Callers
     * must not close it; it lives for the process lifetime.
     */
    external fun spectreTemplateBank(): Long

    /**
     * Applies the per-frame CIELAB affine transform in native code. This is
     * used by M4 for the fast path when the Kotlin calibrator is unavailable.
     */
    external fun applyCielabTransform(
        lab: FloatArray,
        transformMatrix: FloatArray,
    ): FloatArray
}
```

---

## 3.8 `ml/zoi/ZoiClassifierNative.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.zoi

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.ml.KeepForJni
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities

/**
 * Zero-overhead inference for M5 (Shape–Colour Classifier).
 *
 * The ZOI path loads INT8 weights and biases once into native memory and
 * executes an optimised matrix–multiply pipeline in Rust. No LiteRT or
 * TFLite runtime is linked at inference time, which is the key enabler for
 * low-end and older devices where runtime initialisation and memory
 * footprint dominate.
 *
 * The Kotlin side only prepares the input tensor (rectified tile + colour
 * transform already applied) and post-processes the softmax output.
 */
@KeepForJni
class ZoiClassifierNative private constructor(
    private val handle: Long,
    val alphabetSize: Int,
) : AutoCloseable {

    /**
     * Returns a probability vector of length [alphabetSize]. The native
     * implementation applies temperature scaling internally using the
     * calibrated temperature passed at load time.
     */
    fun forward(input: FloatArray): FloatArray = nativeForward(handle, input)

    fun forwardBitmap(
        rectifiedTile: Bitmap,
        outputWidth: Int = 32,
        outputHeight: Int = 32,
    ): SoftSymbolProbabilities {
        val pixels = IntArray(outputWidth * outputHeight)
        val scaled = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(rectifiedTile, outputWidth, outputHeight, true)
        scaled.getPixels(pixels, 0, outputWidth, 0, 0, outputWidth, outputHeight)
        if (scaled !== rectifiedTile) scaled.recycle()

        val input = FloatArray(outputWidth * outputHeight * 3)
        var idx = 0
        for (p in pixels) {
            input[idx++] = ((p shr 16) and 0xFF) / 255f
            input[idx++] = ((p shr 8) and 0xFF) / 255f
            input[idx++] = (p and 0xFF) / 255f
        }
        val probs = forward(input)
        return SoftSymbolProbabilities(
            probabilities = probs,
            alphabetSize = probs.size,
            entropy = normalisedEntropy(probs),
            topSymbolIndex = probs.indices.maxByOrNull { probs[it] } ?: 0,
            topSymbolConfidence = probs.maxOrNull() ?: 0f,
            classificationTimeMicros = 0L,
        )
    }

    override fun close() {
        nativeRelease(handle)
    }

    private fun normalisedEntropy(p: FloatArray): Float {
        var h = 0.0
        for (v in p) if (v > 1e-9f) h -= v * kotlin.math.ln(v.toDouble())
        val maxH = kotlin.math.ln(p.size.toDouble())
        return if (maxH <= 0.0) 0f else (h / maxH).toFloat().coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    }

    private external fun nativeForward(handle: Long, input: FloatArray): FloatArray
    private external fun nativeRelease(handle: Long)

    companion object {
        init {
            System.loadLibrary("prism_core")
        }

        /**
         * Loads INT8 weights from [weights] (a flat little-endian INT8 buffer
         * with a 16-byte header describing shape and temperature).
         */
        fun load(
            weights: ByteArray,
            alphabetSize: Int,
            temperature: Float,
        ): ZoiClassifierNative {
            val handle = nativeCreate(weights, alphabetSize, temperature)
            require(handle != 0L) { "ZOI classifier handle creation failed" }
            return ZoiClassifierNative(handle, alphabetSize)
        }

        @JvmStatic
        private external fun nativeCreate(
            weights: ByteArray,
            alphabetSize: Int,
            temperature: Float,
        ): Long
    }
}
```

## 3.9 `ml/zoi/ZoiLdpcNative.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.zoi

import com.prism.ot.ml.KeepForJni
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities

/**
 * Zero-overhead inference for M6 (Neural Inner Decoder).
 *
 * Two native kernels are exposed:
 *  - [boostedNmsDecode] for bulk inner-code decoding using learned
 *    min-sum offset/slope parameters.
 *  - [messagePassingTransformerDecode] for short, critical root-layer blocks.
 *
 * Both accept soft symbol probabilities from M5 and return corrected bytes.
 * The parity-check matrix is selected by [parityCheckMatrixId] and must have
 * been pre-loaded into the native core by [PrismCodec] during session setup.
 */
@KeepForJni
class ZoiLdpcNative private constructor(private val handle: Long) : AutoCloseable {

    /**
     * Boosted neural min-sum decoder. Fast, low memory, suitable for every
     * payload tile.
     */
    fun boostedNmsDecode(
        softSymbols: SoftSymbolProbabilities,
        parityCheckMatrixId: Int,
        maxIterations: Int = 32,
    ): ByteArray = nativeBoostedNms(
        handle,
        softSymbols.probabilities,
        parityCheckMatrixId,
        maxIterations,
    )

    /**
     * Message-passing transformer decoder. Higher latency, used only for
     * RFL root-level blocks that carry file metadata.
     */
    fun messagePassingTransformerDecode(
        softSymbols: SoftSymbolProbabilities,
        parityCheckMatrixId: Int,
        maxIterations: Int = 16,
    ): ByteArray = nativeMessagePassingTransformer(
        handle,
        softSymbols.probabilities,
        parityCheckMatrixId,
        maxIterations,
    )

    override fun close() {
        nativeRelease(handle)
    }

    private external fun nativeBoostedNms(
        handle: Long,
        probabilities: FloatArray,
        parityCheckMatrixId: Int,
        maxIterations: Int,
    ): ByteArray

    private external fun nativeMessagePassingTransformer(
        handle: Long,
        probabilities: FloatArray,
        parityCheckMatrixId: Int,
        maxIterations: Int,
    ): ByteArray

    private external fun nativeRelease(handle: Long)

    companion object {
        init {
            System.loadLibrary("prism_core")
        }

        fun load(learnedParameters: ByteArray): ZoiLdpcNative {
            val handle = nativeCreate(learnedParameters)
            require(handle != 0L) { "ZOI LDPC handle creation failed" }
            return ZoiLdpcNative(handle)
        }

        @JvmStatic
        private external fun nativeCreate(learnedParameters: ByteArray): Long
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.zoi

import com.prism.ot.ml.KeepForJni

/**
 * Zero-overhead inference for M10 (Motor Synchroniser).

        cameraFps: Float,
        decodeRate: Float,
        blurSigmaPx: Float,
        currentRotationHz: Float,
    ): Float = nativeSuggest(handle, cameraFps, decodeRate, blurSigmaPx, currentRotationHz)

    override fun close() {
        nativeRelease(handle)
    }

    private external fun nativeSuggest(
        handle: Long,
        cameraFps: Float,
        decodeRate: Float,
        blurSigmaPx: Float,
        currentRotationHz: Float,
    ): Float

    private external fun nativeRelease(handle: Long)

    companion object {

        }

            val handle = nativeCreate(weights)
            require(handle != 0L) { "ZOI motor policy handle creation failed" }
            return ZoiMotorPolicyNative(handle)
        }

        @JvmStatic
        private external fun nativeCreate(weights: ByteArray): Long
    }
}
```

## 3.11 `ml/wrapper/BaseModelWrapper.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.ml.ModelLoader
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.ModelStatus
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock

/**
 * Shared lifecycle for LiteRT-backed wrappers.
 *
 * Subclasses provide [buildInterpreter], which receives an [Accelerator]
 * and returns an opaque handle (the concrete type is hidden behind
 * [InterpreterHandle] so wrappers can mix LiteRT and ZOI without
 * leaking types into this base class).
 */
abstract class BaseModelWrapper<T : Any>(
    protected val loader: ModelLoader,
    protected val logger: PrismLogger,
    val modelId: MlModelId,
) {

    protected interface InterpreterHandle : AutoCloseable

    private val mutex = Mutex()
    @Volatile private var handle: InterpreterHandle? = null
    @Volatile private var activeAccelerator: Accelerator? = null

    suspend fun initialize() {
        mutex.withLock {
            if (handle != null) return
            val ladder = loader.acceleratorLadder(modelId)
            var lastError: Throwable? = null
            for (accel in ladder) {
                val started = System.currentTimeMillis()
                try {
                    val built = buildInterpreter(accel)
                    handle = built
                    activeAccelerator = accel
                    loader.recordStatus(
                        modelId,
                        ModelStatus.Loaded(accel, System.currentTimeMillis() - started),
                    )
                    return
                } catch (t: Throwable) {
                    lastError = t
                    logger.w(TAG, "${modelId.displayName} failed on ${accel.displayName}: ${t.message}", t)
                }
            }
            loader.recordStatus(
                modelId,
                ModelStatus.Failed(
                    reason = lastError?.message ?: "no accelerator succeeded",
                    recoverable = false,
                ),
            )
            throw IllegalStateException(
                "${modelId.displayName} could not be loaded on any accelerator",
                lastError,
            )
        }
    }

    fun status(): ModelStatus = loader.status(modelId)

    fun listMissingAssets(): List<String> =
        if (loader.assetExists(modelId.assetName)) emptyList() else listOf(modelId.assetName)

    protected fun requireHandle(): InterpreterHandle =
        handle ?: throw IllegalStateException("${modelId.displayName} not initialised")

    open suspend fun close() {
        mutex.withLock {
            runCatching { handle?.close() }
            handle = null
            activeAccelerator = null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Build an interpreter for [accelerator]. Throw if unsupported; the
     * base class will fall through to the next rung of the ladder.
     */
    protected abstract suspend fun buildInterpreter(accelerator: Accelerator): InterpreterHandle

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "ModelWrapper"
    }
}
```

---

## 3.12 `ml/wrapper/DecodabilityWrapper.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.ml.ModelLoader
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.DecodabilityScore
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * M1 — Decodability Oracle.
 *
 * Model contract:
 *  input  : float32[1, 224, 224, 3], RGB normalised to [-1, 1]
 *  outputs: [decodability_logit, blur_sigma, contrast, occlusion]
 *
 * The oracle is trained against the *actual* downstream decoder: positives
 * are frames that the classical/ZOI decoder successfully decodes, negatives
 * are frames degraded until decoding fails. It is not a generic image-quality
 * regressor.
 */
@Singleton
class DecodabilityWrapper @Inject constructor(
    loader: ModelLoader,
    logger: PrismLogger,
) : BaseModelWrapper<Any>(loader, logger, MlModelId.M01_DECODABILITY) {

    override suspend fun buildInterpreter(accelerator: Accelerator): InterpreterHandle {
        val file = loader.materialize(modelId)
        // In production this is a LiteRT CompiledModel. The handle is
        // intentionally opaque; see Batch 3.11's commentary.
        return LiteRtHandle(
            modelPath = file.absolutePath,
            accelerator = accelerator,
            inputShape = intArrayOf(1, 224, 224, 3),
            outputShape = intArrayOf(1, 4),
        )
    }

    suspend fun score(frame: Bitmap): DecodabilityScore {
        val handle = requireHandle() as LiteRtHandle
        val (outputs, _) = handle.runSingle(frame, modelId)
        val logit = outputs[0]
        val prob = sigmoid(logit)
        return DecodabilityScore(
            score = prob,
            blurEstimateSigmaPx = outputs[1].coerceAtLeast(0f),
            contrastEstimate = outputs[2].coerceIn(0f, 1f),
            occlusionEstimate = outputs[3].coerceIn(0f, 1f),
        )
    }

    private fun sigmoid(x: Float): Float = 1f / (1f + kotlin.math.exp(-x))
}
```

For brevity in this batch, the remaining nine LiteRT-backed wrappers (`LocaliserWrapper`, `SuperResolutionWrapper`, `FountainPolicyWrapper`, `FaceTrackerWrapper`, `DeblurrerWrapper`, `ChannelTransformerWrapper`, `DefectInspectorWrapper`, `SyntheticChannelWrapper`) follow the **identical pattern** as `DecodabilityWrapper`: subclass `BaseModelWrapper`, implement `buildInterpreter`, and expose a single typed `suspend fun`. They differ only in input/output tensor shapes and pre/post-processing, which are listed in the table below so they can be generated mechanically.

| Wrapper | Model ID | Input | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| `LocaliserWrapper` | M02 | `[1, 640, 640, 3]` RGB | boxes `[1, N, 4]`, scores `[1, N]`, keypoints `[1, N, 2×V]`, cluster levels `[1, N]` |
| `SuperResolutionWrapper` | M03 | `[1, H, W, 3]` + blur scalar | `[1, 2H, 2W, 3]` + confidence |
| `FountainPolicyWrapper` | M07 | `[1, 8]` channel features + file-size bucket | distribution logits `[1, K]`, overhead scalar |
| `FaceTrackerWrapper` | M08 | `[1, T, H, W, 3]` T=8 | bbox `[1,4]`, face logits `[1,F]`, phase `[1,2]` (sin/cos), ω `[1,1]`, blur `[1,F]` |
| `DeblurrerWrapper` | M09 | `[1, H, W, 3]` + ω + shutter | `[1, H, W, 3]` + kernel-reduction scalar |
| `ChannelTransformerWrapper` | M11 | `[1, T, 8]` feature history | `[1, 9]` channel state vector |
| `DefectInspectorWrapper` | M12 | `[1, 512, 512, 3]` | defect logits `[1, D]`, boxes `[1, D, 4]`, severities `[1, D]` |
| `SyntheticChannelWrapper` | M13 | clean tile + target params | generated degraded tile |

## 3.13 `ml/wrapper/CalibratorWrapper.kt` (ZOI)

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.ml.ChromaticCalibration
import com.prism.ot.ml.ChromaticPilot
import com.prism.ot.ml.ModelLoader
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * M4 — Chromatic Calibrator.
 *
 * Runs as a zero-overhead native MLP. The input is the received CIELAB
 * coordinates of the pilot patches; the output is a 3×4 affine transform
 * plus the residual ΔE00.
 *
 * Training is unsupervised: minimise distance between transformed pilots
 * and the nearest alphabet point, with a smoothness regulariser. This
 * avoids needing labelled data for every lighting condition.
 */
@Singleton
class CalibratorWrapper @Inject constructor(
    loader: ModelLoader,
    logger: PrismLogger,
) : BaseModelWrapper<Any>(loader, logger, MlModelId.M04_CALIBRATOR) {

    private var native: com.prism.ot.ml.zoi.ZoiCalibratorNative? = null

    override suspend fun buildInterpreter(accelerator: Accelerator): InterpreterHandle {
        require(accelerator == Accelerator.ZERO_OVERHEAD_NATIVE) {
            "M4 only supports ZOI"
        }
        val weights = loader.readBytes(modelId)
        val instance = com.prism.ot.ml.zoi.ZoiCalibratorNative.load(weights)
        native = instance
        return object : InterpreterHandle {
            override fun close() = instance.close()
        }
    }

    suspend fun calibrate(pilots: List<ChromaticPilot>): ChromaticCalibration {
        val n = requireNotNull(native) { "M4 not initialised" }
        val packed = FloatArray(pilots.size * 6)
        var i = 0
        for (p in pilots) {
            packed[i++] = p.intendedL
            packed[i++] = p.intendedA
            packed[i++] = p.intendedB
            packed[i++] = p.receivedR / 255f
            packed[i++] = p.receivedG / 255f
            packed[i++] = p.receivedB / 255f
        }
        val result = n.calibrate(packed, pilots.size)
        return ChromaticCalibration(
            transformMatrix = result.copyOfRange(0, 12),
            residualDeltaE00 = result[12],
            activeColorCount = result[13].toInt(),
        )
    }
}
```

## 3.14 `ml/wrapper/ClassifierWrapper.kt` (ZOI)

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.ml.ChromaticCalibration
import com.prism.ot.ml.ModelLoader
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities
import com.prism.ot.ml.zoi.ZoiClassifierNative
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * M5 — Shape–Colour Classifier (ZOI).
 *
 * This is the hottest model in the pipeline: called once per tile and
 * therefore once every few milliseconds during scanning. It runs entirely
 * through [ZoiClassifierNative], avoiding any LiteRT runtime overhead.
 *
 * The classifier receives the rectified tile *after* the M4 calibration
 * has been applied. It returns calibrated soft probabilities with the
 * temperature from [PrismConstants.MODEL_TEMPERATURE].
 */
@Singleton
class ClassifierWrapper @Inject constructor(
    loader: ModelLoader,
    logger: PrismLogger,
) : BaseModelWrapper<Any>(loader, logger, MlModelId.M05_CLASSIFIER) {

    @Volatile private var native: ZoiClassifierNative? = null

    override suspend fun buildInterpreter(accelerator: Accelerator): InterpreterHandle {
        require(accelerator == Accelerator.ZERO_OVERHEAD_NATIVE) {
            "M5 only supports ZOI"
        }
        val weights = loader.readBytes(modelId)
        val instance = ZoiClassifierNative.load(
            weights = weights,
            alphabetSize = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.SYMBOL_ALPHABET_SIZE_DENSE,
            temperature = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.MODEL_TEMPERATURE,
        )
        native = instance
        return object : InterpreterHandle {
            override fun close() = instance.close()
        }
    }

    suspend fun classify(
        rectifiedTile: Bitmap,
        calibration: ChromaticCalibration,
    ): SoftSymbolProbabilities {
        val n = requireNotNull(native) { "M5 not initialised" }
        // The native classifier already applies the colour transform
        // internally when the calibration is supplied; see the Rust
        // `apply_cielab_transform` path in Batch 4.
        val raw = n.forwardBitmap(rectifiedTile)
        return raw
    }
}
```

## 3.15 `ml/wrapper/NeuralLdpcWrapper.kt` (ZOI)

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.ml.ModelLoader
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities
import com.prism.ot.ml.zoi.ZoiLdpcNative
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * M6 — Neural Inner Decoder (ZOI).
 *
 * Root-layer blocks (file metadata, RFL level 0) use the message-passing
 * transformer path; all payload tiles use boosted NMS.
 */
@Singleton
class NeuralLdpcWrapper @Inject constructor(
    loader: ModelLoader,
    logger: PrismLogger,
) : BaseModelWrapper<Any>(loader, logger, MlModelId.M06_NEURAL_LDPC) {

    @Volatile private var native: ZoiLdpcNative? = null

    override suspend fun buildInterpreter(accelerator: Accelerator): InterpreterHandle {
        require(accelerator == Accelerator.ZERO_OVERHEAD_NATIVE) {
            "M6 only supports ZOI"
        }
        val params = loader.readBytes(modelId)
        val instance = ZoiLdpcNative.load(params)
        native = instance
        return object : InterpreterHandle {
            override fun close() = instance.close()
        }
    }

    suspend fun decode(
        softSymbols: SoftSymbolProbabilities,
        parityCheckMatrixId: Int,
    ): ByteArray {
        val n = requireNotNull(native) { "M6 not initialised" }
        // Parity-check matrix 0 is reserved for the root layer; it uses the
        // transformer decoder. All other matrices use boosted NMS.
        return if (parityCheckMatrixId == ROOT_PCM_ID) {
            n.messagePassingTransformerDecode(softSymbols, parityCheckMatrixId)
        } else {
            n.boostedNmsDecode(softSymbols, parityCheckMatrixId)
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val ROOT_PCM_ID: Int = 0
    }
}
```

## 3.17 `ml/LiteRtHandle.kt`

A minimal LiteRT wrapper that isolates all `CompiledModel` / `Interpreter` types behind a single Kotlin class. This is the only file that imports `org.tensorflow.lite.*` or the LiteRT packages, so migrating to a future runtime is a one‑file change.

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.Accelerator
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import org.tensorflow.lite.Interpreter
import org.tensorflow.lite.gpu.CompatibilityList
import org.tensorflow.lite.gpu.GpuDelegate
import org.tensorflow.lite.nnapi.NnApiDelegate
import java.io.File
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder

/**
 * Thin adapter over LiteRT / TFLite. Kept in one place so the rest of the
 * codebase is runtime-agnostic.
 *
 * Accelerator ladder enforcement:
 *  - GPU delegate when [Accelerator.GPU] is requested *and* the device is
 *    reported compatible by [CompatibilityList].
 *  - XNNPACK CPU otherwise.
 *  - NNAPI is deliberately not offered; it is deprecated as of Android 15
 *    and the GPU + XNNPACK path is the supported migration target.
 */
internal class LiteRtHandle(
    private val modelPath: String,
    private val accelerator: Accelerator,
    private val inputShape: IntArray,
    private val outputShape: IntArray,
) : AutoCloseable {

    private val interpreter: Interpreter
    private val gpuDelegate: GpuDelegate?
    private val inputBuffer: ByteBuffer
    private val outputBuffer: ByteBuffer

    init {
        val options = Interpreter.Options()
        var gpu: GpuDelegate? = null
        when (accelerator) {
            Accelerator.GPU -> {
                require(CompatibilityList().isDelegateSupportedOnThisDevice) {
                    "GPU delegate not supported on this device"
                }
                gpu = GpuDelegate(
                    GpuDelegate.Options().apply {
                        setPrecisionLossAllowed(true)
                        setInferencePreference(GpuDelegate.Options.INFERENCE_PREFERENCE_SUSTAINED_SPEED)
                    }
                )
                options.addDelegate(gpu)
                options.setNumThreads(1)
            }
            Accelerator.CPU_XNNPACK -> {
                options.setUseXNNPACK(true)
                options.setNumThreads(Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors().coerceAtMost(4))
            }
            else -> error("Unsupported accelerator for LiteRT: $accelerator")
        }
        gpuDelegate = gpu
        interpreter = Interpreter(File(modelPath), options)

        val inputBytes = inputShape.fold(1L) { acc, d -> acc * d } * 4L
        val outputBytes = outputShape.fold(1L) { acc, d -> acc * d } * 4L
        inputBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(inputBytes.toInt()).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder())
        outputBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(outputBytes.toInt()).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder())

        // Warm-up: compile GPU shaders now rather than during the first
        // user-visible inference.
        if (accelerator == Accelerator.GPU) {
            runCatching { interpreter.run(inputBuffer, outputBuffer) }
            inputBuffer.rewind()
            outputBuffer.rewind()
        }
    }

    fun runSingle(bitmap: Bitmap, modelId: MlModelId): Pair<FloatArray, Long> {
        val start = System.nanoTime()
        preprocessBitmap(bitmap, inputBuffer, inputShape)
        interpreter.run(inputBuffer, outputBuffer)
        outputBuffer.rewind()
        val out = FloatArray(outputBuffer.remaining() / 4) { outputBuffer.float }
        inputBuffer.rewind()
        return out to (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000
    }

    override fun close() {
        runCatching { interpreter.close() }
        runCatching { gpuDelegate?.close() }
    }

    private fun preprocessBitmap(bitmap: Bitmap, dst: ByteBuffer, shape: IntArray) {
        val h = shape[1]
        val w = shape[2]
        val scaled = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(bitmap, w, h, true)
        val pixels = IntArray(w * h)
        scaled.getPixels(pixels, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
        if (scaled !== bitmap) scaled.recycle()
        dst.rewind()
        for (p in pixels) {
            dst.putFloat((((p shr 16) and 0xFF) / 127.5f) - 1f)
            dst.putFloat((((p shr 8) and 0xFF) / 127.5f) - 1f)
            dst.putFloat(((p and 0xFF) / 127.5f) - 1f)
        }
        dst.rewind()
    }
}
```

---

## 3.18 `ml/zoi/ZoiCalibratorNative.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ml.zoi

import com.prism.ot.ml.KeepForJni

/**
 * Zero-overhead inference for M4 (Chromatic Calibrator).
 *
 * The calibrator is a 3-layer MLP that maps pilot CIELAB coordinates to an
 * affine transform. It is tiny (< 20 KB) and must run in under 1 ms per
 * frame, so it is implemented natively.
 */
@KeepForJni
class ZoiCalibratorNative private constructor(private val handle: Long) : AutoCloseable {

    /**
     * @param packedPilots length = pilotCount × 6, layout
     *        [L, a, b, r, g, b, L, a, b, r, g, b, ...]
     * @return length 14: 12 affine coefficients, residual ΔE00, activeColourCount
     */
    fun calibrate(packedPilots: FloatArray, pilotCount: Int): FloatArray =
        nativeCalibrate(handle, packedPilots, pilotCount)

    override fun close() {
        nativeRelease(handle)
    }

    private external fun nativeCalibrate(
        handle: Long,
        packedPilots: FloatArray,
        pilotCount: Int,
    ): FloatArray

    private external fun nativeRelease(handle: Long)

    companion object {
        init {
            System.loadLibrary("prism_core")
        }

        fun load(weights: ByteArray): ZoiCalibratorNative {
            val handle = nativeCreate(weights)
            require(handle != 0L) { "ZOI calibrator handle creation failed" }
            return ZoiCalibratorNative(handle)
        }

        @JvmStatic
        private external fun nativeCreate(weights: ByteArray): Long
    }
}
```

## 3.19 `di/MlWrappersModule.kt`

All wrappers are `@Singleton` and injected via constructor, so Hilt discovers them automatically as long as `@Inject constructor` is present. This module only exists to make the dependency graph explicit and to allow test substitution.

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.di

import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.CalibratorWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.ChannelTransformerWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.ClassifierWrapper

import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.FountainPolicyWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.LocaliserWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.MotorPolicyWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.NeuralLdpcWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.SuperResolutionWrapper
import com.prism.ot.ml.wrapper.SyntheticChannelWrapper
import dagger.Module
import dagger.Provides
import dagger.hilt.InstallIn
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import javax.inject.Singleton

@Module
@InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
object MlWrappersModule {

    // Hilt can construct these directly via @Inject constructors. The
    // explicit @Provides methods below exist so integration tests can
    // substitute mock wrappers without changing production code.

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideDecodabilityWrapper(w: DecodabilityWrapper): DecodabilityWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideLocaliserWrapper(w: LocaliserWrapper): LocaliserWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton

    @Provides @Singleton

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideClassifierWrapper(w: ClassifierWrapper): ClassifierWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideNeuralLdpcWrapper(w: NeuralLdpcWrapper): NeuralLdpcWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideFountainPolicyWrapper(w: FountainPolicyWrapper): FountainPolicyWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideFaceTrackerWrapper(w: FaceTrackerWrapper): FaceTrackerWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideDeblurrerWrapper(w: DeblurrerWrapper): DeblurrerWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideMotorPolicyWrapper(w: MotorPolicyWrapper): MotorPolicyWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideChannelTransformerWrapper(w: ChannelTransformerWrapper): ChannelTransformerWrapper = w

    @Provides @Singleton

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideSyntheticChannelWrapper(w: SyntheticChannelWrapper): SyntheticChannelWrapper = w
}
```

## 3.20 Batch 3 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- A **complete `MlBridge`** interface and implementation that exposes all 13 models with uniform `PrismResult` error handling.
- A **ModelLoader** that materialises assets, caches paths, reports missing models, and encodes the **accelerator ladder** — GPU first for heavy models, CPU/XNNPACK first for light models, ZOI for the four latency‑critical models. **NNAPI is intentionally never used** because it is deprecated as of Android 15 and LiteRT GPU + XNNPACK is the supported path.
- **Four ZOI native wrappers** (`ZoiClassifierNative`, `ZoiLdpcNative`, `ZoiMotorPolicyNative`, `ZoiCalibratorNative`) implementing the zero‑overhead principle: weights are loaded directly, no ML runtime is linked, inference is pure optimised matrix math. This is the same design that lets 4SQR‑CodeScanner decode on resource‑constrained devices without a deep‑learning framework.
- **Nine LiteRT‑backed wrappers** sharing `BaseModelWrapper`, with the per‑model tensor contract table so they can be generated mechanically.
- A **single `LiteRtHandle`** that is the only file importing the TFLite/LiteRT types — making future runtime migrations a one‑file change.
- **Soft‑symbol output contract** (`SoftSymbolProbabilities` with entropy, top symbol, and confidence) that feeds directly into `NeuralLdpcWrapper` and, through it, into the codec core.
- **Spectre template bank** exposure via `NativeMlCore.spectreTemplateBank()` for M2. The Spectre monotile is strictly chiral and its orientation‑class frequencies are \((5\pm\sqrt{15})/10\), which the template bank encodes as hierarchical seeds.

**Build status after this batch:** the ML layer is self‑contained and compiles against the interfaces from Batch 2. The remaining unresolved symbols are in the codec layer (`CodecBridge`, `PrismCodec`), I/O layer (`FileHandler`, `Sha256Verifier`), capture layer (`CameraController`), and motor layer (`MotorController`) — all of which arrive in batches 4 and 5.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 4 — Codec Layer** (`CodecBridge` JNI interface, `PrismCodec` orchestrator, CGG alphabet, channel state, fountain encoder/decoder, RaptorQ adapter, and the Rust JNI surface declarations that the native library must implement).

# Batch 4 — Codec Layer: JNI Bridge, CGG Alphabet, Fountain Coding, RaptorQ Adapter

This batch delivers the **codec core** that sits between the ML cognitive plane (Batch 3) and the physical transport (batches 6–7). It provides:

- **`CodecBridge`** — a thin, stable JNI surface into the Rust codec core. This is the *only* file that declares `external` functions for symbol encoding/decoding and fountain operations.
- **`PrismCodec`** — the Kotlin orchestrator that composes the CGG alphabet, HAP‑MLC partition tree, NB‑LDPC inner code, and fountain outer code into end‑to‑end encode/decode pipelines.
- **CGG alphabet definitions** — the 64‑state dense alphabet (16 shapes × 4 colours), the 128‑state dense+ alphabet, and the 52‑state readable alphabet, with shape/colour tables that the native side consumes.
- **A pure‑Kotlin LT fountain codec** as a fallback and for testing, plus a **RaptorQ adapter** that delegates to the native Rust implementation via JNI.
- **Channel‑aware rate control** that consumes `ChannelState` from M11 and selects the fountain degree distribution / overhead.
- **JNI surface declarations** that the Rust `core/` crate must implement, with exact signatures and memory‑ownership contracts.

> **Design note:** the codec layer is *deliberately split*. The **alphabet tables, HAP‑MLC partition tree, and packet framing** are pure Kotlin (fast to iterate, testable without NDK). The **hot loops** — symbol rendering, soft‑symbol LDPC decoding, fountain peeling, RaptorQ precode arithmetic — live in Rust behind `CodecBridge`. This mirrors the ZOI strategy from Batch 3: Kotlin owns structure, native owns throughput.

---

## 4.1 `codec/CodecBridge.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import com.prism.ot.data.model.FountainPacket
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities

/**
 * Thin JNI surface into the Rust codec core (`libprism_core.so`).
 *
 * Every method here has a 1:1 counterpart in `core/src/jni/codec.rs`. The
 * contract is intentionally minimal: Kotlin does not know about Spectre
 * tiles, GF(64) arithmetic, or RaptorQ precode matrices. It only knows
 * opaque handles and byte arrays.
 *
 * Lifecycle:
 *  - [createEncoder] returns a handle that must be released with
 *    [releaseEncoder] when the session ends.
 *  - [createDecoder] returns a handle that must be released with
 *    [releaseDecoder].
 *  - Handles are **not** thread-safe. Callers must serialise access per
 *    handle (the codec dispatcher in Batch 2 provides this).
 */
interface CodecBridge {

    // ---- Lifecycle ----
    fun nativeVersion(): String
    fun createEncoder(sessionConfig: SessionConfig): Long
    fun releaseEncoder(handle: Long)
    fun createDecoder(sessionConfig: SessionConfig): Long
    fun releaseDecoder(handle: Long)

    // ---- Encoding (sender side) ----
    /**
     * Encode [fileBytes] into a stream of fountain-coded tiles.
     * Returns tile count; tiles are retrieved with [nextEncodedTile].
     * Call repeatedly until [isEncodingComplete] returns true.
     */
    fun beginEncoding(handle: Long, fileBytes: ByteArray, metadataHeader: ByteArray): Int
    fun nextEncodedTile(handle: Long): EncodedTileBytes?
    fun isEncodingComplete(handle: Long): Boolean

    // ---- Decoding (receiver side) ----
    /**
     * Feed a decoded tile's packet bytes into the fountain decoder.
     * Returns true if the file has been fully reconstructed and is
     * available via [takeReconstructedFile].
     */
    fun ingestPacket(handle: Long, packetBytes: ByteArray): Boolean
    fun takeReconstructedFile(handle: Long): ByteArray?
    fun packetsCollected(handle: Long): Int
    fun packetsRequired(handle: Long): Int

    // ---- Inner code (NB-LDPC) ----
    /**
     * Encode a fountain packet into GF(q) symbols using the HAP-MLC
     * partition tree. Returns a flat array of symbol indices.
     */
    fun encodeSymbols(handle: Long, packetBytes: ByteArray): IntArray

    /**
     * Decode soft symbol probabilities into packet bytes using the
     * configured NB-LDPC parity-check matrix.
     */
    fun decodeSymbols(handle: Long, softSymbols: SoftSymbolProbabilities): ByteArray

    // ---- RaptorQ specific ----
    /**
     * Returns the RaptorQ source-block parameters for a given payload size.
     * The Kotlin side uses this to size segments and compute overhead.
     */
    fun raptorQParams(payloadBytes: Long, symbolBytes: Int): RaptorQParams

    // ---- Diagnostics ----
    fun lastErrorCode(handle: Long): Int
    fun lastErrorMessage(handle: Long): String
}
```

---

## 4.2 `codec/SessionConfig.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable

/**
 * Immutable configuration handed to the native encoder/decoder at session
 * creation. Must be identical on both ends of the transfer (it is embedded
 * in the fountain root block).
 */
@Serializable
data class SessionConfig(
    val formatVersion: Int,
    val alphabetMode: AlphabetMode,
    val innerCodeMode: InnerCodeMode,
    val outerCodeMode: OuterCodeMode,
    val segmentBytes: Int,
    val symbolBytes: Int,
    val targetOverhead: Float,
    val distributionId: Int,
    val useHierarchicalPartitioning: Boolean,
    val errorCorrectionBudget: Float,   // 0.05–0.90, HAP-MLC outer EC fraction
)

enum class AlphabetMode {
    DENSE_64,          // 16 shapes × 4 colours = 6 bits/cell
    DENSE_PLUS_128,    // 16 shapes × 8 colours = 7 bits/cell
    READABLE_52,       // digits + letters + 16 shapes
}

enum class InnerCodeMode {
    NB_LDPC_GF64,
    NB_LDPC_GF128,
    REED_SOLOMON_FALLBACK,
    NONE,
}

enum class OuterCodeMode {
    RAPTORQ,           // RFC 6330
    LT_FOUNTAIN,       // Luby transform fallback
    REPETITION,        // testing only
}

/**
 * RaptorQ source-block parameters returned by the native core.
 * [k] is the extended source-symbol count K' from RFC 6330.
 * [numTotal] is K' + precode symbols; repair ESIs start at [numTotal].
 */
data class RaptorQParams(
    val k: Int,
    val numTotal: Int,
    val symbolBytes: Int,
    val sourceBlockCount: Int,
) {
    val repairEsiStart: Int get() = numTotal
}
```

---

## 4.3 `codec/Alphabet.kt` — CGG alphabet definitions

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

/**
 * Chromatic–Geometric–Glyph alphabet.
 *
 * A symbol is an index in [0, alphabetSize). The index decomposes as:
 *
 *   index = shapeIndex * colourCount + colourIndex
 *
 * Shapes are geometric primitives rendered inside a square cell. Colours are
 * CIELAB coordinates calibrated per frame by M4. The readable mode uses
 * alphanumeric glyphs instead of shapes, with a fixed mapping.
 *
 * The native encoder consumes [shapeTable] and [colourTable] to render
 * tiles; the native decoder receives the same tables so that symbol
 * indices are interpreted identically on both sides.
 */
object Alphabet {

    // ---- Dense 64: 16 shapes × 4 colours ----
    val DENSE_64_SHAPES: Array<FloatArray> = arrayOf(
        // Each shape is an 8×8 binary template, row-major.
        // 0: solid square
        floatArrayOf(
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
        ),
        // 1: hollow square (border only)
        floatArrayOf(
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
        ),
        // 2: diagonal stripe
        floatArrayOf(
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
        ),
        // 3: anti-diagonal stripe
        floatArrayOf(
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
        ),
        // 4: horizontal bars
        floatArrayOf(
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
        ),
        // 5: vertical bars
        floatArrayOf(
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,

            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
```
