```kotlin
            0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
        ),
        // 15: dot (center only)
        floatArrayOf(
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 1f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
            0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
        ),
    )

    /**
     * Dense 64 colour table: 4 CIELAB centres. Values are chosen to
     * maximise ΔE00 separation within the screen-camera gamut intersection.
     * The native renderer converts these to sRGB using the per-frame
     * calibration from M4.
     */
    val DENSE_64_COLOURS: Array<FloatArray> = arrayOf(
        floatArrayOf(52.0f, 40.0f, 18.0f),    // dark red
        floatArrayOf(48.0f, -28.0f, 42.0f),   // blue
        floatArrayOf(88.0f, -18.0f, 78.0f),   // light green
        floatArrayOf(62.0f, 32.0f, -58.0f),   // purple-blue
    )

    // ---- Dense+ 128: 16 shapes × 8 colours ----
    val DENSE_PLUS_128_COLOURS: Array<FloatArray> = arrayOf(
        floatArrayOf(52.0f, 40.0f, 18.0f),
        floatArrayOf(48.0f, -28.0f, 42.0f),
        floatArrayOf(88.0f, -18.0f, 78.0f),
        floatArrayOf(62.0f, 32.0f, -58.0f),
        floatArrayOf(30.0f, 18.0f, -42.0f),
        floatArrayOf(78.0f, 22.0f, 68.0f),
        floatArrayOf(42.0f, -42.0f, -18.0f),
        floatArrayOf(70.0f, 8.0f, -22.0f),
    )

    // ---- Readable 52: digits + letters + shapes ----
    val READABLE_GLYPHS: Array<Char> = (
        ('0'..'9') + ('A'..'Z') + ('a'..'z') + ('#'..'&')
    ).toList().take(52).toTypedArray()

    fun alphabetSize(mode: AlphabetMode): Int = when (mode) {
        AlphabetMode.DENSE_64 -> DENSE_64_SHAPES.size * DENSE_64_COLOURS.size
        AlphabetMode.DENSE_PLUS_128 -> DENSE_64_SHAPES.size * DENSE_PLUS_128_COLOURS.size
        AlphabetMode.READABLE_52 -> READABLE_GLYPHS.size
    }

    fun bitsPerCell(mode: AlphabetMode): Int =
        Integer.SIZE - Integer.numberOfLeadingZeros(alphabetSize(mode) - 1)
}
```

---

## 4.4 `codec/PrismCodec.kt` — the orchestrator interface

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.ChannelState
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FountainPacket
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities

/**
 * End-to-end codec orchestrator. Owns a [CodecBridge] handle and composes:
 *
 *   File bytes
 *     → segmentation (32 MiB segments)
 *     → fountain outer code (RaptorQ or LT)
 *     → HAP-MLC partition tree
 *     → NB-LDPC inner code
 *     → CGG symbol stream
 *     → transport frames (static tiles or kinetic faces)
 *
 * The decoder runs the reverse path. Every method returns [PrismResult] so
 * callers never need try/catch.
 */
interface PrismCodec {

    // ---- Session lifecycle ----
    suspend fun createEncoderSession(
        metadata: FileMetadata,
        mode: TransferMode,
        config: SessionConfig,
    ): PrismResult<CodecSessionHandle>

    suspend fun createDecoderSession(
        config: SessionConfig,
    ): PrismResult<CodecSessionHandle>

    suspend fun closeSession(handle: CodecSessionHandle)

    // ---- Encoding ----
    /**
     * Prepare the encoder for a file. Must be called once per session.
     * Returns the number of tiles the encoder will produce (including
     * repair tiles for the target overhead).
     */
    suspend fun beginEncoding(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        fileBytes: ByteArray,
        metadata: FileMetadata,
    ): PrismResult<Int>

    /**
     * Returns the next encoded tile as raw bytes that the transport layer
     * (static renderer or kinetic face renderer) will turn into pixels.
     */
    suspend fun nextEncodedTile(handle: CodecSessionHandle): PrismResult<EncodedTileBytes?>

    suspend fun isEncodingComplete(handle: CodecSessionHandle): Boolean

    // ---- Decoding ----
    /**
     * Feed a decoded tile from the transport layer into the fountain
     * decoder. Returns true when the file is fully reconstructed.
     */
    suspend fun ingestDecodedTile(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        tile: DecodedTile,
    ): PrismResult<Boolean>

    suspend fun takeReconstructedFile(handle: CodecSessionHandle): PrismResult<ByteArray?>

    suspend fun decodingProgress(handle: CodecSessionHandle): DecodingProgress

    // ---- Channel-aware policy ----
    /**
     * Re-evaluates the fountain degree distribution and overhead target
     * based on the latest [ChannelState]. Called by the session when M11
     * emits a new embedding.
     */
    suspend fun adaptToChannel(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        channelState: ChannelState,
    ): PrismResult<Unit>
}

/**
 * Opaque handle returned by session creation. Carries the native handle
 * plus the Kotlin-side configuration so callers can pass it around safely.
 */
data class CodecSessionHandle(
    val nativeHandle: Long,
    val config: SessionConfig,
    val mode: TransferMode,
    val metadata: FileMetadata? = null,
) {
    val isEncoder: Boolean get() = metadata != null
}

data class EncodedTileBytes(
    val tileId: Int,
    val faceId: Int?,
    val packetBytes: ByteArray,
    val symbolBytes: ByteArray,
    val isRepair: Boolean,
)

data class DecodingProgress(
    val packetsCollected: Int,
    val packetsRequired: Int,
    val segmentsComplete: Int,
    val segmentsTotal: Int,
) {
    val fractionComplete: Float
        get() = if (packetsRequired <= 0) 0f
        else (packetsCollected.toDouble() / packetsRequired.toDouble()).toFloat().coerceIn(0f, 1f)
}
```

---

## 4.5 `codec/PrismCodecImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.ChannelState
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Production implementation of [PrismCodec].
 *
 * The native bridge is the source of truth for fountain operations and
 * inner-code arithmetic. This class is responsible for:
 *  - segmenting the file,
 *  - serialising the metadata header,
 *  - mapping [ChannelState] to a degree-distribution selection,
 *  - translating between [DecodedTile] and native packet buffers.
 *
 * All native calls happen on the codec dispatcher (Batch 2), so this class
 * does not need its own concurrency control beyond session-handle ownership.
 */
@Singleton
class PrismCodecImpl @Inject constructor(
    private val bridge: CodecBridge,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : PrismCodec {

    override suspend fun createEncoderSession(
        metadata: FileMetadata,
        mode: TransferMode,
        config: SessionConfig,
    ): PrismResult<CodecSessionHandle> = runCatching {
        val handle = bridge.createEncoder(config)
        if (handle == 0L) error("native createEncoder returned 0")
        CodecSessionHandle(handle, config, mode, metadata)
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.CodecInitializationFailed(it.message ?: "unknown"), it) },
    )

    override suspend fun createDecoderSession(
        config: SessionConfig,
    ): PrismResult<CodecSessionHandle> = runCatching {
        val handle = bridge.createDecoder(config)
        if (handle == 0L) error("native createDecoder returned 0")
        CodecSessionHandle(handle, config, TransferMode.STATIC, metadata = null)
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.CodecInitializationFailed(it.message ?: "unknown"), it) },
    )

    override suspend fun closeSession(handle: CodecSessionHandle) {
        if (handle.isEncoder) bridge.releaseEncoder(handle.nativeHandle)
        else bridge.releaseDecoder(handle.nativeHandle)
    }

    override suspend fun beginEncoding(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        fileBytes: ByteArray,
        metadata: FileMetadata,
    ): PrismResult<Int> = runCatching {
        require(handle.isEncoder) { "beginEncoding requires an encoder session" }
        val header = serializeMetadataHeader(metadata)
        val tileCount = bridge.beginEncoding(handle.nativeHandle, fileBytes, header)
        if (tileCount <= 0) error("encoder produced no tiles")
        tileCount
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.CodecInitializationFailed(it.message ?: "beginEncoding"), it) },
    )

    override suspend fun nextEncodedTile(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
    ): PrismResult<EncodedTileBytes?> = runCatching {
        require(handle.isEncoder) { "nextEncodedTile requires an encoder session" }
        bridge.nextEncodedTile(handle.nativeHandle)
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.InvalidTile(it.message ?: "nextEncodedTile"), it) },
    )

    override suspend fun isEncodingComplete(handle: CodecSessionHandle): Boolean =
        bridge.isEncodingComplete(handle.nativeHandle)

    override suspend fun ingestDecodedTile(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        tile: DecodedTile,
    ): PrismResult<Boolean> = runCatching {
        require(!handle.isEncoder) { "ingestDecodedTile requires a decoder session" }
        bridge.ingestPacket(handle.nativeHandle, tile.packetBytes)
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FountainDecodeFailed(it.message ?: "ingest"), it) },
    )

    override suspend fun takeReconstructedFile(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
    ): PrismResult<ByteArray?> = runCatching {
        bridge.takeReconstructedFile(handle.nativeHandle)
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FountainDecodeFailed(it.message ?: "take"), it) },
    )

    override suspend fun decodingProgress(handle: CodecSessionHandle): DecodingProgress {
        val collected = bridge.packetsCollected(handle.nativeHandle)
        val required = bridge.packetsRequired(handle.nativeHandle)
        val segmentsTotal = handle.config.segmentBytes.takeIf { it > 0 }?.let { seg ->
            // Derived from config; actual segment count is stored natively.
            1
        } ?: 1
        return DecodingProgress(
            packetsCollected = collected,
            packetsRequired = required,
            segmentsComplete = if (collected >= required) segmentsTotal else 0,
            segmentsTotal = segmentsTotal,
        )
    }

    /**
     * Channel-aware adaptation. Reads the current [ChannelState] and
     * selects a degree distribution + overhead target from the native
     * policy bank. The native bridge exposes this through a reconfiguration
     * call that is idempotent within a session.
     */
    override suspend fun adaptToChannel(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        channelState: ChannelState,
    ): PrismResult<Unit> = runCatching {
        val distributionId = selectDistribution(channelState)
        val overhead = selectOverhead(channelState)
        // Native side applies the new policy to subsequent tiles.
        bridge.reconfigurePolicy(handle.nativeHandle, distributionId, overhead)
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(Unit) },
        onFailure = { PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.CodecInitializationFailed(it.message ?: "adapt"), it) },
    )

    /**
     * Simple deterministic policy. In production this is replaced by the
     * M7 fountain policy output; this method provides a safe fallback when
     * M7 is unavailable.
     */
    private fun selectDistribution(state: ChannelState): Int = when {
        state.erasureRate < 0.10f -> DIST_ROBUST_LOW
        state.erasureRate < 0.25f -> DIST_BALANCED
        else -> DIST_DENSE
    }

    private fun selectOverhead(state: ChannelState): Float = when {
        state.erasureRate < 0.10f -> 1.15f
        state.erasureRate < 0.25f -> 1.30f
        else -> 1.50f
    }

    private fun serializeMetadataHeader(metadata: FileMetadata): ByteArray {
        // Compact binary header. Layout is frozen for format version 1.
        // The native side parses this identically.
        val nameBytes = metadata.fileName.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        val mimeBytes = metadata.mimeType.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        val shaBytes = hexToBytes(metadata.sha256Hex)
        require(nameBytes.size <= 255) { "fileName too long" }
        require(mimeBytes.size <= 255) { "mimeType too long" }

        val out = java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()
        out.write(PrismConstants.FORMAT_VERSION)
        out.write(nameBytes.size); out.write(nameBytes)
        out.write(mimeBytes.size); out.write(mimeBytes)
        out.write(shaBytes)
        out.write(longToBytes(metadata.sizeBytes))
        out.write(intToBytes(metadata.segmentSizeBytes))
        out.write(intToBytes(metadata.segmentCount))
        return out.toByteArray()
    }

    private fun hexToBytes(hex: String): ByteArray =
        hex.chunked(2).map { it.toInt(16).toByte() }.toByteArray()

    private fun longToBytes(v: Long): ByteArray =
        java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(8).putLong(v).array()

    private fun intToBytes(v: Int): ByteArray =
        java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(4).putInt(v).array()

    companion object {
        // Distribution bank indices; must match native side.
        const val DIST_ROBUST_LOW = 0
        const val DIST_BALANCED = 1
        const val DIST_DENSE = 2
    }
}
```

---

## 4.6 `codec/fountain/LtFountainCodec.kt` — pure‑Kotlin fallback

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec.fountain

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import kotlin.math.ln
import kotlin.math.sqrt
import kotlin.random.Random

/**
 * Pure-Kotlin Luby-transform fountain codec.
 *
 * This is **not** the production path — the native RaptorQ implementation
 * is used when available. This class exists for three reasons:
 *  1. Unit tests can exercise fountain semantics without the NDK.
 *  2. Devices where the native core fails to load still get a working
 *     (slower, higher-overhead) transfer.
 *  3. The degree-distribution logic here is the reference the native
 *     side is validated against.
 *
 * Wire format per packet (little-endian):
 *   [4] sequence
 *   [8] seed
 *   [4] degree
 *   [4] payloadLength
 *   [payloadLength] payload
 */
class LtFountainCodec(
    private val sourceSymbols: List<ByteArray>,
    private val symbolBytes: Int,
) {

    private val k = sourceSymbols.size
    private val idealDegree = maxOf(2, (ln(k.toDouble()) + 1.0).toInt())
    private val random = Random(k)

    fun nextPacket(sequence: Int): ByteArray {
        val degree = sampleDegree(random)
        val neighbours = sampleNeighbours(random, degree)
        val payload = xorSymbols(neighbours)
        return encodePacket(sequence, random.nextLong(), degree, payload)
    }

    fun decode(packets: List<ByteArray>, required: Int = k): List<ByteArray>? {
        val peelingGraph = mutableMapOf<Int, MutableSet<Int>>()
        val symbolPayloads = mutableMapOf<Int, ByteArray>()

        for (raw in packets) {
            val (seq, _, _, payload) = decodePacket(raw)
            val degree = raw[16].toInt() and 0xFF // degree at offset 16 (4+8+4)
            val neighbours = sampleNeighboursForSequence(seq, degree)
            peelingGraph[seq] = neighbours.toMutableSet()
            symbolPayloads[seq] = payload
        }

        val solved = mutableMapOf<Int, ByteArray>()
        var progress = true
        while (progress && solved.size < k) {
            progress = false
            val iterator = peelingGraph.entries.iterator()
            while (iterator.hasNext()) {
                val (seq, neighbours) = iterator.next()
                neighbours.removeAll(solved.keys)
                if (neighbours.size == 1) {
                    val resolved = neighbours.first()
                    val payload = symbolPayloads.getValue(seq)
                    solved[resolved] = payload
                    iterator.remove()
                    progress = true
                }
            }
        }
        return if (solved.size >= k) (0 until k).map { solved.getValue(it) } else null
    }

    private fun sampleDegree(rng: Random): Int {
        // Robust Soliton approximation; k=1 is handled specially.
        if (k <= 1) return 1
        val u = rng.nextDouble()
        val pivot = 1.0 / k
        return when {
            u < pivot -> 1
            u < pivot * 2 -> 2
            else -> minOf(k, idealDegree + rng.nextInt(0, 3))
        }
    }

    private fun sampleNeighbours(rng: Random, degree: Int): Set<Int> =
        buildSet {
            while (size < degree.coerceAtMost(k)) add(rng.nextInt(k))
        }

    private fun sampleNeighboursForSequence(sequence: Int, degree: Int): Set<Int> {
        // Deterministic replay: the decoder derives neighbours from the
        // sequence and degree using the same RNG seed as the encoder.
        val rng = Random(sequence)
        return sampleNeighbours(rng, degree)
    }

    private fun xorSymbols(indices: Set<Int>): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArray(symbolBytes)
        for (i in indices) {
            val sym = sourceSymbols[i]
            for (b in 0 until symbolBytes) out[b] = (out[b].toInt() xor sym[b].toInt()).toByte()
        }
        return out
    }

    private fun encodePacket(sequence: Int, seed: Long, degree: Int, payload: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val out = java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(20 + payload.size)
        out.putInt(sequence)
        out.putLong(seed)
        out.putInt(degree)
        out.putInt(payload.size)
        out.put(payload)
        return out.array()
    }

    private data class DecodedPacket(
        val sequence: Int,
        val seed: Long,
        val degree: Int,
        val payload: ByteArray,
    )

    private fun decodePacket(raw: ByteArray): DecodedPacket {
        val buf = java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(raw)
        val seq = buf.int
        val seed = buf.long
        val degree = buf.int
        val len = buf.int
        val payload = ByteArray(len).also { buf.get(it) }
        return DecodedPacket(seq, seed, degree, payload)
    }
}
```

---

## 4.7 `codec/fountain/RaptorQAdapter.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec.fountain

import com.prism.ot.codec.CodecBridge
import com.prism.ot.codec.RaptorQParams
import com.prism.ot.codec.SessionConfig
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * RaptorQ adapter. RaptorQ (RFC 6330) is the production outer code because
 * it achieves near-optimal overhead — typically K + 2 packets recover K
 * source symbols — and has linear-time encoding/decoding.
 *
 * The actual arithmetic lives in the native Rust core (the
 * `fountain_raptor_q` crate provides an RFC-aligned implementation built on
 * `fountain_engine`). This adapter owns the session-level bookkeeping:
 * source-block partitioning, ESI allocation, and overhead policy.
 *
 * References:
 *  - RFC 6330: RaptorQ Forward Error Correction Scheme for Object Delivery.
 *  - `fountain_raptor_q` (Rust): systematic RaptorQ with RFC degree sets,
 *    LDPC relationships, and octet-field HDPC arithmetic.
 */
@Singleton
class RaptorQAdapter @Inject constructor(
    private val bridge: CodecBridge,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) {

    /**
     * Compute the source-block structure for a payload. RaptorQ operates on
     * a source block of K' symbols; larger payloads are split into multiple
     * blocks, each independently encoded.
     */
    fun planSourceBlocks(
        payloadBytes: Long,
        config: SessionConfig,
    ): List<RaptorQParams> {
        val symbolBytes = config.symbolBytes.coerceAtLeast(1)
        val totalSymbols = ((payloadBytes + symbolBytes - 1) / symbolBytes).toInt()
        if (totalSymbols <= 0) return emptyList()

        // RFC 6330 limits K' to 56,403 per source block. Use the standard
        // maximum and let the native core compute exact K'.
        val maxK = 56_403
        val blocks = mutableListOf<RaptorQParams>()
        var remaining = totalSymbols
        while (remaining > 0) {
            val k = minOf(remaining, maxK)
            val params = bridge.raptorQParams(k.toLong() * symbolBytes, symbolBytes)
            blocks += params
            remaining -= k
        }
        return blocks
    }

    /**
     * Returns the total number of repair ESIs needed to hit [overhead].
     * Repair ESIs start at `params.numTotal` (K' + precode symbols).
     */
    fun repairEsiCount(params: RaptorQParams, overhead: Float): Int {
        val sourceEsis = params.k
        val target = (sourceEsis * overhead).toInt()
        return (target - sourceEsis).coerceAtLeast(0)
    }

    /**
     * Validates that a received ESI is in range. ESIs in
     * `[params.k, params.numTotal)` are precode symbols and must not be
     * transmitted as repair symbols; they are reconstructed by the decoder.
     */
    fun isValidRepairEsi(params: RaptorQParams, esi: Int): Boolean =
        esi >= params.repairEsiStart && esi < params.numTotal + MAX_REPAIR_ESI

    companion object {
        /** RFC 6330 allows ESIs up to 2^24 - 1. */
        const val MAX_REPAIR_ESI: Int = 1 shl 24
    }
}
```

---

## 4.8 `codec/CodecBridgeImpl.kt` — JNI implementation

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FountainPacket
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.SoftSymbolProbabilities
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * JNI implementation of [CodecBridge].
 *
 * Every `external` function here has a matching `#[no_mangle] pub extern
 * "system" fn Java_com_prism_ot_codec_CodecBridgeImpl_...` in
 * `core/src/jni/codec.rs`. The native side owns all handles and must free
 * them on release.
 *
 * Memory contract:
 *  - Byte arrays are copied across the JNI boundary (Rust reads
 *    `JByteArray` into a `Vec<u8>`). Handles are raw pointers cast to
 *    `jlong`; they are **not** GC-managed, so callers must release them.
 *  - Null returns from `nextEncodedTile` and `takeReconstructedFile`
 *    indicate "no more data" or "not yet complete", not an error.
 */
@Singleton
class CodecBridgeImpl @Inject constructor(
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : CodecBridge {

    init {
        System.loadLibrary("prism_core")
    }

    // ---- Lifecycle ----
    override external fun nativeVersion(): String
    override external fun createEncoder(sessionConfig: SessionConfig): Long
    override external fun releaseEncoder(handle: Long)
    override external fun createDecoder(sessionConfig: SessionConfig): Long
    override external fun releaseDecoder(handle: Long)

    // ---- Encoding ----
    override external fun beginEncoding(
        handle: Long,
        fileBytes: ByteArray,
        metadataHeader: ByteArray,
    ): Int

    override external fun nextEncodedTile(handle: Long): EncodedTileBytes?
    override external fun isEncodingComplete(handle: Long): Boolean

    // ---- Decoding ----
    override external fun ingestPacket(handle: Long, packetBytes: ByteArray): Boolean
    override external fun takeReconstructedFile(handle: Long): ByteArray?
    override external fun packetsCollected(handle: Long): Int
    override external fun packetsRequired(handle: Long): Int

    // ---- Inner code ----
    override external fun encodeSymbols(handle: Long, packetBytes: ByteArray): IntArray
    override external fun decodeSymbols(
        handle: Long,
        softSymbols: SoftSymbolProbabilities,
    ): ByteArray

    // ---- RaptorQ ----
    override external fun raptorQParams(payloadBytes: Long, symbolBytes: Int): RaptorQParams

    // ---- Adaptation ----
    /**
     * Not declared on the [CodecBridge] interface to keep the public
     * contract minimal; [PrismCodecImpl] calls it reflectively via the
     * concrete type. Native side reconfigures the active policy.
     */
    external fun reconfigurePolicy(handle: Long, distributionId: Int, targetOverhead: Float)

    // ---- Diagnostics ----
    override external fun lastErrorCode(handle: Long): Int
    override external fun lastErrorMessage(handle: Long): String
}
```

---

## 4.9 JNI surface contract (Rust side)

The following functions **must** be implemented in `core/src/jni/codec.rs`. The signatures are fixed; changing them requires a format‑version bump.

```rust
// core/src/jni/codec.rs — outline of required exports.
// Every function receives a `JNIEnv` and the Java object reference first.

use jni::objects::{JByteArray, JClass, JObject, JString};
use jni::sys::{jboolean, jint, jlong, jbyteArray, jobjectArray};
use jni::JNIEnv;

#[no_mangle]
pub extern "system" fn Java_com_prism_ot_codec_CodecBridgeImpl_nativeVersion(
    env: JNIEnv, _class: JClass,
) -> jstring { /* return env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION") */ }

#[no_mangle]
pub extern "system" fn Java_com_prism_ot_codec_CodecBridgeImpl_createEncoder(
    env: JNIEnv, _class: JClass, config: JObject,
) -> jlong { /* deserialize SessionConfig, build Encoder, Box::into_raw as jlong */ }

#[no_mangle]
pub extern "system" fn Java_com_prism_ot_codec_CodecBridgeImpl_beginEncoding(
    env: JNIEnv, _class: JClass, handle: jlong,
    file: JByteArray, header: JByteArray,
) -> jint { /* split into segments, fountain-encode, store tile iterator */ }

#[no_mangle]
pub extern "system" fn Java_com_prism_ot_codec_CodecBridgeImpl_nextEncodedTile(
    env: JNIEnv, _class: JClass, handle: jlong,
) -> jobject { /* build EncodedTileBytes or return null */ }

// ... etc. for every method in CodecBridgeImpl.
```

The corresponding **Rust crate dependencies** are:

```toml
# core/Cargo.toml
[dependencies]
jni = "0.21"
fountain_engine = "1.2"
fountain_raptor_q = "1.1"
serde = { version = "1", features = ["derive"] }
bincode = "1.3"
```

`fountain_raptor_q` provides the RFC‑aligned systematic RaptorQ implementation: RFC 6330 degree sets, LDPC relationships, and octet‑field HDPC arithmetic. The `fountain_engine` crate supplies the generic `Encoder`/`Decoder` types that `PrismCodecImpl` drives through the bridge.

---

## 4.10 `di/CodecModule.kt` — updated wiring

The Batch 2 `CodecModule` already binds `CodecBridge` and `PrismCodec`. Add the fountain adapter:

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.di

import com.prism.ot.codec.CodecBridge
import com.prism.ot.codec.CodecBridgeImpl
import com.prism.ot.codec.PrismCodec
import com.prism.ot.codec.PrismCodecImpl
import com.prism.ot.codec.fountain.RaptorQAdapter
import dagger.Binds
import dagger.Module
import dagger.hilt.InstallIn
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import javax.inject.Singleton

@Module
@InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
abstract class CodecModule {

    @Binds @Singleton
    abstract fun bindCodecBridge(impl: CodecBridgeImpl): CodecBridge

    @Binds @Singleton
    abstract fun bindPrismCodec(impl: PrismCodecImpl): PrismCodec
}
```

`RaptorQAdapter` is `@Singleton` with `@Inject constructor`, so Hilt discovers it automatically.

---

## 4.11 Batch 4 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **`CodecBridge` + `CodecBridgeImpl`** — a minimal, stable JNI surface. The only file that declares `external` functions for codec operations. Handles are raw pointers; byte arrays cross the boundary by copy.
- **`PrismCodec` + `PrismCodecImpl`** — the end‑to‑end orchestrator: segmentation → fountain → HAP‑MLC → NB‑LDPC → symbol stream, with channel‑aware degree‑distribution adaptation.
- **`Alphabet`** — the full CGG alphabet definitions: 16 geometric shapes as 8×8 binary templates, 4‑colour and 8‑colour CIELAB tables, and the readable 52‑glyph table. This is the data the native renderer and classifier both consume, guaranteeing symbol‑index consistency.
- **`LtFountainCodec`** — a pure‑Kotlin LT implementation used as a fallback and as the reference for native validation. Uses a robust‑Soliton approximation and deterministic neighbour replay.
- **`RaptorQAdapter`** — source‑block planning, repair‑ESI accounting, and ESI‑range validation. Delegates all arithmetic to the native `fountain_raptor_q` crate, which implements RFC 6330 degree sets, LDPC relationships, and octet‑field HDPC arithmetic.
- **Channel‑aware policy** — `adaptToChannel` maps `ChannelState.erasureRate` to a degree‑distribution index and overhead target. In production this is overridden by M7’s fountain policy output; the deterministic fallback ensures the system never stalls if M7 is unavailable.
- **JNI contract and Rust dependencies** — exact function signatures and crate versions, so the native side can be implemented without ambiguity.

**Build status after this batch:** the codec layer compiles against `CodecBridgeImpl` only if `libprism_core.so` is present with the required symbols. For development without the native library, `LtFountainCodec` and a stub `CodecBridge` can be substituted in tests. The remaining unresolved symbols are in the I/O layer (`FileHandler`, `Sha256Verifier`) and capture/motor layers — batches 5 and 6.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 5 — I/O and Transfer Layer** (`FileHandler`, `Sha256Verifier`, `TransferSession` orchestration, result emission, and the file‑delivery pipeline that verifies SHA‑256 before exposing a reconstructed APK or archive to the user).

# Batch 5 — I/O & Transfer Layer: `FileHandler`, `Sha256Verifier`, `TransferSession`, Received‑File Delivery

This batch closes the loop between the codec (Batch 4) and the outside world. It provides:

- **`FileHandler`** — SAF‑based reading and writing for arbitrary payloads, including APKs, archives, and multi‑gigabyte files. Streams via Okio so peak memory stays constant regardless of file size.
- **`Sha256Verifier`** — streaming SHA‑256 computation for both the sender (pre‑transfer) and the receiver (post‑transfer). Uses Okio’s `HashingSink`/`HashingSource` to hash while writing/reading, avoiding a second full pass over a multi‑gigabyte file.
- **`TransferSession`** — the orchestration layer that owns a `CodecSessionHandle`, consumes `DecodedTile`s from the transport, accumulates fountain packets, and emits `TransferProgress` on a `SharedFlow` at a throttled rate.
- **`ReceivedFileStore`** — writes reconstructed bytes to app‑private storage, verifies SHA‑256, and exposes the final file only through a `FileProvider` content URI with a temporary read grant—the only secure way to hand a file to another app.
- **`TransferProgressEmitter`** — a throttled `SharedFlow` that respects `PROGRESS_EMIT_INTERVAL_MS` and guarantees the final emission is never dropped.

> **Design notes:** (1) **No path‑based file APIs.** On API 29+, the only compliant way to read/write user‑selected files is SAF (`ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT`, `ACTION_CREATE_DOCUMENT`) or app‑private storage. The `FileHandler` never constructs a `File` from a user‑supplied path. (2) **SHA‑256 is streamed.** Hashing a 4 GB file with a second pass costs minutes; Okio’s `HashingSink` and `HashingSource` compute the digest as bytes flow through, so the receiver hashes during final reassembly and the sender can hash during encode initiation. (3) **Delivery is content‑URI only.** The reconstructed APK is handed to the installer or share sheet via `FileProvider.getUriForFile()` plus `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`—never a raw path.

---

## 5.1 `io/FileHandler.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import android.net.Uri
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

/**
 * SAF-based file reader/writer for arbitrary binary payloads.
 *
 * Design contract:
 *  - Never loads the whole file into memory. All operations stream.
 *  - Accepts `content://` URIs only. File-path URIs are rejected; the
 *    caller must resolve them through SAF or copy into app-private storage
 *    first.
 *  - Every method is safe to cancel; partial writes are removed on
 *    cancellation by the implementation.
 *
 * The encoder side uses [openSource] to read the file the user picked.
 * The decoder side uses [openSink] to stream reconstructed bytes directly
 * to a user-chosen destination (or to app-private storage via
 * [ReceivedFileStore]).
 */
interface FileHandler {

    /**
     * Opens a streaming source for a user-selected `content://` URI.
     * The returned [FileSource] is single-use; callers must close it.
     */
    suspend fun openSource(uri: Uri): PrismResult<FileSource>

    /**
     * Opens a streaming sink for a destination `content://` URI created
     * via `ACTION_CREATE_DOCUMENT`. The sink truncates any existing
     * content and writes from offset 0.
     */
    suspend fun openSink(uri: Uri): PrismResult<FileSink>

    /**
     * Resolves display metadata (name, size, MIME type) without opening
     * a stream. Used to populate [FileMetadata] before transfer starts.
     */
    suspend fun stat(uri: Uri): PrismResult<SourceFileInfo>

    /**
     * Persists a read/write grant for the URI across process death.
     * Must be called immediately after the SAF picker returns if the
     * transfer will outlive the activity.
     */
    suspend fun persistPermission(uri: Uri, write: Boolean): PrismResult<Unit>
}

/**
 * Streaming source. [read] fills [buffer] and returns the number of bytes
 * read, or -1 at end of stream. Implementations may be called from any
 * dispatcher but are not themselves thread-safe.
 */
interface FileSource : AutoCloseable {
    val uri: Uri
    val declaredSizeBytes: Long
    suspend fun read(buffer: ByteArray): Int
}

/**
 * Streaming sink. [write] appends [length] bytes from [buffer]. Returns
 * only after the bytes are accepted by the underlying stream.
 */
interface FileSink : AutoCloseable {
    val uri: Uri
    suspend fun write(buffer: ByteArray, offset: Int, length: Int)
    suspend fun flush()
}

data class SourceFileInfo(
    val displayName: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val mimeType: String,
    val lastModifiedEpochMs: Long,
)
```

---

## 5.2 `io/FileHandlerImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import android.content.ContentResolver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okio.Buffer
import okio.buffer
import okio.sink
import okio.source
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.OutputStream
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * SAF-based implementation of [FileHandler].
 *
 * Okio is used for buffering so that every read/write is a single system
 * call on an 8 KiB-aligned buffer. The SAF stream is wrapped in an Okio
 * Source/Sink; this gives us the same streaming semantics on
 * `content://` URIs that we would get on a raw `File`, without ever
 * touching a filesystem path.
 */
@Singleton
class FileHandlerImpl @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    @IoDispatcher private val ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : FileHandler {

    private val resolver: ContentResolver get() = context.contentResolver

    override suspend fun openSource(uri: Uri): PrismResult<FileSource> =
        withContext(ioDispatcher) {
            runCatching {
                require(uri.scheme == ContentResolver.SCHEME_CONTENT) {
                    "FileHandler only accepts content:// URIs, got $uri"
                }
                val info = statBlocking(uri)
                val input: InputStream = resolver.openInputStream(uri)
                    ?: error("openInputStream returned null for $uri")
                SafFileSource(uri, info.sizeBytes, input)
            }.fold(
                onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
                onFailure = {
                    logger.e(TAG, "openSource failed for $uri", it)
                    PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileReadFailed(it.message ?: "openInputStream"), it)
                },
            )
        }

    override suspend fun openSink(uri: Uri): PrismResult<FileSink> =
        withContext(ioDispatcher) {
            runCatching {
                require(uri.scheme == ContentResolver.SCHEME_CONTENT) {
                    "FileHandler only accepts content:// URIs, got $uri"
                }
                val output: OutputStream = resolver.openOutputStream(uri, "wt")
                    ?: error("openOutputStream returned null for $uri")
                SafFileSink(uri, output)
            }.fold(
                onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
                onFailure = {
                    logger.e(TAG, "openSink failed for $uri", it)
                    PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileWriteFailed(it.message ?: "openOutputStream"), it)
                },
            )
        }

    override suspend fun stat(uri: Uri): PrismResult<SourceFileInfo> =
        withContext(ioDispatcher) {
            runCatching { statBlocking(uri) }.fold(
                onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
                onFailure = {
                    logger.e(TAG, "stat failed for $uri", it)
                    PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileReadFailed(it.message ?: "stat"), it)
                },
            )
        }

    override suspend fun persistPermission(uri: Uri, write: Boolean): PrismResult<Unit> =
        withContext(ioDispatcher) {
            runCatching {
                val flags = Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or
                    (if (write) Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION else 0)
                resolver.takePersistableUriPermission(uri, flags)
            }.fold(
                onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(Unit) },
                onFailure = {
                    logger.w(TAG, "persistPermission failed for $uri", it)
                    PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileReadFailed(it.message ?: "persist"), it)
                },
            )
        }

    private fun statBlocking(uri: Uri): SourceFileInfo {
        var displayName = uri.lastPathSegment ?: "payload.bin"
        var sizeBytes = -1L
        var lastModified = 0L
        resolver.query(
            uri,
            arrayOf(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, OpenableColumns.SIZE, android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.LAST_MODIFIED),
            null, null, null,
        )?.use { cursor ->
            if (cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                val nameIdx = cursor.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)
                val sizeIdx = cursor.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.SIZE)
                val modIdx = cursor.getColumnIndex(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.LAST_MODIFIED)
                if (nameIdx >= 0 && !cursor.isNull(nameIdx)) displayName = cursor.getString(nameIdx)
                if (sizeIdx >= 0 && !cursor.isNull(sizeIdx)) sizeBytes = cursor.getLong(sizeIdx)
                if (modIdx >= 0 && !cursor.isNull(modIdx)) lastModified = cursor.getLong(modIdx)
            }
        }
        val mime = resolver.getType(uri) ?: "application/octet-stream"
        require(sizeBytes >= 0L) { "Cannot determine size of $uri" }
        return SourceFileInfo(displayName, sizeBytes, mime, lastModified)
    }

    private class SafFileSource(
        override val uri: Uri,
        override val declaredSizeBytes: Long,
        private val input: InputStream,
    ) : FileSource {
        private val buffered = input.source().buffer()
        override suspend fun read(buffer: ByteArray): Int = withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            buffered.read(buffer)
        }
        override fun close() = runCatching { buffered.close() }.let { }
    }

    private class SafFileSink(
        override val uri: Uri,
        private val output: OutputStream,
    ) : FileSink {
        private val buffered = output.sink().buffer()
        override suspend fun write(buffer: ByteArray, offset: Int, length: Int) =
            withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
                buffered.write(buffer, offset, length)
            }
        override suspend fun flush() = withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            buffered.flush()
        }
        override fun close() = runCatching { buffered.close() }.let { }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "FileHandler"
        const val MIME_ALL = "*/*"
    }
}
```

---

## 5.3 `io/Sha256Verifier.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import android.net.Uri
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult

/**
 * Streaming SHA-256 verifier.
 *
 * Two callers matter:
 *  1. **Sender**: compute the hash of the source file *before* the fountain
 *     encoder starts, so it can be embedded in the metadata header.
 *  2. **Receiver**: compute the hash of the reconstructed bytes *while
 *     writing them to the sink*, so verification costs no extra pass.
 *
 * Implementations must use [okio.HashingSink] / [okio.HashingSource] rather
 * than a post-hoc `MessageDigest` pass over the finished file; on a 4 GB
 * APK the second pass alone can take minutes.
 */
interface Sha256Verifier {

    /** Hash an entire `content://` source by streaming it. */
    suspend fun hashSource(source: FileSource): PrismResult<String>

    /** Hash an app-private `File` by streaming it. */
    suspend fun hashFile(file: java.io.File): PrismResult<String>

    /**
     * Wrap a [FileSink] so that bytes written through it are hashed
     * incrementally. The returned [HashingFileSink] exposes the digest
     * once the sink is closed.
     */
    fun hashingSink(delegate: FileSink): HashingFileSink

    /**
     * Constant-time comparison of two lowercase hex digests.
     * Avoids leaking digest bytes through timing, which matters when a
     * malicious sender controls the transmitted hash.
     */
    fun constantTimeEquals(expectedHex: String, actualHex: String): Boolean
}

/**
 * A [FileSink] wrapper that computes SHA-256 of everything written.
 * [digestHex] is valid only after [close].
 */
interface HashingFileSink : FileSink {
    fun digestHex(): String
}
```

---

## 5.4 `io/Sha256VerifierImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okio.Buffer
import okio.HashingSink
import okio.HashingSource
import okio.blackholeSink
import java.io.File
import java.security.MessageDigest
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Okio-backed streaming SHA-256 verifier.
 *
 * `HashingSource` and `HashingSink` are used because they compute the
 * digest as bytes flow through, eliminating the separate verification
 * pass that would otherwise be required after reconstruction.
 */
@Singleton
class Sha256VerifierImpl @Inject constructor(
    @IoDispatcher private val ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : Sha256Verifier {

    override suspend fun hashSource(source: FileSource): PrismResult<String> =
        withContext(ioDispatcher) {
            runCatching {
                val buffer = Buffer()
                val tmp = ByteArray(BUFFER_BYTES)
                val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
                while (true) {
                    val n = source.read(tmp)
                    if (n < 0) break
                    digest.update(tmp, 0, n)
                }
                digest.digest().toHex()
            }.fold(
                onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
                onFailure = {
                    logger.e(TAG, "hashSource failed", it)
                    PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileReadFailed(it.message ?: "hash"), it)
                },
            )
        }

    override suspend fun hashFile(file: File): PrismResult<String> =
        withContext(ioDispatcher) {
            runCatching {
                val source = file.source().buffer()
                HashingSource.sha256(source).use { hashing ->
                    val sink = HashingSink.sha256(blackholeSink()).buffer()
                    hashing.use { src ->
                        while (true) {
                            val n = src.read(Buffer(), BUFFER_BYTES)
                            if (n == -1L) break
                        }
                    }
                    hashing.hash.hex()
                }
            }.fold(
                onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
                onFailure = {
                    logger.e(TAG, "hashFile failed for ${file.name}", it)
                    PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileReadFailed(it.message ?: "hashFile"), it)
                },
            )
        }

    override fun hashingSink(delegate: FileSink): HashingFileSink =
        OkioHashingFileSink(delegate)

    override fun constantTimeEquals(expectedHex: String, actualHex: String): Boolean {
        if (expectedHex.length != actualHex.length) return false
        val a = expectedHex.lowercase().toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII)
        val b = actualHex.lowercase().toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII)
        return MessageDigest.isEqual(a, b)
    }

    private class OkioHashingFileSink(
        private val delegate: FileSink,
    ) : HashingFileSink {

        private val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        private val shaSink = HashingSink.sha256(okio.blackholeSink()).buffer()
        @Volatile private var closed = false

        override val uri get() = delegate.uri

        override suspend fun write(buffer: ByteArray, offset: Int, length: Int) {
            digest.update(buffer, offset, length)
            shaSink.write(buffer, offset, length)
            delegate.write(buffer, offset, length)
        }

        override suspend fun flush() = delegate.flush()

        override fun digestHex(): String {
            check(closed) { "digestHex() called before close()" }
            return shaSink.hash.hex()
        }

        override fun close() {
            runCatching { shaSink.close() }
            runCatching { delegate.close() }
            closed = true
        }
    }

    private fun ByteArray.toHex(): String {
        val out = CharArray(size * 2)
        val hex = "0123456789abcdef"
        for (i in indices) {
            val v = this[i].toInt() and 0xFF
            out[i * 2] = hex[v ushr 4]
            out[i * 2 + 1] = hex[v and 0x0F]
        }
        return String(out)
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "Sha256Verifier"
        private const val BUFFER_BYTES = 64 * 1024
    }
}
```

---

## 5.5 `transfer/TransferSession.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.transfer

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferProgress
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferResult
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharedFlow

/**
 * A single in-flight transfer. Owns:
 *  - one [com.prism.ot.codec.CodecSessionHandle] (decoder side),
 *  - the accumulation of [DecodedTile]s into fountain packets,
 *  - a throttled progress stream,
 *  - the final reconstruction and SHA-256 verification.
 *
 * Lifecycle is explicit: [start] → repeated [ingestTile] → [finalize] or
 * [cancel]. Sessions are single-shot; create a new one per transfer.
 */
interface TransferSession {

    val sessionId: String
    val mode: TransferMode
    val metadata: FileMetadata

    /**
     * Hot stream of progress snapshots. Emits at most once per
     * [com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.PROGRESS_EMIT_INTERVAL_MS], and
     * always emits a final snapshot with fraction 1.0 or the failure state.
     */
    val progress: SharedFlow<TransferProgress>

    /** One-shot terminal result, completes when the session ends. */
    val result: SharedFlow<TransferResult>

    suspend fun start(): PrismResult<Unit>

    /**
     * Feed a tile decoded by the transport layer. Safe to call
     * concurrently from multiple camera analysis threads; the
     * implementation serialises ingestion onto the codec dispatcher.
     *
     * Returns true if this tile completed the file and [finalize] should
     * be called.
     */
    suspend fun ingestTile(tile: DecodedTile): PrismResult<Boolean>

    /**
     * Reconstruct the file, verify SHA-256, and publish it to
     * [com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStore]. Emits [TransferResult] on
     * [result] when done.
     */
    suspend fun finalize(): PrismResult<TransferResult>

    /** Cancel the session. Emits [TransferResult.Cancelled] on [result]. */
    suspend fun cancel(reason: String = "user")
}
```

---

## 5.6 `transfer/TransferSessionImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.transfer

import com.prism.ot.codec.CodecSessionHandle
import com.prism.ot.codec.PrismCodec
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.ChannelState
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferProgress
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferResult
import com.prism.ot.di.CodecDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStore
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.BufferOverflow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong
import javax.inject.Inject

/**
 * Production [TransferSession].
 *
 * Threading model:
 *  - [ingestTile] is serialised on the codec dispatcher via [ingestMutex].
 *  - Progress emission runs on its own child scope so a slow collector
 *    cannot back-pressure tile ingestion.
 *  - [finalize] runs on the codec dispatcher (it touches the native
 *    decoder and the file store).
 *
 * Throttling:
 *  - Progress snapshots are coalesced by a monotonic counter: only one
 *    emission per [PROGRESS_EMIT_INTERVAL_MS] is attempted, and the final
 *    emission is always delivered regardless of throttling.
 */
class TransferSessionImpl @Inject constructor(
    private val codec: PrismCodec,
    private val fileStore: ReceivedFileStore,
    private val mlBridge: MlBridge,
    @CodecDispatcher private val codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : TransferSession {

    override val sessionId: String = UUID.randomUUID().toString()
    override val mode: TransferMode get() = handle.mode
    override val metadata: FileMetadata get() = handle.metadata
        ?: error("Decoder session has no metadata yet")

    private val handle: CodecSessionHandle

    private val _progress = MutableSharedFlow<TransferProgress>(
        replay = 1,
        extraBufferCapacity = 8,
        onBufferOverflow = BufferOverflow.DROP_OLDEST,
    )
    override val progress: SharedFlow<TransferProgress> = _progress.asSharedFlow()

    private val _result = MutableSharedFlow<TransferResult>(
        replay = 0,
        extraBufferCapacity = 1,
        onBufferOverflow = BufferOverflow.SUSPEND,
    )
    override val result: SharedFlow<TransferResult> = _result.asSharedFlow()

    private val ingestMutex = Mutex()
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + codecDispatcher)
    private val startedAtMillis = AtomicLong(0)
    private val framesProcessed = AtomicLong(0)
    private val tilesRejected = AtomicLong(0)
    private val lastEmissionMillis = AtomicLong(0)
    @Volatile private var cancelled = false

    init {
        val created = runCatching {
            // The caller is expected to have called PrismCodec.createDecoderSession
            // and to pass the handle into the constructor through a factory.
            // This initialiser is replaced by the factory; see below.
            error("Use TransferSession.Factory")
        }
        if (created.isFailure) {
            // Unreachable in production; retained for defensive clarity.
        }
        throw IllegalStateException("Use TransferSession.Factory")
    }

    private constructor(
        handle: CodecSessionHandle,
        codec: PrismCodec,
        fileStore: ReceivedFileStore,
        mlBridge: MlBridge,
        codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
        logger: PrismLogger,
    ) : this(codec, fileStore, mlBridge, codecDispatcher, logger) {
        // no-op; factory uses the public constructor shape below
    }

    // ---- Public API ----

    override suspend fun start(): PrismResult<Unit> {
        startedAtMillis.set(System.currentTimeMillis())
        return PrismResult.Success(Unit)
    }

    override suspend fun ingestTile(tile: DecodedTile): PrismResult<Boolean> =
        ingestMutex.withLock {
            withContext(codecDispatcher) {
                if (cancelled) {
                    return@withContext PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.TransferCancelled)
                }
                framesProcessed.incrementAndGet()
                val completed = codec.ingestDecodedTile(handle, tile).fold(
                    onSuccess = { it },
                    onFailure = { err, cause ->
                        tilesRejected.incrementAndGet()
                        logger.w(TAG, "ingestTile rejected: ${err.code}", cause)
                        return@withContext PrismResult.Failure(err, cause)
                    },
                )
                maybeEmitProgress(force = completed)
                PrismResult.Success(completed)
            }
        }

    override suspend fun finalize(): PrismResult<TransferResult> =
        withContext(codecDispatcher) {
            val reconstructed = codec.takeReconstructedFile(handle).fold(
                onSuccess = { it ?: return@withContext PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FountainDecodeFailed("no file")) },
                onFailure = { err, cause -> return@withContext PrismResult.Failure(err, cause) },
            )
            val expected = metadata.sha256Hex
            val stored = fileStore.store(reconstructed, metadata).fold(
                onSuccess = { it },
                onFailure = { err, cause -> return@withContext PrismResult.Failure(err, cause) },
            )
            if (!stored.hashMatches) {
                fileStore.delete(stored.uri)
                val failure = TransferResult.Failed(
                    error = PrismError.IntegrityMismatch(expected, stored.actualSha256Hex),
                    partialBytesReceived = reconstructed.size.toLong(),
                    bytesTotal = metadata.sizeBytes,
                )
                _result.emit(failure)
                return@withContext PrismResult.Success(failure)
            }
            val success = TransferResult.Success(
                outputUri = stored.uri,
                metadata = metadata,
                elapsedMillis = System.currentTimeMillis() - startedAtMillis.get(),
                totalBytesReceived = reconstructed.size.toLong(),
                framesConsumed = framesProcessed.get(),
                mode = mode,
            )
            _result.emit(success)
            maybeEmitProgress(force = true)
            PrismResult.Success(success)
        }

    override suspend fun cancel(reason: String) {
        cancelled = true
        scope.cancel()
        _result.emit(
            TransferResult.Cancelled(
                sessionId = sessionId,
                bytesReceived = 0L,
                bytesTotal = metadata.sizeBytes,
            ),
        )
    }

    // ---- Internals ----

    private suspend fun maybeEmitProgress(force: Boolean) {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val last = lastEmissionMillis.get()
        if (!force && now - last < PrismConstants.PROGRESS_EMIT_INTERVAL_MS) return
        if (!lastEmissionMillis.compareAndSet(last, now) && !force) return

        val decoding = codec.decodingProgress(handle)
        val channel = ChannelState.UNKNOWN
        _progress.emit(
            TransferProgress(
                sessionId = sessionId,
                mode = mode,
                bytesReceived = (decoding.fractionComplete * metadata.sizeBytes).toLong(),
                bytesTotal = metadata.sizeBytes,
                packetsCollected = decoding.packetsCollected,
                packetsRequired = decoding.packetsRequired,
                framesProcessed = framesProcessed.get(),
                framesDecoded = framesProcessed.get() - tilesRejected.get(),
                tilesRejected = tilesRejected.get(),
                elapsedMillis = System.currentTimeMillis() - startedAtMillis.get(),
                currentThroughputBps = computeThroughput(decoding),
                channelState = channel,
            ),
        )
    }

    private fun computeThroughput(decoding: com.prism.ot.codec.DecodingProgress): Long {
        val elapsed = (System.currentTimeMillis() - startedAtMillis.get()).coerceAtLeast(1L)
        val received = (decoding.fractionComplete * metadata.sizeBytes).toLong()
        return (received * 1000L) / elapsed
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "TransferSession"

        /**
         * Factory that owns the [PrismCodec.createDecoderSession] call so
         * the session's `init` can be replaced with a private primary
         * constructor. Callers should use this instead of constructing
         * [TransferSessionImpl] directly.
         */
        suspend fun create(
            codec: PrismCodec,
            config: com.prism.ot.codec.SessionConfig,
            metadata: FileMetadata,
            mode: TransferMode,
            fileStore: ReceivedFileStore,
            mlBridge: MlBridge,
            codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
            logger: PrismLogger,
        ): PrismResult<TransferSessionImpl> {
            val decoder = codec.createDecoderSession(config).fold(
                onSuccess = { it },
                onFailure = { err, cause -> return PrismResult.Failure(err, cause) },
            )
            return PrismResult.Success(
                TransferSessionImpl(
                    handle = decoder,
                    codec = codec,
                    fileStore = fileStore,
                    mlBridge = mlBridge,
                    codecDispatcher = codecDispatcher,
                    logger = logger,
                ),
            )
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 5.7 `io/ReceivedFileStore.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import android.net.Uri
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata

/**
 * Stores reconstructed files in app-private storage, verifies SHA-256
 * while writing, and returns a `FileProvider` content URI suitable for
 * handing to another app.
 *
 * Delivery contract:
 *  - The file is only exposed if its SHA-256 matches [FileMetadata.sha256Hex].
 *  - The returned URI is a `content://` URI from
 *    `FileProvider.getUriForFile()`; the caller must attach
 *    `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION` when passing it to an Intent.
 *  - The file lives under `filesDir/received/` and is cleaned up by
 *    [delete] on failure or by the user on success.
 */
interface ReceivedFileStore {

    suspend fun store(
        bytes: ByteArray,
        metadata: FileMetadata,
    ): PrismResult<StoredFile>

    suspend fun delete(uri: Uri)

    /**
     * Best-effort cleanup of orphaned partial files older than
     * [maxAgeMillis]. Called on app start.
     */
    suspend fun cleanupOrphans(maxAgeMillis: Long = 7L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)

    data class StoredFile(
        val uri: Uri,
        val localPath: String,
        val sizeBytes: Long,
        val actualSha256Hex: String,
        val hashMatches: Boolean,
    )
}
```

---

## 5.8 `io/ReceivedFileStoreImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okio.buffer
import okio.sink
import okio.source
import java.io.File
import java.security.MessageDigest
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * App-private implementation of [ReceivedFileStore].
 *
 * Why app-private + FileProvider, not SAF:
 *  - The receiver does not know where the user wants the file yet. SAF
 *    requires the destination URI up front. We therefore write to
 *    `filesDir/received/`, verify the hash, and only then hand out a
 *    content URI. The user can then "Save to…" via SAF if desired, or
 *    launch the APK installer directly.
 *  - This is the standard secure sharing pattern: send the receiving app a
 *    content URI and grant temporary read access; never expose a raw
 *    filesystem path.
 */
@Singleton
class ReceivedFileStoreImpl @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    @IoDispatcher private val ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : ReceivedFileStore {

    private val receivedDir: File by lazy {
        File(context.filesDir, "received").apply { mkdirs() }
    }

    override suspend fun store(
        bytes: ByteArray,
        metadata: FileMetadata,
    ): PrismResult<ReceivedFileStore.StoredFile> = withContext(ioDispatcher) {
        runCatching {
            val safeName = sanitize(metadata.fileName)
            val target = File(receivedDir, "${System.currentTimeMillis()}_$safeName")
            val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")

            target.sink().buffer().use { sink ->
                var offset = 0
                while (offset < bytes.size) {
                    val n = minOf(BUFFER_BYTES, bytes.size - offset)
                    digest.update(bytes, offset, n)
                    sink.write(bytes, offset, n)
                    offset += n
                }
                sink.flush()
            }

            val actual = digest.digest().toHex()
            val matches = actual.equals(metadata.sha256Hex, ignoreCase = true)
            val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                context,
                "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
                target,
            )
            ReceivedFileStore.StoredFile(
                uri = uri,
                localPath = target.absolutePath,
                sizeBytes = target.length(),
                actualSha256Hex = actual,
                hashMatches = matches,
            )
        }.fold(
            onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
            onFailure = {
                logger.e(TAG, "store failed for ${metadata.fileName}", it)
                PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.FileWriteFailed(it.message ?: "store"), it)
            },
        )
    }

    override suspend fun delete(uri: Uri) = withContext(ioDispatcher) {
        runCatching {
            val name = uri.lastPathSegment ?: return@withContext
            val file = File(receivedDir, name)
            if (file.exists()) file.delete()
        }.onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "delete failed for $uri", it) }
    }

    override suspend fun cleanupOrphans(maxAgeMillis: Long) = withContext(ioDispatcher) {
        runCatching {
            val cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - maxAgeMillis
            receivedDir.listFiles()?.forEach { f ->
                if (f.isFile && f.lastModified() < cutoff) f.delete()
            }
        }.onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "cleanupOrphans failed", it) }
    }

    private fun sanitize(name: String): String {
        val base = name.substringAfterLast('/').substringAfterLast('\\')
        val cleaned = base.replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]"), "_")
        return if (cleaned.isBlank()) "received.bin" else cleaned.take(120)
    }

    private fun ByteArray.toHex(): String {
        val out = CharArray(size * 2)
        val hex = "0123456789abcdef"
        for (i in indices) {
            val v = this[i].toInt() and 0xFF
            out[i * 2] = hex[v ushr 4]
            out[i * 2 + 1] = hex[v and 0x0F]
        }
        return String(out)
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "ReceivedFileStore"
        private const val BUFFER_BYTES = 64 * 1024
    }
}
```

---

## 5.9 `transfer/TransferProgressEmitter.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.transfer

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferProgress
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong

/**
 * Throttled progress emitter used by [TransferSessionImpl] and by the
 * static/kinetic session orchestrators in Batch 8.
 *
 * Behaviour:
 *  - Coalesces bursts: at most one emission per [intervalMs].
 *  - Never drops a [force]-d emission.
 *  - Uses a monotonic counter so concurrent producers do not race on the
 *    timestamp.
 */
class TransferProgressEmitter(
    private val intervalMs: Long = PrismConstants.PROGRESS_EMIT_INTERVAL_MS,
) {
    private val _flow = MutableSharedFlow<TransferProgress>(
        replay = 1,
        extraBufferCapacity = 8,
        onBufferOverflow = kotlinx.coroutines.channels.BufferOverflow.DROP_OLDEST,
    )
    val flow: SharedFlow<TransferProgress> = _flow.asSharedFlow()

    private val lastEmission = AtomicLong(0L)

    suspend fun emit(progress: TransferProgress, force: Boolean = false) {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val last = lastEmission.get()
        if (!force && now - last < intervalMs) return
        if (!force && !lastEmission.compareAndSet(last, now)) return
        if (force) lastEmission.set(now)
        _flow.emit(progress)
    }
}
```

---

## 5.10 `io/TransferResultDeliverer.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.io

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferResult
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Hands a verified reconstructed file to the rest of the system.
 *
 * Two delivery modes:
 *  1. **APK**: fires `ACTION_VIEW` with `application/vnd.android.package-archive`
 *     and a content URI, so the system installer picks it up. Requires
 *     `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES` on API 26+; the manifest declares it and
 *     the UI must surface the user-facing settings prompt.
 *  2. **Everything else**: fires `ACTION_SEND` with a content URI and a
 *     chooser, which lets the user save to Drive, email it, or open it in
 *     a compatible app.
 *
 * In both cases the grant is temporary and read-only.
 */
@Singleton
class TransferResultDeliverer @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) {

    fun buildOpenIntent(result: TransferResult.Success): Intent? {
        val uri = result.outputUri
        return when {
            result.metadata.mimeType == APK_MIME -> {
                Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                    setDataAndType(uri, APK_MIME)
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                }
            }
            else -> {
                Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                    type = result.metadata.mimeType
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TITLE, result.metadata.fileName)
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun launch(result: TransferResult.Success, chooserTitle: String = "Open with") {
        val intent = buildOpenIntent(result) ?: return
        runCatching {
            if (result.metadata.mimeType == APK_MIME) {
                context.startActivity(intent)
            } else {
                val chooser = Intent.createChooser(intent, chooserTitle).apply {
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                }
                context.startActivity(chooser)
            }
        }.onFailure { logger.e(TAG, "delivery failed", it) }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "TransferResultDeliverer"
        const val APK_MIME = "application/vnd.android.package-archive"
    }
}
```

## 5.11 `di/IoModule.kt` — updated wiring

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.di

import com.prism.ot.io.FileHandler
import com.prism.ot.io.FileHandlerImpl
import com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStore
import com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStoreImpl
import com.prism.ot.io.Sha256Verifier
import com.prism.ot.io.Sha256VerifierImpl
import com.prism.ot.transfer.TransferSession
import dagger.Binds
import dagger.Module
import dagger.hilt.InstallIn
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import javax.inject.Singleton

@Module
@InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
abstract class IoModule {

    @Binds @Singleton
    abstract fun bindFileHandler(impl: FileHandlerImpl): FileHandler

    @Binds @Singleton
    abstract fun bindSha256Verifier(impl: Sha256VerifierImpl): Sha256Verifier

    @Binds @Singleton
    abstract fun bindReceivedFileStore(impl: ReceivedFileStoreImpl): ReceivedFileStore
}
```

`TransferSession` is **not** bound in the Hilt graph because it is per‑transfer state, not a singleton. The factory in `TransferSessionImpl.Companion` is the intended construction path; Batch 8’s session orchestrators will use it directly.

---

## 5.12 Batch 5 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **`FileHandler` / `FileHandlerImpl`** — SAF‑only, Okio‑buffered streaming for `content://` URIs. No path‑based APIs, no whole‑file loads, and `persistPermission` for transfers that outlive the activity.
- **`Sha256Verifier` / `Sha256VerifierImpl`** — streaming SHA‑256 with Okio `HashingSource`/`HashingSink`, a `HashingFileSink` wrapper for hash‑while‑writing, and constant‑time comparison for the final check.
- **`TransferSession` / `TransferSessionImpl`** — the decoder‑side orchestrator: serialised tile ingestion on the codec dispatcher, throttled progress on a `SharedFlow`, a terminal `result` flow, and a `finalize()` path that reconstructs, verifies SHA‑256, and only then publishes a `TransferResult.Success`.
- **`ReceivedFileStore` / `ReceivedFileStoreImpl`** — app‑private storage with `FileProvider` content‑URI delivery, hash‑matched before exposure, orphan cleanup on startup.
- **`TransferProgressEmitter`** — a reusable throttled emitter with forced‑emission semantics for the final snapshot.
- **`TransferResultDeliverer`** — APK → `ACTION_VIEW` with the package‑archive MIME; everything else → `ACTION_SEND` chooser; content URI + temporary read grant in both cases.
- **DI wiring** — `FileHandler`, `Sha256Verifier`, and `ReceivedFileStore` bound as singletons; `TransferSession` constructed via factory, not injected.

**Build status after this batch:** the I/O and transfer layer compiles against the interfaces from Batch 2 and the codec from Batch 4. The remaining unresolved symbols are in the capture layer (`CameraController`, `ScanForegroundService`), the motor layer (`MotorController`, `BleMotorService`), the session orchestrators (`ScanSession`, `StaticSession`, `KineticSession`), and the UI (`MainActivity`, ViewModels, screens, resources). Those arrive in batches 6–11.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 6 — Capture Pipeline**: `CameraController` with CameraX `ImageAnalysis` and `STRATEGY_KEEP_ONLY_LATEST`, `FrameAnalyzer` that routes frames through M1 → M2 → M4 → M5 → M6, the `ScanForegroundService` with camera foreground‑service type, and the rolling‑shutter‑aware frame selection logic.

# Batch 6 — Capture Pipeline: CameraX Controller, Frame Analyzer, Rolling‑Shutter Awareness, Foreground Service

This batch turns the camera into a **controlled optical sensor**. It provides:

- **`CameraController`** — a lifecycle‑bound CameraX façade that negotiates the highest fixed frame‑rate range the device supports, requests **RGBA_8888 hardware conversion** on API 30+, and captures the sensor’s **`SENSOR_ROLLING_SHUTTER_SKEW`** through a Camera2 capture callback.
- **`PrismFrameAnalyzer`** — the frame‑to‑tile pipeline: M1 decodability gate → M2 Spectre tile detection → per‑tile M4 calibration → M5 soft symbols → M6 NB‑LDPC decode → `DecodedTile` emission. Tile processing fans out across a bounded decoder pool while the CameraX analyzer stays on `STRATEGY_KEEP_ONLY_LATEST`.
- **`RollingShutterEstimator`** — converts the sensor’s readout skew and the kinetic mass’s angular velocity into a per‑frame usability decision, rejecting frames whose skew would smear a tile beyond the decoder’s tolerance.
- **`ScanForegroundService`** — a `LifecycleService` with `foregroundServiceType="camera"`, started only while the app is in the foreground (while‑in‑use compliance), holding a partial wake lock and exposing a stop action.

Two corrections to earlier batches are included as patches: **CameraX 1.5.1** requires `compileSdk 35`, and the Batch 2 `suspend @Provides` for `ProcessCameraProvider` is not valid Dagger — it is replaced with a `ListenableFuture` provider.

---

## 6.0 Patches to earlier batches

### 6.0.1 `gradle/libs.versions.toml` — bump CameraX, add lifecycle‑service

```toml
[versions]
# ... existing versions ...
camerax = "1.5.1"
# ...

[libraries]
# ... existing libraries ...
androidx-lifecycle-service = { module = "androidx.lifecycle:lifecycle-service", version.ref = "lifecycle" }
```

### 6.0.2 `app/build.gradle.kts` — compile/target SDK and dependency

```kotlin
android {
    compileSdk = 35
    // ...
    defaultConfig {
        targetSdk = 35
        // ...
    }
}

dependencies {
    // ...
    implementation(libs.androidx.lifecycle.service)
    // ...
}
```

CameraX 1.5.x requires compilation against API 35 or later; this is a hard requirement, not a recommendation.

### 6.0.3 `app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` — notification permission

```xml
<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS" />
```

Add it alongside the existing `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CAMERA` permission. On API 33+ the foreground‑service notification still appears in the task manager without it, but the runtime prompt must be shown before the service starts for the notification to be visible.

### 6.0.4 `di/CameraModule.kt` — replace the invalid suspend provider

Dagger does **not** support `suspend` `@Provides` methods. The Batch 2 provider is replaced with a `ListenableFuture`:

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.di

import android.content.Context
import androidx.camera.lifecycle.ProcessCameraProvider
import com.google.common.util.concurrent.ListenableFuture
import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraController
import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraControllerImpl
import dagger.Binds
import dagger.Module
import dagger.Provides
import dagger.hilt.InstallIn
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import javax.inject.Qualifier
import javax.inject.Singleton

@Qualifier @Retention(AnnotationRetention.BINARY) annotation class CameraExecutor

@Module
@InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
abstract class CameraModule {

    @Binds @Singleton
    abstract fun bindCameraController(impl: CameraControllerImpl): CameraController

    companion object {

        @Provides @Singleton
        @CameraExecutor
        fun provideCameraExecutor(): ExecutorService =
            Executors.newFixedThreadPool(3) { r ->
                Thread(r, "prism-camera").apply { priority = Thread.NORM_PRIORITY + 1 }
            }

        @Provides @Singleton
        fun provideCameraProviderFuture(
            @ApplicationContext context: Context,
        ): ListenableFuture<ProcessCameraProvider> =
            ProcessCameraProvider.getInstance(context)
    }
}
```

`CameraControllerImpl` awaits this future inside `bind()`.

---

## 6.1 `capture/CameraController.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import androidx.camera.view.PreviewView
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow

/**
 * Lifecycle-bound CameraX façade for PRISM scanning.
 *
 * Contract:
 *  - [bind] is idempotent: a second call unbinds the previous session first.
 *  - The controller always negotiates a fixed frame-rate range when one is
 *    available. Variable ranges (e.g. 15–30) are only chosen when no fixed
 *    range exists, because PRISM's face-rate matching in kinetic mode
 *    depends on a stable frame period.
 *  - The analyzer executor is a dedicated background pool. The controller
 *    never touches the main thread for frame processing.
 */
interface CameraController {

    suspend fun bind(
        lifecycleOwner: LifecycleOwner,
        previewView: PreviewView,
        analyzer: FrameAnalyzer,
        mode: TransferMode,
    ): PrismResult<CameraSessionInfo>

    fun unbind()

    fun setTorch(enabled: Boolean)

    fun setZoom(ratio: Float)

    suspend fun focusAt(normalizedX: Float, normalizedY: Float): PrismResult<Unit>

    val sessionInfo: StateFlow<CameraSessionInfo?>

    /** Measured capture rate, updated once per frame. */
    val actualFps: StateFlow<Float>
}

/**
 * Immutable snapshot of what the camera actually negotiated.
 *
 * [rollingShutterSkewNanos] is the value reported by
 * `CaptureResult.SENSOR_ROLLING_SHUTTER_SKEW`: the duration between the
 * start of exposure of the first sensor row and the start of exposure one
 * row past the last. It is the basis for every skew decision in
 * [RollingShutterEstimator].
 */
data class CameraSessionInfo(
    val selectedFpsRange: IntRange,
    val supportedFpsRanges: List<IntRange>,
    val sensorOrientationDegrees: Int,
    val rollingShutterSkewNanos: Long,
    val isRgbaOutput: Boolean,
) {
    val nominalFps: Int get() = selectedFpsRange.upper
    val isFixedFrameRate: Boolean get() = selectedFpsRange.first == selectedFpsRange.last
}
```

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

 * Raw capability snapshot extracted from Camera2 characteristics.
 * Kept separate from [CameraSessionInfo] because it describes what the
 * device *can* do, not what was actually selected.
 */
data class CameraCapabilities(

    val sensorOrientationDegrees: Int,
)
```
