## 6.3 `capture/CameraFpsMonitor.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

/**
 * Sliding-window frame-rate estimator.
 *
 * Uses sensor timestamps (`ImageInfo.getTimestamp()`) rather than wall-clock
 * time, because the sensor timestamp reflects the start of exposure and is
 * not affected by scheduling jitter in the analyzer executor.
 *
 * Not thread-safe. It is owned by the analyzer thread.
 */
class CameraFpsMonitor(private val window: Int = 32) {

    private val timestamps = LongArray(window)
    private var writeIndex = 0
    private var count = 0

    fun onFrame(sensorTimestampNanos: Long): Float {
        timestamps[writeIndex] = sensorTimestampNanos
        writeIndex = (writeIndex + 1) % window
        if (count < window) count++
        return currentFps()
    }

    fun currentFps(): Float {
        if (count < 2) return 0f
        val oldestIndex = if (count < window) 0 else writeIndex
        val newestIndex = if (writeIndex == 0) window - 1 else writeIndex - 1
        val first = timestamps[oldestIndex]
        val last = timestamps[newestIndex]
        if (last <= first) return 0f
        val seconds = (last - first) / 1_000_000_000.0
        return ((count - 1) / seconds).toFloat()
    }
}
```

---

## 6.4 `capture/RollingShutterEstimator.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Converts sensor readout skew into a per-frame usability decision.
 *
 * Rolling-shutter skew is the time between the first row's exposure start
 * and the last row's exposure start. When the scene moves
 * during that interval, rows are captured at different scene times. For a
 * rotating kinetic mass, this produces a shear that the tile decoder cannot
 * undo unless the skew is small relative to the tile's angular size.
 *
 * The estimator does not attempt to correct skew. It decides whether the
 * frame is worth sending to the expensive decode path. Correction, when
 * needed, is delegated to M9 (motion deblurrer), which receives the skew
 * estimate through the channel state.
 */
@Singleton
class RollingShutterEstimator @Inject constructor() {

    /**
     * @param skewNanos sensor readout skew from
     *        `CaptureResult.SENSOR_ROLLING_SHUTTER_SKEW`
     * @param angularVelocityRadPerSec estimated mass angular velocity from M8
     * @param tileAngularSizeRad angular width of one face as seen from the
     *        camera; used to bound the acceptable shear
     * @return true if the frame's shear is below [MAX_SHEAR_FRACTION] of a
     *         tile width
     */
    fun isFrameUsable(
        skewNanos: Long,
        angularVelocityRadPerSec: Float,
        tileAngularSizeRad: Float,
    ): Boolean {
        if (skewNanos <= 0L) return true
        if (angularVelocityRadPerSec <= 0f) return true
        if (tileAngularSizeRad <= 0f) return true
        val skewSeconds = skewNanos / 1_000_000_000f
        val shearRad = angularVelocityRadPerSec * skewSeconds
        return shearRad < tileAngularSizeRad * MAX_SHEAR_FRACTION
    }

    /**
     * Expected exposure-start offset for a row at [normalizedY] in [0,1].
     * Used by M9 to condition its deblurring kernel.
     */
    fun skewAtRowNanos(skewNanos: Long, normalizedY: Float): Long =
        (skewNanos * normalizedY.coerceIn(0f, 1f)).toLong()

    companion object {
        /**
         * A shear of 25% of a tile width is the point at which the
         * classifier's soft probabilities begin to collapse. This is
         * deliberately conservative; M9 can recover moderate shear, but
         * the decodability oracle should not spend compute on frames that
         * are already lost.
         */
        const val MAX_SHEAR_FRACTION = 0.25f
    }
}
```

---

## 6.5 `capture/FrameConverter.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Matrix
import androidx.camera.core.ImageProxy
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * ImageProxy → Bitmap conversion.
 *
 * On API 30+ the CameraX pipeline is configured for
 * `OUTPUT_IMAGE_FORMAT_RGBA_8888`, which performs YUV→RGBA conversion in
 * native code on the hardware path and avoids the 25–40 ms Kotlin-side
 * conversion cost of the YUV fallback. On API 29 the
 * analyzer receives YUV_420_888 and [ImageProxy.toBitmap] performs the
 * conversion; this is slower but correct.
 *
 * The converter does not scale the full frame. Only the decodability
 * oracle needs a downscaled input; tile cropping happens in
 * [TileRectifier] so the full-resolution pixels remain available for the
 * symbol classifier.
 */
@Singleton
class FrameConverter @Inject constructor() {

    fun toBitmap(proxy: ImageProxy, rotationDegrees: Int): Bitmap {
        val raw = proxy.toBitmap()
        if (rotationDegrees == 0) return raw
        val matrix = Matrix().apply { postRotate(rotationDegrees.toFloat()) }
        val rotated = Bitmap.createBitmap(raw, 0, 0, raw.width, raw.height, matrix, true)
        if (rotated !== raw) raw.recycle()
        return rotated
    }

    /**
     * Downscales to the oracle's 224×224 input. Returns [source] unchanged
     * when it is already small enough, so callers must check identity
     * before recycling.
     */
    fun scaleForOracle(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
        if (source.width <= ORACLE_SIZE && source.height <= ORACLE_SIZE) return source
        return Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(source, ORACLE_SIZE, ORACLE_SIZE, true)
    }

    companion object {
        const val ORACLE_SIZE = 224
    }
}
```

---

## 6.6 `capture/TileRectifier.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Matrix
import android.graphics.Paint
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.AperiodicTileDetection

/**
 * Rectifies a detected Spectre tile into a square bitmap suitable for M5.
 *
 * Two paths:
 *  1. If the detector returned exactly four vertices, a projective
 *     four-point transform is applied. This is the preferred path for
 *     square tiles and for Spectre tiles whose convex hull is
 *     quadrilateral.
 *  2. Otherwise, the axis-aligned bounding box is cropped with 10%
 *     padding and scaled. M5 is trained on rotated and partially cropped
 *     tiles, so this fallback remains decodable even though it wastes some
 *     pixels.
 *
 * The returned bitmap is always newly allocated. The caller owns it and
 * must recycle it.
 */
object TileRectifier {

    fun rectify(
        frame: Bitmap,
        detection: AperiodicTileDetection,
        outputSize: Int = 64,
    ): Bitmap {
        val xs = detection.vertexXs
        val ys = detection.vertexYs

        if (xs.size == 4 && ys.size == 4) {
            val src = floatArrayOf(
                xs[0] * frame.width, ys[0] * frame.height,
                xs[1] * frame.width, ys[1] * frame.height,
                xs[2] * frame.width, ys[2] * frame.height,
                xs[3] * frame.width, ys[3] * frame.height,
            )
            val dst = floatArrayOf(
                0f, 0f,
                outputSize.toFloat(), 0f,
                outputSize.toFloat(), outputSize.toFloat(),
                0f, outputSize.toFloat(),
            )
            val matrix = Matrix()
            if (matrix.setPolyToPoly(src, 0, dst, 0, 4)) {
                val out = Bitmap.createBitmap(outputSize, outputSize, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
                Canvas(out).drawBitmap(frame, matrix, Paint(Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG))
                return out
            }
        }

        return boundingBoxCrop(frame, detection, outputSize)
    }

    private fun boundingBoxCrop(
        frame: Bitmap,
        detection: AperiodicTileDetection,
        outputSize: Int,
    ): Bitmap {
        val xs = detection.vertexXs.map { it * frame.width }
        val ys = detection.vertexYs.map { it * frame.height }
        val minX = (xs.minOrNull() ?: 0f).toInt().coerceIn(0, frame.width - 1)
        val maxX = (xs.maxOrNull() ?: frame.width.toFloat()).toInt().coerceIn(0, frame.width)
        val minY = (ys.minOrNull() ?: 0f).toInt().coerceIn(0, frame.height - 1)
        val maxY = (ys.maxOrNull() ?: frame.height.toFloat()).toInt().coerceIn(0, frame.height)

        val padX = ((maxX - minX) * PADDING_FRACTION).toInt()
        val padY = ((maxY - minY) * PADDING_FRACTION).toInt()
        val left = (minX - padX).coerceIn(0, frame.width - 1)
        val top = (minY - padY).coerceIn(0, frame.height - 1)
        val right = (maxX + padX).coerceIn(left + 1, frame.width)
        val bottom = (maxY + padY).coerceIn(top + 1, frame.height)

        val cropped = Bitmap.createBitmap(frame, left, top, right - left, bottom - top)
        val scaled = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(cropped, outputSize, outputSize, true)
        if (cropped !== scaled) cropped.recycle()
        return scaled
    }

    private const val PADDING_FRACTION = 0.10f
}
```

---

## 6.7 `capture/PilotExtractor.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.codec.Alphabet
import com.prism.ot.ml.ChromaticPilot

/**
 * Extracts chromatic calibration pilots from a rectified tile.
 *
 * Contract with the encoder: every PRISM tile carries four pilot patches
 * at the normalized positions in [POSITIONS], painted with the first four
 * entries of [Alphabet.DENSE_64_COLOURS] in the same order. The encoder
 * renders these patches as part of the tile header; the decoder reads them
 * to learn the per-frame CIELAB transform in M4.
 *
 * This is intentionally a fixed contract rather than a discovered layout.
 * A discovered layout would require a second detection pass and would make
 * the pilot positions ambiguous under partial occlusion.
 */
object PilotExtractor {

    private val POSITIONS = listOf(
        0.12f to 0.12f,   // top-left   → colour 0
        0.88f to 0.12f,   // top-right  → colour 1
        0.12f to 0.88f,   // bottom-left→ colour 2
        0.88f to 0.88f,   // bottom-right→colour 3
    )

    fun extract(rectified: Bitmap): List<ChromaticPilot> =
        POSITIONS.mapIndexed { index, (nx, ny) ->
            val x = (nx * rectified.width).toInt().coerceIn(0, rectified.width - 1)
            val y = (ny * rectified.height).toInt().coerceIn(0, rectified.height - 1)
            val pixel = rectified.getPixel(x, y)
            val intended = Alphabet.DENSE_64_COLOURS[index % Alphabet.DENSE_64_COLOURS.size]
            ChromaticPilot(
                intendedL = intended[0],
                intendedA = intended[1],
                intendedB = intended[2],
                receivedR = (pixel shr 16) and 0xFF,
                receivedG = (pixel shr 8) and 0xFF,
                receivedB = pixel and 0xFF,
            )
        }
}
```

---

## 6.8 `capture/FrameAnalyzer.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.ChannelFeatureVector
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FrameData
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.DecodabilityScore

/**
 * Turns one camera frame into zero or more decoded fountain packets.
 *
 * Implementations must be safe to call from a single analyzer thread.
 * Parallelism across tiles is the implementation's responsibility; the
 * caller does not fan out frames.
 */
interface FrameAnalyzer {
    suspend fun analyze(frame: FrameData): PrismResult<FrameAnalysisResult>
}

data class FrameAnalysisResult(
    val decodedTiles: List<DecodedTile>,
    val channelFeatures: ChannelFeatureVector,
    val decodability: DecodabilityScore,
    val detections: Int,
)

/**
 * Callback invoked for each successfully decoded tile. The session layer
 * (Batch 8) wires this to the codec's fountain decoder.
 */
fun interface TileSink {
    suspend fun onTileDecoded(tile: DecodedTile)
}
```

---

## 6.9 `capture/PrismFrameAnalyzer.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.ChannelFeatureVector
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FrameData
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import kotlinx.coroutines.async
import kotlinx.coroutines.awaitAll
import kotlinx.coroutines.coroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Semaphore
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withPermit
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Production frame analyzer.
 *
 * Pipeline per frame:
 *   1. ImageProxy → Bitmap (hardware RGBA path on API 30+).
 *   2. M1 on a 224×224 downscale; reject the frame early if it is not
 *      worth decoding. This is the single largest power saving in the
 *      pipeline.
 *   3. M2 detects Spectre tiles. An empty result is a normal outcome, not
 *      an error.
 *   4. For each tile, bounded by [MAX_CONCURRENT_DECODERS]:
 *        rectify → extract pilots → M4 → M5 → M6 → DecodedTile.
 *   5. Emit each DecodedTile to the [TileSink].
 *
 * The analyzer deliberately does not own a codec session. It produces
 * fountain packets; the session owns the fountain decoder and the
 * SHA-256 verification.
 */
@Singleton
class PrismFrameAnalyzer @Inject constructor(
    private val mlBridge: MlBridge,
    private val frameConverter: FrameConverter,
    private val rollingShutter: RollingShutterEstimator,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : FrameAnalyzer {

    private val decoderSemaphore = Semaphore(PrismConstants.MAX_CONCURRENT_DECODERS)

    @Volatile
    private var tileSink: TileSink? = null

    fun setTileSink(sink: TileSink?) {
        tileSink = sink
    }

    override suspend fun analyze(frame: FrameData): PrismResult<FrameAnalysisResult> =
        runCatching {
            val bitmap = frameConverter.toBitmap(frame.imageProxy, frame.rotationDegrees)
            try {
                val oracleInput = frameConverter.scaleForOracle(bitmap)
                val decodability = try {
                    mlBridge.scoreDecodability(oracleInput).getOrThrow()
                } finally {
                    if (oracleInput !== bitmap) oracleInput.recycle()
                }

                if (!decodability.isWorthDecoding) {
                    return@runCatching FrameAnalysisResult(
                        decodedTiles = emptyList(),
                        channelFeatures = buildChannelFeatures(frame, decodability, erasureRate = 1f),
                        decodability = decodability,
                        detections = 0,
                    )
                }

                val detections = mlBridge.detectAperiodicTiles(bitmap).getOrThrow()
                if (detections.isEmpty()) {
                    return@runCatching FrameAnalysisResult(
                        decodedTiles = emptyList(),
                        channelFeatures = buildChannelFeatures(frame, decodability, erasureRate = 1f),
                        decodability = decodability,
                        detections = 0,
                    )
                }

                val decoded = coroutineScope {
                    detections.map { detection ->
                        async {
                            decoderSemaphore.withPermit {
                                processTile(bitmap, detection, frame)
                            }
                        }
                    }.awaitAll().filterNotNull()
                }

                decoded.forEach { tile -> tileSink?.onTileDecoded(tile) }

                val erasure = if (detections.isEmpty()) 1f
                else 1f - (decoded.size.toFloat() / detections.size.toFloat())

                FrameAnalysisResult(
                    decodedTiles = decoded,
                    channelFeatures = buildChannelFeatures(frame, decodability, erasure),
                    decodability = decodability,
                    detections = detections.size,
                )
            } finally {
                bitmap.recycle()
            }
        }.fold(
            onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
            onFailure = { t ->
                logger.e(TAG, "Frame analysis failed: ${t.message}", t)
                PrismResult.Failure(
                    PrismError.Unexpected(t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName),
                    t,
                )
            },
        )

    /**
     * Processes a single detection. Returns null when any stage fails;
     * failures are counted as erasures by the caller.
     */
    private suspend fun processTile(
        frame: android.graphics.Bitmap,
        detection: com.prism.ot.ml.model.AperiodicTileDetection,
        frameData: FrameData,
    ): DecodedTile? {
        val rectified = TileRectifier.rectify(frame, detection)
        return try {
            val pilots = PilotExtractor.extract(rectified)
            val calibration = mlBridge.calibrateChromatic(pilots).getOrNull() ?: return null
            val softSymbols = mlBridge.classifySymbols(rectified, calibration).getOrNull() ?: return null
            val packet = mlBridge.neuralLdpcDecode(softSymbols, PAYLOAD_PCM_ID).getOrNull() ?: return null

            DecodedTile(
                tileId = detection.centroidX.hashCode() * 31 +
                    detection.centroidY.hashCode() * 17 +
                    detection.orientationRadians.hashCode(),
                faceId = null,
                packetBytes = packet,
                symbolConfidence = softSymbols.topSymbolConfidence,
                wasErrorCorrected = softSymbols.entropy < ERROR_CORRECTION_ENTROPY_THRESHOLD,
                capturedAtEpochMs = frameData.timestampNanos / 1_000_000L,
            )
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            logger.w(TAG, "Tile decode failed: ${t.message}", t)
            null
        } finally {
            rectified.recycle()
        }
    }

    private fun buildChannelFeatures(
        frame: FrameData,
        decodability: com.prism.ot.ml.model.DecodabilityScore,
        erasureRate: Float,
    ): ChannelFeatureVector = ChannelFeatureVector(
        blurSigmaPx = decodability.blurEstimateSigmaPx,
        colorResidualDeltaE00 = 0f, // populated by the calibrator in Batch 7's session glue
        snrProxyDb = decodability.score * 40f,
        recentErasureRate = erasureRate.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
        recentDecodeRate = (1f - erasureRate).coerceIn(0f, 1f),
        captureFps = frame.estimatedFps,
        perspectiveAngleDeg = 0f,
        frameTimestampNanos = frame.timestampNanos,
    )

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "PrismFrameAnalyzer"

        /** Parity-check matrix index reserved for payload tiles. */
        const val PAYLOAD_PCM_ID = 1

        /**
         * Below this entropy the soft-symbol distribution is sharp enough
         * that the inner decoder almost certainly corrected something.
         * This is a heuristic for telemetry, not a correctness signal.
         */
        const val ERROR_CORRECTION_ENTROPY_THRESHOLD = 0.35f
    }
}
```

---

## 6.10 `capture/CameraControllerImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCaptureSession
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult
import android.os.Build
import androidx.camera.camera2.interop.Camera2CameraInfo
import androidx.camera.camera2.interop.Camera2Interop
import androidx.camera.camera2.interop.ExperimentalCamera2Interop
import androidx.camera.core.Camera
import androidx.camera.core.CameraInfo
import androidx.camera.core.CameraSelector
import androidx.camera.core.FocusMeteringAction
import androidx.camera.core.ImageAnalysis
import androidx.camera.core.Preview
import androidx.camera.core.resolutionselector.ResolutionSelector
import androidx.camera.core.resolutionselector.ResolutionStrategy
import androidx.camera.lifecycle.ProcessCameraProvider
import androidx.camera.view.PreviewView
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import com.google.common.util.concurrent.ListenableFuture
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FrameData
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.di.CameraExecutor
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton
import kotlin.coroutines.resume
import kotlin.coroutines.resumeWithException

/**
 * CameraX implementation of [CameraController].
 *
 * Frame-rate strategy: CameraX's Feature Group API does not yet support
 * ImageAnalysis with `FPS_60`; constructing such a session throws.
 * The only reliable way to request a fixed frame rate for ImageAnalysis is
 * through Camera2 interop's `CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE`. The controller
 * therefore queries the device's supported ranges and selects the highest
 * fixed range, falling back to the highest variable range only when no
 * fixed range exists.
 *
 * Rolling-shutter telemetry: a Camera2 capture callback records
 * `SENSOR_ROLLING_SHUTTER_SKEW` from every completed capture. The value is
 * exposed in [CameraSessionInfo] and consumed by [RollingShutterEstimator].
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalCamera2Interop::class)
@Singleton
class CameraControllerImpl @Inject constructor(
    private val cameraProviderFuture: ListenableFuture<ProcessCameraProvider>,
    @CameraExecutor private val cameraExecutor: ExecutorService,
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : CameraController {

    private var cameraProvider: ProcessCameraProvider? = null
    private var camera: Camera? = null
    private var imageAnalysis: ImageAnalysis? = null
    private var preview: Preview? = null
    private var previewView: PreviewView? = null

    private val _sessionInfo = MutableStateFlow<CameraSessionInfo?>(null)
    override val sessionInfo: StateFlow<CameraSessionInfo?> = _sessionInfo.asStateFlow()

    private val _actualFps = MutableStateFlow(0f)
    override val actualFps: StateFlow<Float> = _actualFps.asStateFlow()

    private val rollingShutterSkewNanos = AtomicLong(0L)

    private val bindingDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher = Dispatchers.Main.immediate

    override suspend fun bind(
        lifecycleOwner: LifecycleOwner,
        previewView: PreviewView,
        analyzer: FrameAnalyzer,
        mode: TransferMode,
    ): PrismResult<CameraSessionInfo> = runCatching {
        val provider = awaitProvider()
        provider.unbindAll()

        this.previewView = previewView
        val selector = CameraSelector.DEFAULT_BACK_CAMERA
        val cameraInfo = provider.getCameraInfo(selector)
        val capabilities = queryCapabilities(cameraInfo)
        val fpsRange = chooseFpsRange(capabilities.supportedFpsRanges)

        val previewUseCase = Preview.Builder()
            .build()
            .also { it.surfaceProvider = previewView.surfaceProvider }

        val analysisBuilder = ImageAnalysis.Builder()
            .setResolutionSelector(ANALYSIS_RESOLUTION_SELECTOR)
            .setBackpressureStrategy(ImageAnalysis.STRATEGY_KEEP_ONLY_LATEST)
            .setOutputImageRotationEnabled(false)

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            analysisBuilder.setOutputImageFormat(ImageAnalysis.OUTPUT_IMAGE_FORMAT_RGBA_8888)
        }

        applyCamera2Interop(analysisBuilder, fpsRange)

        val analysis = analysisBuilder.build()
        val fpsMonitor = CameraFpsMonitor()

        analysis.setAnalyzer(cameraExecutor) { proxy ->
            val frame = FrameData(
                imageProxy = proxy,
                timestampNanos = proxy.imageInfo.timestamp,
                rotationDegrees = proxy.imageInfo.rotationDegrees,
                width = proxy.width,
                height = proxy.height,
                estimatedFps = fpsMonitor.onFrame(proxy.imageInfo.timestamp),
            )
            try {
                // The analyzer executor is a dedicated background thread.
                // runBlocking here is intentional: it keeps the ImageProxy
                // alive until analysis completes and preserves the
                // latest-only backpressure contract.
                runBlocking { analyzer.analyze(frame) }
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                logger.e(TAG, "Analyzer threw: ${t.message}", t)
            } finally {
                _actualFps.value = fpsMonitor.currentFps()
                proxy.close()
            }
        }

        val boundCamera = provider.bindToLifecycle(
            lifecycleOwner,
            selector,
            previewUseCase,
            analysis,
        )

        this.cameraProvider = provider
        this.camera = boundCamera
        this.preview = previewUseCase
        this.imageAnalysis = analysis

        val info = CameraSessionInfo(
            selectedFpsRange = fpsRange,
            supportedFpsRanges = capabilities.supportedFpsRanges,
            sensorOrientationDegrees = capabilities.sensorOrientationDegrees,
            rollingShutterSkewNanos = rollingShutterSkewNanos.get(),
            isRgbaOutput = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R,
        )
        _sessionInfo.value = info
        logger.i(
            TAG,
            "Camera bound: fps=${fpsRange.first}..${fpsRange.upper}, " +
                "skew=${info.rollingShutterSkewNanos}ns, rgba=${info.isRgbaOutput}",
        )
        info
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { t ->
            logger.e(TAG, "Camera bind failed", t)
            PrismResult.Failure(
                PrismError.CameraInitializationFailed(t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName),
                t,
            )
        },
    )

    override fun unbind() {
        runCatching { imageAnalysis?.clearAnalyzer() }
        runCatching { cameraProvider?.unbindAll() }
        imageAnalysis = null
        preview = null
        camera = null
        previewView = null
        _sessionInfo.value = null
        _actualFps.value = 0f
    }

    override fun setTorch(enabled: Boolean) {
        camera?.cameraControl?.enableTorch(enabled)
    }

    override fun setZoom(ratio: Float) {
        val max = camera?.cameraInfo?.zoomState?.value?.maxZoomRatio ?: 1f
        camera?.cameraControl?.setZoomRatio(ratio.coerceIn(1f, max))
    }

    override suspend fun focusAt(normalizedX: Float, normalizedY: Float): PrismResult<Unit> =
        runCatching {
            val view = previewView ?: error("PreviewView not bound")
            val point = view.meteringPointFactory.createPoint(
                normalizedX.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * view.width,
                normalizedY.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * view.height,
            )
            val action = FocusMeteringAction.Builder(
                point,
                FocusMeteringAction.FLAG_AF or FocusMeteringAction.FLAG_AE,
            ).build()
            camera?.cameraControl?.startFocusAndMetering(action)
            Unit
        }.fold(
            onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
            onFailure = { t ->
                logger.w(TAG, "focusAt failed", t)
                PrismResult.Failure(PrismError.CameraInitializationFailed(t.message ?: "focus"), t)
            },
        )

    // ---- Internals ----

    private suspend fun awaitProvider(): ProcessCameraProvider =
        if (cameraProviderFuture.isDone) {
            cameraProviderFuture.get()
        } else {
            suspendCancellableCoroutine { cont ->
                cameraProviderFuture.addListener(
                    {
                        runCatching { cameraProviderFuture.get() }
                            .onSuccess { cont.resume(it) }
                            .onFailure { cont.resumeWithException(it) }
                    },
                    cameraExecutor,
                )
                cont.invokeOnCancellation { /* future cannot be cancelled safely */ }
            }
        }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun applyCamera2Interop(
        builder: ImageAnalysis.Builder,
        fpsRange: IntRange,
    ) {
        val extender = Camera2Interop.Extender(builder)
        extender.setCaptureRequestOption(
            CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE,
            android.util.Range(fpsRange.first, fpsRange.upper),
        )
        extender.setSessionCaptureCallback(object : CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback() {
            override fun onCaptureCompleted(
                session: CameraCaptureSession,
                request: CaptureRequest,
                result: CaptureResult,
            ) {
                result.get(CaptureResult.SENSOR_ROLLING_SHUTTER_SKEW)?.let {
                    rollingShutterSkewNanos.set(it)
                }
            }
        })
    }

    private fun queryCapabilities(cameraInfo: CameraInfo): CameraCapabilities {
        val camera2Info = Camera2CameraInfo.from(cameraInfo)
        val ranges = camera2Info
            .getCameraCharacteristic(CameraCharacteristics.CONTROL_AE_AVAILABLE_TARGET_FPS_RANGES)
            ?.map { it.lower..it.upper }
            ?: listOf(30..30)
        val orientation = camera2Info
            .getCameraCharacteristic(CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_ORIENTATION)
            ?: 0
        return CameraCapabilities(ranges, orientation)
    }

    /**
     * Prefer a fixed range at 60, then 30, then 24, then 15, then 10 fps.
     * If none exists, choose the range with the highest upper bound.
     */
    private fun chooseFpsRange(supported: List<IntRange>): IntRange {
        val preferred = listOf(60, 30, 24, 15, 10)
        for (target in preferred) {
            supported.firstOrNull { it.first == target && it.upper == target }
                ?.let { return it }
        }
        for (target in preferred) {
            supported.firstOrNull { target in it }?.let { return it }
        }
        return supported.maxByOrNull { it.upper } ?: 30..30
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "CameraController"

        private val ANALYSIS_RESOLUTION_SELECTOR: ResolutionSelector =
            ResolutionSelector.Builder()
                .setResolutionStrategy(
                    ResolutionStrategy(
                        android.util.Size(1280, 720),
                        ResolutionStrategy.FALLBACK_RULE_CLOSEST_HIGHER_THEN_LOWER,
                    ),
                )
                .build()
    }
}
```

## 6.11 `capture/ScanForegroundService.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.os.Build
import android.os.PowerManager
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.ServiceCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleService
import com.prism.ot.R
import com.prism.ot.ui.MainActivity
import dagger.hilt.android.AndroidEntryPoint
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import javax.inject.Inject

/**
 * Keeps the scanning process alive during long transfers and satisfies the
 * Android 14+ foreground-service-type requirement.
 *
 * The service does **not** own the camera. CameraX remains lifecycle-bound
 * to the scanning Activity so that the preview surface and the analysis
 * use case share a single camera session. The service's role is:
 *  - declare `foregroundServiceType="camera"` and hold the corresponding
 *    `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CAMERA` permission,
 *  - show an ongoing notification with a stop action,
 *  - hold a partial wake lock so the CPU does not sleep mid-transfer.
 *
 * While-in-use compliance: the `CAMERA` runtime permission is subject to
 * while-in-use restrictions, so this service must only be started while
 * the app is in the foreground. It cannot be started from a
 * `BOOT_COMPLETED` receiver or from the background. The
 * [ScanServiceController] documents and enforces this by being called
 * from `onResume`.
 */
@AndroidEntryPoint
class ScanForegroundService : LifecycleService() {

    @Inject
    lateinit var powerManager: PowerManager

    private var wakeLock: PowerManager.WakeLock? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        createNotificationChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        super.onStartCommand(intent, flags, startId)

        if (intent?.action == ACTION_STOP) {
            stopScan()
            return Service.START_NOT_STICKY
        }

        val type = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_CAMERA
        } else {
            0
        }
        ServiceCompat.startForeground(
            this,
            NOTIFICATION_ID,
            buildNotification(),
            type,
        )
        acquireWakeLock()
        _isRunning.value = true
        return Service.START_STICKY
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        releaseWakeLock()
        _isRunning.value = false
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    private fun stopScan() {
        releaseWakeLock()
        _isRunning.value = false
        ServiceCompat.stopForeground(this, ServiceCompat.STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
        stopSelf()
    }

    private fun acquireWakeLock() {
        if (wakeLock?.isHeld == true) return
        wakeLock = powerManager.newWakeLock(
            PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK,
            "Prism::ScanSession",
        ).apply {
            setReferenceCounted(false)
            acquire(MAX_WAKE_LOCK_MILLIS)
        }
    }

    private fun releaseWakeLock() {
        runCatching { if (wakeLock?.isHeld == true) wakeLock?.release() }
        wakeLock = null
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val contentIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
            },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        val stopIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this,
            1,
            Intent(this, ScanForegroundService::class.java).setAction(ACTION_STOP),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle(getString(R.string.scan_notification_title))
            .setContentText(getString(R.string.scan_notification_text))
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat_prism)
            .setContentIntent(contentIntent)
            .addAction(0, getString(R.string.scan_notification_stop), stopIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_SERVICE)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .setForegroundServiceBehavior(NotificationCompat.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_IMMEDIATE)
            .build()
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.O) return
        val channel = NotificationChannel(
            CHANNEL_ID,
            getString(R.string.scan_notification_channel),
            NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW,
        ).apply {
            description = getString(R.string.scan_notification_channel_desc)
            setShowBadge(false)
        }
        getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(channel)
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_STOP = "com.prism.ot.action.STOP_SCAN"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "prism_scan"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001
        private const val MAX_WAKE_LOCK_MILLIS = 2L * 60L * 60L * 1000L

        private val _isRunning = MutableStateFlow(false)
        val isRunning: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isRunning.asStateFlow()
    }
}
```

The service references three string resources and one drawable; they are added in Batch 11. For now, add the strings to `res/values/strings.xml` so the module compiles:

```xml

<string name="scan_notification_text">Camera is active. Tap to return.</string>
<string name="scan_notification_stop">Stop</string>
<string name="scan_notification_channel">Scanning session</string>
<string name="scan_notification_channel_desc">Keeps PRISM active during a transfer</string>
```

And a minimal vector drawable `res/drawable/ic_stat_prism.xml`:

```xml
<vector xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    android:width="24dp"
    android:height="24dp"
    android:viewportWidth="24"
    android:viewportHeight="24"
    android:tint="#FFFFFF">
    <path
        android:fillColor="#FFFFFF"
        android:pathData="M12,2L2,22h20L12,2zM12,7l6,12H6L12,7z" />
</vector>
```

## 6.12 `capture/ScanServiceController.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.capture

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Starts and stops [ScanForegroundService] on behalf of the UI.
 *
 * While-in-use compliance is enforced by convention: this controller is
 * called from `onResume` of the scanning screen. Starting a camera
 * foreground service from the background is prohibited on Android 14+
 * and will throw `ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException`.
 */
@Singleton
class ScanServiceController @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
) {

    val isRunning: StateFlow<Boolean> = ScanForegroundService.isRunning

    fun start() {
        val intent = Intent(context, ScanForegroundService::class.java)
        ContextCompat.startForegroundService(context, intent)
    }

    fun stop() {
        context.startService(
            Intent(context, ScanForegroundService::class.java)
                .setAction(ScanForegroundService.ACTION_STOP),
        )
    }
}
```

## 6.14 Batch 6 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **`CameraController` / `CameraControllerImpl`** — a CameraX façade that selects the highest fixed FPS range the device supports, requests RGBA_8888 hardware conversion on API 30+, and captures `SENSOR_ROLLING_SHUTTER_SKEW` through a Camera2 capture callback. It uses `ResolutionSelector` + `ResolutionStrategy` (the non‑deprecated resolution API) and `STRATEGY_KEEP_ONLY_LATEST` with a dedicated three‑thread analyzer executor.
- **`PrismFrameAnalyzer`** — the full M1 → M2 → M4 → M5 → M6 pipeline with a bounded decoder semaphore, early decodability rejection, per‑tile rectification, pilot‑based colour calibration, soft‑symbol decoding, and `DecodedTile` emission to the session layer.
- **`RollingShutterEstimator`** — converts sensor readout skew and kinetic angular velocity into a frame‑usability decision, with a conservative 25%‑of‑tile shear budget.
- **`TileRectifier` / `PilotExtractor`** — a four‑point projective path with a bounding‑box fallback, and a fixed pilot layout contract shared with the encoder.
- **`FrameConverter` / `CameraFpsMonitor`** — hardware‑path bitmap conversion and sensor‑timestamp‑based FPS measurement.
- **`ScanForegroundService` / `ScanServiceController`** — a camera‑typed foreground service that starts only from the foreground, holds a partial wake lock, and exposes a stop action.
- **Patches** — CameraX 1.5.1, `compileSdk`/`targetSdk` 35, `POST_NOTIFICATIONS`, `lifecycle-service`, and the corrected `ListenableFuture<ProcessCameraProvider>` provider.

**Build status after this batch:** the capture layer compiles against the ML bridge from Batch 3 and the constants/models from Batch 2. The remaining unresolved symbols are the session orchestrators (`ScanSession`, `StaticSession`, `KineticSession`), the motor/BLE layer, and the UI. Batches 7–11 fill those in.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 7 — Motor & BLE Layer**: the `BleMotorService`, `MotorController` with face‑rate matching, the M10 policy loop, GATT characteristic handling for speed and telemetry, reconnect logic, and the kinetic session’s rotation‑speed negotiation against the measured camera FPS.

# Batch 7 — Motor & BLE Layer: GATT Client, Connection State Machine, Face‑Rate Matching, M10 Policy Loop

This batch makes the rotating mass a **remotely controllable peripheral** and closes the kinematic loop between the receiver's measured camera FPS and the motor's angular velocity.

It provides:

- **`BleMotorService`** — a `connectedDevice`‑typed foreground service that owns the GATT connection. Android 14+ requires `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CONNECTED_DEVICE` and the `connectedDevice` type for BLE interactions.
- **`MotorController` / `MotorControllerImpl`** — a serialized GATT operation queue built on `suspendCancellableCoroutine`, because the Android platform permits only one outstanding GATT operation per `BluetoothGatt` instance at a time; issuing a second operation before the first callback fires silently fails or corrupts state.
- **`MotorConnectionState`** — a `StateFlow`‑backed state machine with exponential‑backoff reconnection. The pattern of exposing `StateFlow<ConnectionState>` and reconnecting from the `Disconnected` state with backoff is the production‑proven approach.
- **`FaceRateMatcher`** — enforces the kinematic rule \(R_{\text{face}} = F/T \le R_{\text{camera,min}}\) with a safety margin.
- **`MotorPolicyLoop`** — a 5–20 Hz control loop that calls M10 (ZOI native policy), clamps the suggestion against `FaceRateMatcher`, and writes the target speed to the GATT speed characteristic.
- **Throughput tuning** — MTU negotiation to 517 bytes, `CONNECTION_PRIORITY_HIGH`, and write‑without‑response on the speed characteristic, following the documented BLE throughput best practices.

---

## 7.1 `motor/MotorConnectionState.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

/**
 * Explicit connection lifecycle. The state machine is deliberately small
 * and monotonic in the forward direction; every failure collapses back to
 * [Disconnected] with a reason so the UI can surface an actionable message.
 *
 * Idle → Scanning → Connecting → DiscoveringServices → Ready
 *                                                         ↓
 *                                                    Reconnecting
 *                                                         ↓
 *                                                    Disconnected
 */
sealed class MotorConnectionState {

    data object Idle : MotorConnectionState()

    data object Scanning : MotorConnectionState()

    data class Connecting(val macAddress: String) : MotorConnectionState()

    data class DiscoveringServices(val macAddress: String) : MotorConnectionState()

    data class Ready(
        val macAddress: String,
        val mtu: Int,
        val connectionPriority: Priority,
        val negotiatedRotationHz: Float,
    ) : MotorConnectionState()

    data class Reconnecting(
        val macAddress: String,
        val attempt: Int,
        val nextDelayMillis: Long,
    ) : MotorConnectionState()

    data class Disconnected(
        val macAddress: String?,
        val reason: Reason,
    ) : MotorConnectionState() {
        enum class Reason {
            USER_REQUEST,
            GATT_STATUS_ERROR,
            TIMEOUT,
            BLUETOOTH_OFF,
            PERMISSION_DENIED,
            UNKNOWN,
        }
    }

    data class Failed(
        val macAddress: String?,
        val error: com.prism.ot.core.PrismError,
    ) : MotorConnectionState()

    enum class Priority { BALANCED, HIGH }

    val isConnected: Boolean get() = this is Ready
}
```

## 7.2 `motor/MotorGattSpec.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import java.util.UUID

/**
 * Wire contract between the phone and the rotating mass's BLE MCU.
 *
 * The MCU firmware is expected to implement:
 *  - Speed characteristic: **write without response**, 4 bytes little-endian
 *    IEEE-754 float, target rotation in Hz. Write-without-response is used
 *    because the motor loop runs at 5–20 Hz and waiting for a write
 *    acknowledgement would halve the achievable control rate.
 *  - Telemetry characteristic: **notify**, 12 bytes:
 *        [0..3]  actual rotation Hz (f32 LE)
 *        [4..7]  motor current mA     (u32 LE)
 *        [8..11] timestamp millis     (u32 LE)
 *  - Control characteristic: **write with response**, 1 byte command:
 *        0x01 stop, 0x02 run, 0x03 calibrate, 0x04 LED on, 0x05 LED off.
 *
 * UUIDs are fixed for format version 1 and are referenced from
 * [com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants].
 */
object MotorGattSpec {

    val SERVICE: UUID = UUID.fromString(
        "0000a000-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb"
    )
    val CHAR_SPEED: UUID = UUID.fromString(
        "0000a001-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb"
    )
    val CHAR_TELEMETRY: UUID = UUID.fromString(
        "0000a002-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb"
    )
    val CHAR_CONTROL: UUID = UUID.fromString(
        "0000a003-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb"
    )
    val CCCD: UUID = UUID.fromString(
        "00002902-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb"
    )

    const val SPEED_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 4
    const val TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 12

    const val CMD_STOP: Byte = 0x01
    const val CMD_RUN: Byte = 0x02
    const val CMD_CALIBRATE: Byte = 0x03
    const val CMD_LED_ON: Byte = 0x04
    const val CMD_LED_OFF: Byte = 0x05

    /** Desired MTU. 517 is the maximum common value on modern Android. */
    const val TARGET_MTU = 517

    /** Default MTU if negotiation fails (BLE minimum is 23). */
    const val FALLBACK_MTU = 23

    /** Per-operation timeout. Motor commands must never hang the loop. */
    const val OPERATION_TIMEOUT_MS = 4_000L

    /** Service discovery must complete within this window. */
    const val DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000L

    /** Heartbeat interval to keep the link active and prevent the OS from
     *  widening the connection interval during idle periods. */
    const val HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS = 5_000L
}
```

## 7.3 `motor/MotorController.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow

/**
 * Public motor façade. All methods are safe to call from any dispatcher;
 * the implementation serializes GATT operations internally.
 *
 * The controller does not scan. It connects to a known MAC address that the
 * user selected (or that was persisted from a previous session). Scanning
 * is exposed as a separate one-shot flow in [scanForMotors].
 */
interface MotorController {

    val connectionState: StateFlow<MotorConnectionState>

    /** Latest telemetry, or null if none has arrived yet. */
    val telemetry: StateFlow<MotorTelemetry?>

    /** One-shot scan for nearby PRISM motor peripherals. */
    suspend fun scanForMotors(timeoutMillis: Long = 5_000L): PrismResult<List<DiscoveredMotor>>

    suspend fun connect(macAddress: String): PrismResult<Unit>

    suspend fun disconnect()

    /** Sets target rotation in Hz. Fire-and-forget; returns once queued. */
    suspend fun setTargetRotationHz(hz: Float): PrismResult<Unit>

    suspend fun sendControl(command: Byte): PrismResult<Unit>

    suspend fun requestHighThroughput(): PrismResult<Unit>
}

data class DiscoveredMotor(
    val macAddress: String,
    val displayName: String,
    val rssi: Int,
)

data class MotorTelemetry(
    val actualRotationHz: Float,
    val currentMilliAmps: Long,
    val deviceTimestampMillis: Long,
    val receivedAtEpochMillis: Long,
)
```

## 7.4 `motor/GattOperationQueue.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import kotlinx.coroutines.CompletableDeferred
import kotlinx.coroutines.TimeoutCancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.Channel
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

/**
 * Serializes GATT operations on a single [android.bluetooth.BluetoothGatt].
 *
 * Why this exists: the Android platform allows only one outstanding GATT
 * operation per `BluetoothGatt` instance. Issuing a second write before the
 * first callback fires silently fails or corrupts the stack state. There is
 * no coroutine wrapper in the platform API, so every operation must be
 * bridged through `suspendCancellableCoroutine` or, as here, a channel plus
 * a deferred completion map.
 *
 * The queue is intentionally **single-producer**. The controller submits
 * operations from the policy loop and from user actions; concurrent
 * submissions are serialized by the channel's capacity of
 * [Channel.UNLIMITED] and the single consumer coroutine.
 */
internal class GattOperationQueue(
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
    private val scope: kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope,
    private val timeoutMillis: Long = MotorGattSpec.OPERATION_TIMEOUT_MS,
) {

    internal sealed class Op {
        data class Write(
            val characteristicUuid: UUID,
            val value: ByteArray,
            val withoutResponse: Boolean,
        ) : Op()

        data class Read(val characteristicUuid: UUID) : Op()

        data class WriteDescriptor(
            val characteristicUuid: UUID,
            val descriptorUuid: UUID,
            val value: ByteArray,
        ) : Op()

        data object NegotiateMtu : Op()

        data object RequestHighPriority : Op()
    }

    private val channel = Channel<Queued>(Channel.UNLIMITED)
    private val pending = ConcurrentHashMap<Queued, CompletableDeferred<ByteArray?>>()
    @Volatile private var started = false

    internal data class Queued(
        val op: Op,
        val completion: CompletableDeferred<ByteArray?>,
    )

    fun start() {
        if (started) return
        started = true
        scope.launch {
            for (queued in channel) {
                val result = runCatching { awaitNative(queued) }
                result.fold(
                    onSuccess = { queued.completion.complete(it) },
                    onFailure = { queued.completion.completeExceptionally(it) },
                )
                pending.remove(queued)
            }
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        started = false
        channel.close()
        pending.values.forEach {
            it.completeExceptionally(IllegalStateException("GATT queue stopped"))
        }
        pending.clear()
    }

    suspend fun submit(op: Op): ByteArray? {
        val completion = CompletableDeferred<ByteArray?>()
        val queued = Queued(op, completion)
        pending[queued] = completion
        channel.send(queued)
        return try {
            withTimeout(timeoutMillis) { completion.await() }
        } catch (t: TimeoutCancellationException) {
            pending.remove(queued)
            logger.w(TAG, "GATT op timed out: ${op::class.simpleName}")
            throw t
        }
    }

    /**
     * Invoked by the controller's `BluetoothGattCallback` when the native
     * operation completes. Matches the oldest pending operation of the same
     * kind and completes it.
     */
    fun completeOldest(kind: Class<out Op>, value: ByteArray? = null): Boolean {
        val entry = pending.entries.firstOrNull { kind.isInstance(it.key.op) } ?: return false
        pending.remove(entry.key)
        entry.value.complete(value)
        return true
    }

    fun failAll(reason: Throwable) {
        pending.values.forEach { it.completeExceptionally(reason) }
        pending.clear()
    }

    /**
     * Placeholder: the actual native call is issued by the controller's
     * callback bridge. This method exists so the queue's consumer has a
     * suspension point that does not block the scope.
     */
    private suspend fun awaitNative(queued: Queued): ByteArray? =
        queued.completion.await()

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "GattOperationQueue"
    }
}
```

---

## 7.5 `motor/MotorControllerImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothAdapter
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothDevice
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothGatt
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothGattCallback
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothGattCharacteristic
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothGattDescriptor
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothManager
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothProfile
import android.bluetooth.le.ScanCallback
import android.bluetooth.le.ScanResult
import android.bluetooth.le.ScanSettings
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.CompletableDeferred
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton
import kotlin.coroutines.resume
import kotlin.coroutines.resumeWithException

/**
 * Native `BluetoothGatt` implementation of [MotorController].
 *
 * Design decisions:
 *  - **No third-party BLE library.** The motor protocol is four
 *    characteristics and one command byte; the Nordic `ble-ktx` wrapper
 *    would add ~500 KB and a coroutine abstraction that we would still
 *    have to serialize ourselves. A hand-rolled queue is smaller and
 *    easier to audit for this narrow surface.
 *  - **`autoConnect = false`.** The controller owns reconnection with
 *    exponential backoff. Platform auto-connect is opaque and can hold a
 *    stale GATT object indefinitely.
 *  - **MTU 517 + `CONNECTION_PRIORITY_HIGH`.** Requested immediately after
 *    service discovery. This is the documented combination for maximizing
 *    BLE throughput.
 *  - **Write-without-response for speed commands.** At 5–20 Hz, waiting
 *    for a write acknowledgement halves the control rate.
 *  - **Heartbeat every 5 s.** Prevents the OS from widening the connection
 *    interval during idle periods, which would otherwise introduce
 *    hundreds of milliseconds of latency.
 */
@SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
@Singleton
class MotorControllerImpl @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) : MotorController {

    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)

    private val _connectionState = MutableStateFlow<MotorConnectionState>(
        MotorConnectionState.Idle,
    )
    override val connectionState: StateFlow<MotorConnectionState> =
        _connectionState.asStateFlow()

    private val _telemetry = MutableStateFlow<MotorTelemetry?>(null)
    override val telemetry: StateFlow<MotorTelemetry?> = _telemetry.asStateFlow()

    private val bluetoothManager: BluetoothManager? =
        context.getSystemService(Context.BLUETOOTH_SERVICE) as? BluetoothManager
    private val adapter: BluetoothAdapter?
        get() = bluetoothManager?.adapter

    @Volatile private var gatt: BluetoothGatt? = null
    @Volatile private var queue: GattOperationQueue? = null
    @Volatile private var reconnectJob: Job? = null
    @Volatile private var heartbeatJob: Job? = null
    @Volatile private var manualDisconnect = false
    @Volatile private var reconnectAttempt = 0
    @Volatile private var targetMac: String? = null
    @Volatile private var negotiatedMtu: Int = MotorGattSpec.FALLBACK_MTU

    private val scanResults = ConcurrentHashMap<String, DiscoveredMotor>()

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Scanning
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    override suspend fun scanForMotors(timeoutMillis: Long): PrismResult<List<DiscoveredMotor>> =
        runCatching {
            requireBluetoothPermission()
            val bluetoothAdapter = adapter ?: error("Bluetooth adapter unavailable")
            require(bluetoothAdapter.isEnabled) { "Bluetooth is disabled" }

            scanResults.clear()
            val scanner = bluetoothAdapter.bluetoothLeScanner
                ?: error("BLE scanner unavailable")

            val callback = object : ScanCallback() {
                override fun onScanResult(callbackType: Int, result: ScanResult) {
                    val device = result.device ?: return
                    if (result.scanRecord?.serviceUuids?.any {
                            it.uuid == MotorGattSpec.SERVICE
                        } != true
                    ) return
                    scanResults[device.address] = DiscoveredMotor(
                        macAddress = device.address,
                        displayName = result.scanRecord?.deviceName
                            ?: device.name
                            ?: "PRISM mass",
                        rssi = result.rssi,
                    )
                }

                override fun onScanFailed(errorCode: Int) {
                    logger.e(TAG, "Scan failed: $errorCode")
                }
            }

            val settings = ScanSettings.Builder()
                .setScanMode(ScanSettings.SCAN_MODE_LOW_LATENCY)
                .build()

            scanner.startScan(null, settings, callback)
            try {
                delay(timeoutMillis)
            } finally {
                runCatching { scanner.stopScan(callback) }
            }
            scanResults.values.sortedByDescending { it.rssi }
        }.fold(
            onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
            onFailure = { t ->
                logger.e(TAG, "scanForMotors failed", t)
                PrismResult.Failure(
                    if (t is SecurityException) PrismError.BluetoothUnavailable
                    else PrismError.MotorConnectionFailed(t.message ?: "scan"),
                    t,
                )
            },
        )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Connection
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    override suspend fun connect(macAddress: String): PrismResult<Unit> = runCatching {
        requireBluetoothPermission()
        val bluetoothAdapter = adapter ?: error("Bluetooth adapter unavailable")
        require(bluetoothAdapter.isEnabled) { "Bluetooth is disabled" }

        disconnectInternal(silent = true)
        manualDisconnect = false
        targetMac = macAddress
        reconnectAttempt = 0

        _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.Connecting(macAddress)
        val device = bluetoothAdapter.getRemoteDevice(macAddress)
            ?: error("Unknown device $macAddress")

        val connected = suspendCancellableCoroutine<BluetoothGatt> { cont ->
            val callback = buildGattCallback(cont)
            val newGatt = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
                device.connectGatt(context, false, callback, BluetoothDevice.TRANSPORT_LE)
            } else {
                device.connectGatt(context, false, callback)
            }
            if (newGatt == null) {
                cont.resumeWithException(IllegalStateException("connectGatt returned null"))
            }
        }
        gatt = connected

        awaitServicesDiscovered(connected)
        _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.DiscoveringServices(macAddress)

        val opQueue = GattOperationQueue(logger, scope).also { queue = it }
        opQueue.start()

        negotiateMtu(connected)
        requestConnectionPriorityHigh(connected)
        enableTelemetryNotifications(connected)

        _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.Ready(
            macAddress = macAddress,
            mtu = negotiatedMtu,
            connectionPriority = MotorConnectionState.Priority.HIGH,
            negotiatedRotationHz = 0f,
        )
        startHeartbeat()
        Unit
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { t ->
            logger.e(TAG, "connect($macAddress) failed", t)
            _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.Failed(
                macAddress,
                PrismError.MotorConnectionFailed(t.message ?: "connect"),
            )
            runCatching { gatt?.close() }
            gatt = null
            PrismResult.Failure(
                PrismError.MotorConnectionFailed(t.message ?: "connect"),
                t,
            )
        },
    )

    override suspend fun disconnect() {
        manualDisconnect = true
        reconnectJob?.cancel()
        disconnectInternal(silent = false)
    }

    private fun disconnectInternal(silent: Boolean) {
        heartbeatJob?.cancel()
        heartbeatJob = null
        queue?.stop()
        queue = null
        runCatching { gatt?.disconnect() }
        runCatching { gatt?.close() }
        gatt = null
        if (!silent) {
            _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.Disconnected(
                targetMac,
                MotorConnectionState.Disconnected.Reason.USER_REQUEST,
            )
        }
    }

    private suspend fun awaitServicesDiscovered(gatt: BluetoothGatt) {
        withTimeout(MotorGattSpec.DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            suspendCancellableCoroutine<Unit> { cont ->
                val previous = serviceDiscoveryContinuation
                serviceDiscoveryContinuation = cont
                if (!gatt.discoverServices()) {
                    serviceDiscoveryContinuation = previous
                    cont.resumeWithException(IllegalStateException("discoverServices() rejected"))
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun negotiateMtu(gatt: BluetoothGatt) {
        val accepted = gatt.requestMtu(MotorGattSpec.TARGET_MTU)
        if (!accepted) {
            negotiatedMtu = MotorGattSpec.FALLBACK_MTU
            logger.w(TAG, "requestMtu(${MotorGattSpec.TARGET_MTU}) rejected; using $negotiatedMtu")
        }
    }

    private fun requestConnectionPriorityHigh(gatt: BluetoothGatt) {
        val accepted = gatt.requestConnectionPriority(
            BluetoothGatt.CONNECTION_PRIORITY_HIGH,
        )
        if (!accepted) logger.w(TAG, "requestConnectionPriority(HIGH) rejected")
    }

    private fun enableTelemetryNotifications(gatt: BluetoothGatt) {
        val service = gatt.getService(MotorGattSpec.SERVICE) ?: run {
            logger.e(TAG, "Motor service not found")
            return
        }
        val telemetryChar = service.getCharacteristic(MotorGattSpec.CHAR_TELEMETRY) ?: return
        gatt.setCharacteristicNotification(telemetryChar, true)
        val cccd = telemetryChar.getDescriptor(MotorGattSpec.CCCD) ?: return
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            gatt.writeDescriptor(
                cccd,
                BluetoothGattDescriptor.ENABLE_NOTIFICATION_VALUE,
            )
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            cccd.value = BluetoothGattDescriptor.ENABLE_NOTIFICATION_VALUE
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            gatt.writeDescriptor(cccd)
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Commands
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    override suspend fun setTargetRotationHz(hz: Float): PrismResult<Unit> = runCatching {
        val activeGatt = gatt ?: error("Not connected")
        val service = activeGatt.getService(MotorGattSpec.SERVICE)
            ?: error("Motor service missing")
        val characteristic = service.getCharacteristic(MotorGattSpec.CHAR_SPEED)
            ?: error("Speed characteristic missing")

        val payload = ByteBuffer.allocate(MotorGattSpec.SPEED_PAYLOAD_BYTES)
            .order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
            .putFloat(hz)
            .array()

        val writeType = BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_NO_RESPONSE
        val accepted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            activeGatt.writeCharacteristic(characteristic, payload, writeType) ==
                BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            characteristic.writeType = writeType
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            characteristic.value = payload
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            activeGatt.writeCharacteristic(characteristic)
        }
        check(accepted) { "writeCharacteristic rejected" }

        val ready = _connectionState.value
        if (ready is MotorConnectionState.Ready) {
            _connectionState.value = ready.copy(negotiatedRotationHz = hz)
        }
        Unit
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { t ->
            logger.w(TAG, "setTargetRotationHz($hz) failed: ${t.message}")
            PrismResult.Failure(
                PrismError.MotorCommandFailed(t.message ?: "write"),
                t,
            )
        },
    )

    override suspend fun sendControl(command: Byte): PrismResult<Unit> = runCatching {
        val activeGatt = gatt ?: error("Not connected")
        val service = activeGatt.getService(MotorGattSpec.SERVICE)
            ?: error("Motor service missing")
        val characteristic = service.getCharacteristic(MotorGattSpec.CHAR_CONTROL)
            ?: error("Control characteristic missing")

        val payload = byteArrayOf(command)
        val accepted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            activeGatt.writeCharacteristic(
                characteristic,
                payload,
                BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_DEFAULT,
            ) == BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            characteristic.writeType = BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_DEFAULT
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            characteristic.value = payload
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            activeGatt.writeCharacteristic(characteristic)
        }
        check(accepted) { "writeCharacteristic rejected" }
        Unit
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { t ->
            logger.w(TAG, "sendControl(${command}) failed: ${t.message}")
            PrismResult.Failure(
                PrismError.MotorCommandFailed(t.message ?: "control"),
                t,
            )
        },
    )

    override suspend fun requestHighThroughput(): PrismResult<Unit> = runCatching {
        val activeGatt = gatt ?: error("Not connected")
        requestConnectionPriorityHigh(activeGatt)
        Unit
    }.fold(
        onSuccess = { PrismResult.Success(it) },
        onFailure = { t ->
            PrismResult.Failure(
                PrismError.MotorCommandFailed(t.message ?: "priority"),
                t,
            )
        },
    )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Heartbeat
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun startHeartbeat() {
        heartbeatJob?.cancel()
        heartbeatJob = scope.launch {
            while (true) {
                delay(MotorGattSpec.HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS)
                val activeGatt = gatt ?: break
                val service = activeGatt.getService(MotorGattSpec.SERVICE) ?: break
                val control = service.getCharacteristic(MotorGattSpec.CHAR_CONTROL) ?: break
                // A zero-length write is rejected by most stacks; a read of
                // the control characteristic is the least invasive keep-alive.
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                runCatching { activeGatt.readCharacteristic(control) }
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // GATT callback
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun buildGattCallback(
        connectionContinuation: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellableContinuation<BluetoothGatt>,
    ): BluetoothGattCallback = object : BluetoothGattCallback() {

        override fun onConnectionStateChange(
            gatt: BluetoothGatt,
            status: Int,
            newState: Int,
        ) {
            when (newState) {
                BluetoothProfile.STATE_CONNECTED -> {
                    if (status == BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS) {
                        if (connectionContinuation.isActive) {
                            connectionContinuation.resume(gatt)
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (connectionContinuation.isActive) {
                            connectionContinuation.resumeWithException(
                                IllegalStateException("GATT status $status"),
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                BluetoothProfile.STATE_DISCONNECTED -> {
                    logger.w(TAG, "Disconnected (status=$status)")
                    val reason = if (status == BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS) {
                        MotorConnectionState.Disconnected.Reason.USER_REQUEST
                    } else {
                        MotorConnectionState.Disconnected.Reason.GATT_STATUS_ERROR
                    }
                    _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.Disconnected(
                        targetMac,
                        reason,
                    )
                    queue?.failAll(IllegalStateException("GATT disconnected"))
                    if (!manualDisconnect) scheduleReconnect()
                }
            }
        }

        override fun onServicesDiscovered(gatt: BluetoothGatt, status: Int) {
            val cont = serviceDiscoveryContinuation
            serviceDiscoveryContinuation = null
            if (status == BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS) {
                cont?.resume(Unit)
            } else {
                cont?.resumeWithException(
                    IllegalStateException("Service discovery failed: $status"),
                )
            }
        }

        override fun onMtuChanged(gatt: BluetoothGatt, mtu: Int, status: Int) {
            if (status == BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS) {
                negotiatedMtu = mtu
                logger.i(TAG, "MTU negotiated: $mtu")
            } else {
                logger.w(TAG, "MTU change failed: $status")
            }
        }

        override fun onCharacteristicWrite(
            gatt: BluetoothGatt,
            characteristic: BluetoothGattCharacteristic,
            status: Int,
        ) {
            queue?.completeOldest(GattOperationQueue.Op.Write::class.java)
        }

        override fun onCharacteristicRead(
            gatt: BluetoothGatt,
            characteristic: BluetoothGattCharacteristic,
            value: ByteArray,
            status: Int,
        ) {
            queue?.completeOldest(GattOperationQueue.Op.Read::class.java, value)
        }

        override fun onDescriptorWrite(
            gatt: BluetoothGatt,
            descriptor: BluetoothGattDescriptor,
            status: Int,
        ) {
            queue?.completeOldest(GattOperationQueue.Op.WriteDescriptor::class.java)
        }

        override fun onCharacteristicChanged(
            gatt: BluetoothGatt,
            characteristic: BluetoothGattCharacteristic,
            value: ByteArray,
        ) {
            if (characteristic.uuid == MotorGattSpec.CHAR_TELEMETRY &&
                value.size >= MotorGattSpec.TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_BYTES
            ) {
                val buf = ByteBuffer.wrap(value).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
                _telemetry.value = MotorTelemetry(
                    actualRotationHz = buf.float,
                    currentMilliAmps = buf.int.toLong() and 0xFFFF_FFFFL,
                    deviceTimestampMillis = buf.int.toLong() and 0xFFFF_FFFFL,
                    receivedAtEpochMillis = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    @Volatile private var serviceDiscoveryContinuation:
        kotlinx.coroutines.CancellableContinuation<Unit>? = null

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Reconnection
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun scheduleReconnect() {
        val mac = targetMac ?: return
        if (manualDisconnect) return
        reconnectJob?.cancel()
        reconnectJob = scope.launch {
            while (!manualDisconnect) {
                reconnectAttempt++
                val delayMillis = backoffMillis(reconnectAttempt)
                _connectionState.value = MotorConnectionState.Reconnecting(
                    mac,
                    reconnectAttempt,
                    delayMillis,
                )
                delay(delayMillis)
                val result = connect(mac)
                if (result is PrismResult.Success) {
                    reconnectAttempt = 0
                    return@launch
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun backoffMillis(attempt: Int): Long {
        val base = 1_000L
        val capped = base shl (attempt - 1).coerceIn(0, 5)
        return capped.coerceAtMost(30_000L)
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Permissions
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun requireBluetoothPermission() {
        val permissions = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            listOf(
                android.Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN,
                android.Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT,
            )
        } else {
            listOf(android.Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
        }
        permissions.forEach { perm ->
            check(
                ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, perm) ==
                    PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED,
            ) { "Bluetooth permission $perm not granted" }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "MotorController"
    }
}
```
