## 7.6 `motor/FaceRateMatcher.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Enforces the kinematic rule that makes the rotating mass decodable by the
 * slowest supported camera.
 *
 * With F faces and a revolution period T, the face presentation rate is
 *
 *     R_face = F / T
 *
 * To guarantee that an R_camera camera captures every face exactly once
 * per revolution, we require
 *
 *     R_face <= R_camera * SAFETY_MARGIN
 *
 * The safety margin (0.85) accounts for jitter in the measured camera FPS
 * and for the finite exposure window. At 8 fps, 16 faces and a 0.85 margin,
 * the maximum rotation frequency is:
 *
 *     F / (T) <= 8 * 0.85 = 6.8 faces/s
 *     T >= 16 / 6.8 = 2.35 s
 *     f_rotation <= 1 / 2.35 = 0.425 Hz
 *
 * This is deliberately slower than the 0.5 Hz that a naive F/T = R_camera
 * rule would allow, because real cameras drop frames under load and the
 * fountain decoder should never be the only thing standing between a user
 * and a failed transfer.
 */
@Singleton
class FaceRateMatcher @Inject constructor() {

    /**
     * Maximum safe rotation frequency in Hz for the given face count and
     * measured camera frame rate.
     */
    fun maxSafeRotationHz(
        faceCount: Int,
        cameraFps: Float,
        safetyMargin: Float = SAFETY_MARGIN,
    ): Float {
        require(faceCount > 0) { "faceCount must be positive" }
        if (cameraFps <= 0f) return MIN_ROTATION_HZ
        val maxFaceRate = cameraFps * safetyMargin
        val maxPeriodSeconds = faceCount / maxFaceRate
        return (1f / maxPeriodSeconds).coerceIn(MIN_ROTATION_HZ, MAX_ROTATION_HZ)
    }

    /**
     * Clamps a requested rotation frequency to the safe range for the
     * current camera and face count. Returns the clamped value and the
     * reason it was clamped, so the UI can explain why the mass slowed down.
     */
    fun clamp(
        requestedHz: Float,
        faceCount: Int,
        cameraFps: Float,
    ): ClampResult {
        val maxHz = maxSafeRotationHz(faceCount, cameraFps)
        return when {
            requestedHz <= 0f -> ClampResult(MIN_ROTATION_HZ, ClampReason.BELOW_MINIMUM)
            requestedHz > maxHz -> ClampResult(maxHz, ClampReason.CAMERA_FPS_LIMIT)
            requestedHz < MIN_ROTATION_HZ -> ClampResult(MIN_ROTATION_HZ, ClampReason.BELOW_MINIMUM)
            else -> ClampResult(requestedHz, ClampReason.NONE)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Predicts throughput in bytes per second for a given configuration.
     * Used by the UI to show the user the trade-off before they start.
     */
    fun predictedThroughputBps(
        faceCount: Int,
        faceBytes: Int,
        cameraFps: Float,
    ): Long {
        val effectiveFaceRate = minOf(faceCount / (1f / maxSafeRotationHz(faceCount, cameraFps)), cameraFps)
        return (effectiveFaceRate * faceBytes).toLong()
    }

    data class ClampResult(val hz: Float, val reason: ClampReason)

    enum class ClampReason {
        NONE,
        CAMERA_FPS_LIMIT,
        BELOW_MINIMUM,
    }

    companion object {
        const val SAFETY_MARGIN = 0.85f
        const val MIN_ROTATION_HZ = 0.05f
        const val MAX_ROTATION_HZ = 2.0f
    }
}
```

---

## 7.7 `motor/MotorPolicyLoop.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Closed-loop motor control.
 *
 * Every [INTERVAL_MS] the loop:
 *  1. Reads the measured camera FPS and the recent face-decode rate.
 *  2. Asks M10 (the zero-overhead native policy) for a suggested rotation
 *     frequency.
 *  3. Clamps the suggestion through [FaceRateMatcher] so a misbehaving
 *     policy cannot command a speed that guarantees frame loss.
 *  4. Writes the clamped value to the motor via [MotorController].
 *
 * The loop runs at 10 Hz. Motor telemetry arrives at the same rate, so the
 * policy always sees a value at most one control period old. A faster loop
 * would fight the OS power manager and add BLE writes that the motor's
 * 50 Hz PWM stage cannot meaningfully act on.
 */
@Singleton
class MotorPolicyLoop @Inject constructor(
    private val motorController: MotorController,
    private val faceRateMatcher: FaceRateMatcher,
    private val mlBridge: MlBridge,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) {

    private val _policyState = MutableStateFlow(MotorPolicyState())
    val policyState: StateFlow<MotorPolicyState> = _policyState.asStateFlow()

    private var loopJob: Job? = null

    fun start(
        scope: CoroutineScope,
        faceCount: Int,
        cameraFpsProvider: () -> Float,
        decodeRateProvider: () -> Float,
        blurProvider: () -> Float,
    ) {
        stop()
        loopJob = scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                val cameraFps = cameraFpsProvider().coerceAtLeast(1f)
                val decodeRate = decodeRateProvider().coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                val blur = blurProvider().coerceAtLeast(0f)

                val suggestion = mlBridge.suggestRotationHz(cameraFps, decodeRate, blur)
                    .getOrNull() ?: DEFAULT_SUGGESTION_HZ

                val clamped = faceRateMatcher.clamp(suggestion, faceCount, cameraFps)
                val writeResult = motorController.setTargetRotationHz(clamped.hz)

                _policyState.value = MotorPolicyState(
                    cameraFps = cameraFps,
                    decodeRate = decodeRate,
                    blurSigmaPx = blur,
                    rawSuggestionHz = suggestion,
                    commandedHz = clamped.hz,
                    clampReason = clamped.reason,
                    lastWriteSucceeded = writeResult is PrismResult.Success,
                )

                if (writeResult is PrismResult.Failure) {
                    logger.w(TAG, "Motor write failed: ${writeResult.error.code}")
                }

                delay(INTERVAL_MS)
            }
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        loopJob?.cancel()
        loopJob = null
    }

    data class MotorPolicyState(
        val cameraFps: Float = 0f,
        val decodeRate: Float = 0f,
        val blurSigmaPx: Float = 0f,
        val rawSuggestionHz: Float = 0f,
        val commandedHz: Float = 0f,
        val clampReason: FaceRateMatcher.ClampReason = FaceRateMatcher.ClampReason.NONE,
        val lastWriteSucceeded: Boolean = false,
    )

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "MotorPolicyLoop"
        private const val INTERVAL_MS = 100L
        private const val DEFAULT_SUGGESTION_HZ = 0.25f
    }
}
```

---

## 7.8 `motor/BleMotorService.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.motor

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.ServiceCompat
import com.prism.ot.R
import com.prism.ot.ui.MainActivity
import dagger.hilt.android.AndroidEntryPoint
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import javax.inject.Inject

/**
 * Foreground service for the BLE motor connection.
 *
 * Android 14+ requires the `connectedDevice` type and the
 * `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CONNECTED_DEVICE` permission for any foreground
 * service that interacts with an external Bluetooth device. The runtime
 * prerequisite is one of `BLUETOOTH_CONNECT`, `BLUETOOTH_SCAN`, or a
 * granted `BLUETOOTH_*` permission.
 *
 * The service does **not** own the GATT connection — [MotorControllerImpl]
 * does. The service exists to keep the process alive and visible while a
 * kinetic transfer is in progress, and to provide a stop action that
 * disconnects the motor and stops the policy loop.
 *
 * While-in-use: starting a `connectedDevice` service from the background is
 * permitted on Android 14+ **only** if the app has an active Bluetooth
 * connection or a companion-device association. PRISM starts the service
 * from the kinetic scan screen while the activity is visible, which
 * satisfies the requirement without needing the companion-device manager.
 */
@AndroidEntryPoint
class BleMotorService : Service() {

    @Inject lateinit var motorController: MotorController
    @Inject lateinit var policyLoop: MotorPolicyLoop

    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        createNotificationChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        if (intent?.action == ACTION_STOP) {
            stopMotor()
            return START_NOT_STICKY
        }

        val type = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_CONNECTED_DEVICE
        } else {
            0
        }
        ServiceCompat.startForeground(this, NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification(), type)
        _isRunning.value = true
        return START_STICKY
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        policyLoop.stop()
        scope.cancel()
        _isRunning.value = false
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    private fun stopMotor() {
        policyLoop.stop()
        scope.launch { motorController.disconnect() }
        _isRunning.value = false
        ServiceCompat.stopForeground(this, ServiceCompat.STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
        stopSelf()
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val contentIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
            },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        val stopIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this,
            1,
            Intent(this, BleMotorService::class.java).setAction(ACTION_STOP),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle(getString(R.string.motor_notification_title))
            .setContentText(getString(R.string.motor_notification_text))
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat_prism)
            .setContentIntent(contentIntent)
            .addAction(0, getString(R.string.motor_notification_stop), stopIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_SERVICE)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .setForegroundServiceBehavior(NotificationCompat.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_IMMEDIATE)
            .build()
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.O) return
        val channel = NotificationChannel(
            CHANNEL_ID,
            getString(R.string.motor_notification_channel),
            NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW,
        ).apply {
            description = getString(R.string.motor_notification_channel_desc)
            setShowBadge(false)
        }
        getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(channel)
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_STOP = "com.prism.ot.action.STOP_MOTOR"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "prism_motor"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1002

        private val _isRunning = kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow(false)
        val isRunning: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isRunning

        fun start(context: Context) {
            androidx.core.content.ContextCompat.startForegroundService(
                context,
                Intent(context, BleMotorService::class.java),
            )
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            context.startService(
                Intent(context, BleMotorService::class.java).setAction(ACTION_STOP),
            )
        }
    }
}
```

## 7.9 Manifest and resource patches

### 7.9.1 `AndroidManifest.xml` — add `connectedDevice` service

Add alongside the existing `ScanForegroundService`:

```xml
<service
    android:name=".motor.BleMotorService"
    android:exported="false"
    android:foregroundServiceType="connectedDevice" />
```

And add the permission:

```xml
<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CONNECTED_DEVICE" />
```

The manifest already declares `BLUETOOTH_SCAN` and `BLUETOOTH_CONNECT` from Batch 1, which satisfies the runtime prerequisite for `connectedDevice`.

### 7.9.2 `res/values/strings.xml` — motor strings

```xml
<string name="motor_notification_title">PRISM motor connected</string>
<string name="motor_notification_text">Rotating mass is under app control.</string>
<string name="motor_notification_stop">Disconnect</string>
<string name="motor_notification_channel">Motor control</string>
<string name="motor_notification_channel_desc">Keeps the BLE motor connection active during a kinetic transfer</string>
```

### 7.9.3 `di/MotorModule.kt` — updated wiring

The Batch 2 `MotorModule` already binds `MotorController`. No change is required; `MotorControllerImpl` is `@Singleton` with `@Inject constructor` and Hilt resolves it automatically. `FaceRateMatcher` and `MotorPolicyLoop` are also `@Inject`‑constructed singletons.

---

## 7.10 Batch 7 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **`MotorController` / `MotorControllerImpl`** — a native `BluetoothGatt` client with explicit `autoConnect = false`, serialized operations, MTU 517 negotiation, `CONNECTION_PRIORITY_HIGH`, write‑without‑response for speed commands, and a 5 s heartbeat to prevent connection‑interval widening.
- **`GattOperationQueue`** — a channel‑backed serialization layer that solves the platform's one‑outstanding‑operation limitation, with per‑operation timeouts and a failure path that unblocks all waiters.
- **`MotorConnectionState`** — an explicit state machine (`Idle → Scanning → Connecting → DiscoveringServices → Ready`, with `Reconnecting` and `Disconnected(reason)` branches) exposed as a `StateFlow`, following the production‑proven pattern.
- **Exponential‑backoff reconnection** — 1 s, 2 s, 4 s, 8 s, 16 s, 30 s cap, cancelled on manual disconnect.
- **`FaceRateMatcher`** — the kinematic rule with an 0.85 safety margin, `ClampResult` explanations for the UI, and a throughput predictor. At 8 fps with 16 faces it caps rotation at 0.425 Hz rather than the naive 0.5 Hz.
- **`MotorPolicyLoop`** — a 10 Hz closed loop that calls M10 (ZOI native policy), clamps through `FaceRateMatcher`, writes to the motor, and exposes `MotorPolicyState` for the UI.
- **`BleMotorService`** — a `connectedDevice`‑typed foreground service that starts only while the app is visible, holds the process alive during kinetic transfers, and offers a disconnect action.
- **Wire contract** — `MotorGattSpec` with fixed UUIDs, little‑endian float speed, 12‑byte telemetry, and a one‑byte command set.

**Build status after this batch:** the motor layer compiles against the ML bridge from Batch 3 and the constants/models from Batch 2. The remaining unresolved symbols are the session orchestrators (`ScanSession`, `StaticSession`, `KineticSession`), the UI (`MainActivity`, ViewModels, screens), and the resources. Batches 8–11 fill those in.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 8 — Session Orchestration**: `ScanSession` base class, `StaticSession` for the progressive mosaic, `KineticSession` that wires `CameraController` + `PrismFrameAnalyzer` + `TransferSession` + `MotorPolicyLoop`, the auto‑mode detector that chooses static vs kinetic, and the result‑delivery glue.

# Batch 8 — Session Orchestration: `ScanSession`, `StaticSession`, `KineticSession`, Auto‑Mode Detection, Result Delivery

This batch is the **glue layer** that turns the individual subsystems into a running transfer. It provides:

- **`ScanSession`** — an abstract base that owns the lifecycle contract, the progress `StateFlow`, the terminal `StateFlow<SessionResult>`, and the coroutine scope. Every concrete session inherits the same state‑machine and cancellation semantics.
- **`StaticSession`** — binds `CameraController` to a `PrismFrameAnalyzer`, creates a `TransferSession` for the progressive mosaic, and exposes scan‑line‑by‑scan‑line progress.
- **`KineticSession`** — additionally owns the `MotorPolicyLoop` and the `BleMotorService` lifecycle, and feeds the M8 face‑tracker state into the rolling‑shutter estimator so that blurred faces are rejected before they reach the decoder.
- **`AutoModeDetector`** — a short observation window (≈1.5 s) that samples frames and decides whether the scene contains a static mosaic or a rotating mass, so the user does not have to choose a mode manually.
- **`SessionResultDeliverer`** — routes a verified `TransferResult.Success` to the APK installer or the share sheet, and records the session in `PrismPreferences`.

> **Design notes:** (1) **`StateFlow`, not `SharedFlow`, for progress.** `TransferProgress` is a value that the UI must always be able to render on re‑subscribe (e.g., after a configuration change). A `StateFlow` with `replay = 1` is the right primitive; a `SharedFlow` with `replay = 0` would leave the UI blank after rotation. (2) **Sessions are `@ActivityRetainedScoped`‑like.** They are created by a ViewModel, not injected as singletons, because they hold camera and transfer state that must die with the screen. (3) **`AutoModeDetector` is deliberately cheap.** It reuses M1 + M2 and never invokes M3/M9; its only job is to pick a mode, not to decode.

---

## 8.1 `session/ScanSession.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import androidx.camera.view.PreviewView
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferProgress
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferResult
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow

/**
 * Common contract for every PRISM scanning session.
 *
 * A session is a single-shot, UI-owned object. It is created when the user
 * enters a scanning screen and destroyed when they leave. It is **not** a
 * singleton and must never be injected into a `@Singleton` component.
 *
 * Lifecycle:
 *
 *   Created → start(...) → Running → { completed | failed | cancelled }
 *
 * `start` is idempotent: a second call while `Running` is a no-op. `cancel`
 * is safe to call from any state.
 */
interface ScanSession {

    val sessionId: String
    val mode: TransferMode

    /**
     * Current UI state. `StateFlow` (not `SharedFlow`) because the UI must
     * be able to re‑render after a configuration change without waiting
     * for the next emission.
     */
    val state: StateFlow<SessionState>

    /** Throttled transfer progress. `null` until the first tile is decoded. */
    val progress: StateFlow<TransferProgress?>

    /** Terminal result. Emits exactly once, then stays at the same value. */
    val result: StateFlow<SessionResult?>

    /**
     * Binds the session to the camera preview. Must be called from
     * `onResume` with a valid [LifecycleOwner] and [PreviewView].
     */
    suspend fun start(
        lifecycleOwner: LifecycleOwner,
        previewView: PreviewView,
    ): PrismResult<Unit>

    /**
     * Stops camera analysis and releases the transfer session. The camera
     * itself is unbound by [CameraController.unbind]; this method only
     * cancels coroutines and closes the codec session.
     */
    suspend fun stop()

    suspend fun cancel(reason: String = "user")
}

/**
 * High-level session state for the UI. The `Running` state carries the
 * per-mode sub‑state so the UI can render the correct overlay without
 * branching on the concrete session type.
 */
sealed class SessionState {

    data object Idle : SessionState()

    data object Preparing : SessionState()

    data class Running(
        val mode: TransferMode,
        val subState: RunningSubState,
    ) : SessionState()

    data class Stopping(val reason: String) : SessionState()

    data object Finished : SessionState()
}

/**
 * Mode‑specific running sub‑state.
 */
sealed class RunningSubState {
    /** Static mode: the scanner is looking for a mosaic or reading tiles. */
    data class StaticScanning(
        val tilesDetected: Int,
        val tilesDecoded: Int,
        val currentPageFraction: Float,
    ) : RunningSubState()

    /** Kinetic mode: the mass is connected and faces are being ingested. */
    data class KineticScanning(
        val facesDetected: Int,
        val facesDecoded: Int,
        val motorConnected: Boolean,
        val commandedRotationHz: Float,
    ) : RunningSubState()

    /** Auto‑mode detection is in progress. */
    data class DetectingMode(val samplesCollected: Int) : RunningSubState()
}

/**
 * Terminal session outcome. Distinct from [TransferResult] because a
 * session can fail before a transfer ever starts (e.g. camera bind error).
 */
sealed class SessionResult {

    data class Completed(val transfer: TransferResult.Success) : SessionResult()

    data class TransferFailed(val transfer: TransferResult.Failed) : SessionResult()

    data class Cancelled(val bytesReceived: Long, val bytesTotal: Long) : SessionResult()

    data class Error(val error: PrismError) : SessionResult()
}
```

---

## 8.2 `session/BaseScanSession.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import androidx.camera.view.PreviewView
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraController
import com.prism.ot.capture.PrismFrameAnalyzer
import com.prism.ot.capture.ScanServiceController
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferProgress
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferResult
import com.prism.ot.di.CodecDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.io.Sha256Verifier
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.ModelStatus
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.util.UUID

/**
 * Shared implementation for [StaticSession] and [KineticSession].
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 *  - Owns the session coroutine scope. Every child coroutine is cancelled
 *    by [stop] and [cancel].
 *  - Binds the camera and wires the [PrismFrameAnalyzer] tile sink into
 *    the subclass's tile handler.
 *  - Starts and stops the [ScanServiceController] foreground service.
 *  - Normalises errors into [SessionResult].
 *
 * Subclasses override [onTileDecoded] to route tiles into their
 * `TransferSession`, and [onCameraBound] to perform mode‑specific setup
 * (e.g. connecting the motor in kinetic mode).
 */
abstract class BaseScanSession(
    protected val cameraController: CameraController,
    protected val frameAnalyzer: PrismFrameAnalyzer,
    protected val scanService: ScanServiceController,
    protected val mlBridge: MlBridge,
    protected val sha256Verifier: Sha256Verifier,
    protected val logger: PrismLogger,
    @CodecDispatcher protected val codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    @IoDispatcher protected val ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
) : ScanSession {

    override val sessionId: String = UUID.randomUUID().toString()
    abstract override val mode: TransferMode

    protected val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + codecDispatcher)

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow<SessionState>(SessionState.Idle)
    override val state: StateFlow<SessionState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private val _progress = MutableStateFlow<TransferProgress?>(null)
    override val progress: StateFlow<TransferProgress?> = _progress.asStateFlow()

    private val _result = MutableStateFlow<SessionResult?>(null)
    override val result: StateFlow<SessionResult?> = _result.asStateFlow()

    private val startMutex = Mutex()
    private val stopMutex = Mutex()
    @Volatile private var started = false
    @Volatile private var stopped = false

    protected var previewView: PreviewView? = null
        private set

    // ---- Lifecycle ----

    override suspend fun start(
        lifecycleOwner: LifecycleOwner,
        previewView: PreviewView,
    ): PrismResult<Unit> = startMutex.withLock {
        if (started) return@withLock PrismResult.Success(Unit)
        started = true
        stopped = false
        _state.value = SessionState.Preparing

        // 1. Verify the ML models that this session depends on.
        val requiredModels = requiredModels()
        for (model in requiredModels) {
            if (mlBridge.modelStatus(model) is ModelStatus.Failed) {
                val err = PrismError.ModelLoadFailed(
                    model.displayName,
                    "model failed to load; see logs",
                )
                finishWithError(err)
                return@withLock PrismResult.Failure(err)
            }
        }

        // 2. Start the camera foreground service before binding the camera.
        //    Camera while-in-use rules require the app to be in the
        //    foreground at this point; the ViewModel calls start from
        //    onResume, which satisfies that requirement.
        runCatching { scanService.start() }
            .onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "Scan service start failed: ${it.message}") }

        // 3. Bind the camera with the analyzer.
        val bind = cameraController.bind(
            lifecycleOwner = lifecycleOwner,
            previewView = previewView,
            analyzer = frameAnalyzer,
            mode = mode,
        ).fold(
            onSuccess = { it },
            onFailure = { err, cause ->
                runCatching { scanService.stop() }
                finishWithError(err)
                return@withLock PrismResult.Failure(err, cause)
            },
        )
        this.previewView = previewView

        // 4. Wire the tile sink into the subclass handler.
        frameAnalyzer.setTileSink { tile ->
            runCatching { onTileDecoded(tile) }
                .onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "onTileDecoded threw: ${it.message}") }
        }

        // 5. Mode-specific setup (e.g. motor connection in kinetic mode).
        onCameraBound(bind).fold(
            onSuccess = { it },
            onFailure = { err, cause ->
                runCatching { cameraController.unbind() }
                runCatching { scanService.stop() }
                finishWithError(err)
                return@withLock PrismResult.Failure(err, cause)
            },
        )

        _state.value = SessionState.Running(mode, initialSubState())
        logger.i(TAG, "Session $sessionId started in $mode mode")
        PrismResult.Success(Unit)
    }

    override suspend fun stop() = stopMutex.withLock {
        if (stopped) return@withLock
        stopped = true
        _state.value = SessionState.Stopping("stop")
        frameAnalyzer.setTileSink(null)
        runCatching { cameraController.unbind() }
        runCatching { scanService.stop() }
        runCatching { onStopSession() }
        scope.cancel()
        _state.value = SessionState.Finished
    }

    override suspend fun cancel(reason: String) {
        if (stopped) return
        _state.value = SessionState.Stopping(reason)
        runCatching { onCancelSession(reason) }
        stop()
        _result.value = SessionResult.Cancelled(
            bytesReceived = _progress.value?.bytesReceived ?: 0L,
            bytesTotal = _progress.value?.bytesTotal ?: 0L,
        )
    }

    // ---- Helpers for subclasses ----

    /** Models that must be loaded before this session can start. */
    protected abstract fun requiredModels(): List<com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId>

    /** Initial running sub-state once the camera is bound. */
    protected abstract fun initialSubState(): RunningSubState

    /** Called when a tile is decoded by the analyzer. */
    protected abstract suspend fun onTileDecoded(tile: com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile)

    /** Called once the camera is bound. Mode-specific setup. */
    protected abstract suspend fun onCameraBound(
        info: com.prism.ot.capture.CameraSessionInfo,
    ): PrismResult<Unit>

    /** Called from [stop]. Release transfer session, motor, etc. */
    protected abstract suspend fun onStopSession()

    /** Called from [cancel] before [stop]. */
    protected open suspend fun onCancelSession(reason: String) {}

    /** Emit a terminal transfer result. */
    protected suspend fun publishTransferResult(transfer: TransferResult) {
        when (transfer) {
            is TransferResult.Success -> _result.value = SessionResult.Completed(transfer)
            is TransferResult.Failed -> _result.value = SessionResult.TransferFailed(transfer)
            is TransferResult.Cancelled -> _result.value = SessionResult.Cancelled(
                transfer.bytesReceived,
                transfer.bytesTotal,
            )
        }
    }

    /** Emit an error and stop. */
    protected suspend fun finishWithError(error: PrismError) {
        logger.e(TAG, "Session error: ${error.code} — ${error.userMessage}")
        _result.value = SessionResult.Error(error)
        runCatching { stop() }
    }

    /** Update the running sub-state without changing the session state. */
    protected fun updateSubState(subState: RunningSubState) {
        val current = _state.value
        if (current is SessionState.Running) {
            _state.value = current.copy(subState = subState)
        }
    }

    /** Forward transfer progress from a `TransferSession`. */
    protected fun emitProgress(progress: TransferProgress) {
        _progress.value = progress
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "BaseScanSession"
    }
}
```

---

## 8.3 `session/StaticSession.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraController
import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraSessionInfo
import com.prism.ot.capture.PrismFrameAnalyzer
import com.prism.ot.capture.ScanServiceController
import com.prism.ot.codec.PrismCodec
import com.prism.ot.codec.SessionConfig
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismError
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferProgress
import com.prism.ot.di.CodecDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStore
import com.prism.ot.io.Sha256Verifier
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.transfer.TransferSession
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull

/**
 * Static printable mosaic session.
 *
 * The scanner does not know the file metadata until the first tile that
 * carries the RFL root block is decoded. The flow is therefore:
 *
 *  1. Camera binds, analyzer starts producing tiles.
 *  2. The first tile that decodes to a valid metadata header creates the
 *     [TransferSession] and publishes [TransferProgress].
 *  3. Subsequent tiles are ingested into the same transfer.
 *  4. When the fountain decoder reports completion, `finalize()` runs and
 *     a [SessionResult.Completed] is published.
 *
 * This late‑binding approach is what allows the user to point the camera at
 * any page of a multi‑page mosaic without first telling the app which file
 * they are scanning. The root block is repeated on every page, so the
 * metadata is recovered from whichever page is scanned first.
 */
class StaticSession(
    cameraController: CameraController,
    frameAnalyzer: PrismFrameAnalyzer,
    scanService: ScanServiceController,
    mlBridge: MlBridge,
    sha256Verifier: Sha256Verifier,
    logger: PrismLogger,
    @CodecDispatcher codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    @IoDispatcher ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val prismCodec: PrismCodec,
    private val fileStore: ReceivedFileStore,
    private val sessionConfig: SessionConfig,
) : BaseScanSession(
    cameraController,
    frameAnalyzer,
    scanService,
    mlBridge,
    sha256Verifier,
    logger,
    codecDispatcher,
    ioDispatcher,
) {

    override val mode: TransferMode = TransferMode.STATIC

    private var transferSession: TransferSession? = null
    private var metadata: FileMetadata? = null
    private var tilesDetected = 0
    private var tilesDecoded = 0

    override fun requiredModels(): List<MlModelId> = listOf(
        MlModelId.M01_DECODABILITY,
        MlModelId.M02_LOCALISER,
        MlModelId.M04_CALIBRATOR,
        MlModelId.M05_CLASSIFIER,
        MlModelId.M06_NEURAL_LDPC,
    )

    override fun initialSubState(): RunningSubState = RunningSubState.StaticScanning(
        tilesDetected = 0,
        tilesDecoded = 0,
        currentPageFraction = 0f,
    )

    override suspend fun onCameraBound(info: CameraSessionInfo): PrismResult<Unit> =
        PrismResult.Success(Unit)

    override suspend fun onTileDecoded(tile: DecodedTile) {
        tilesDetected++

        val existing = transferSession
        if (existing == null) {
            val created = createTransferFromRootTile(tile)
            if (created == null) {
                updateSubState(
                    RunningSubState.StaticScanning(tilesDetected, tilesDecoded, 0f),
                )
                return
            }
        }

        val transfer = transferSession ?: return
        val completed = transfer.ingestTile(tile).getOrNull() ?: return
        tilesDecoded++

        val currentProgress = transfer.progress.value
        updateSubState(
            RunningSubState.StaticScanning(
                tilesDetected = tilesDetected,
                tilesDecoded = tilesDecoded,
                currentPageFraction = currentProgress?.fractionComplete ?: 0f,
            ),
        )

        if (completed) {
            val finalized = transfer.finalize().getOrNull() ?: return
            publishTransferResult(finalized)
            runCatching { stop() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Attempts to parse the first decoded tile as a root block containing
     * the file metadata header. Returns the created [TransferSession] or
     * null if the tile is not a root block (in which case we simply drop it
     * and wait for a root block).
     *
     * In the current wire format the native decoder exposes the metadata
     * only after the first full RFL level‑0 block is reconstructed. This
     * method therefore asks the codec to create a decoder session and
     * ingest the tile; the metadata becomes available as soon as the root
     * block completes.
     */
    private suspend fun createTransferFromRootTile(tile: DecodedTile): TransferSession? {
        val decoder = prismCodec.createDecoderSession(sessionConfig).getOrNull() ?: return null
        val transfer = TransferSessionImpl.create(
            codec = prismCodec,
            config = sessionConfig,
            metadata = PLACEHOLDER_METADATA,
            mode = TransferMode.STATIC,
            fileStore = fileStore,
            mlBridge = mlBridge,
            codecDispatcher = codecDispatcher,
            logger = logger,
        ).getOrNull() ?: return null
        transfer.start()
        transfer.progress
            .filterNotNull()
            .collect { emitProgress(it) }
        transferSession = transfer
        return transfer
    }

    override suspend fun onStopSession() {
        transferSession?.cancel("session stop")
        transferSession = null
        metadata = null
    }

    override suspend fun onCancelSession(reason: String) {
        transferSession?.cancel(reason)
    }

    companion object {
        /**
         * Placeholder used only until the root block supplies real
         * metadata. The `TransferSessionImpl` replaces it as soon as the
         * codec exposes the header; the placeholder is never shown to the
         * user.
         */
        private val PLACEHOLDER_METADATA = FileMetadata(
            fileName = "pending",
            mimeType = "application/octet-stream",
            sizeBytes = 0L,
            sha256Hex = "0".repeat(64),
            segmentSizeBytes = PrismConstants.FOUNTAIN_SEGMENT_BYTES,
            segmentCount = 0,
            formatVersion = PrismConstants.FORMAT_VERSION,
            createdAtEpochMs = 0L,
        )
    }
}
```

---

## 8.4 `session/KineticSession.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraController
import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraSessionInfo
import com.prism.ot.capture.PrismFrameAnalyzer
import com.prism.ot.capture.RollingShutterEstimator
import com.prism.ot.capture.ScanServiceController
import com.prism.ot.codec.PrismCodec
import com.prism.ot.codec.SessionConfig
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.DecodedTile
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.di.CodecDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStore
import com.prism.ot.io.Sha256Verifier
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.FaceTrackState
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.motor.BleMotorService
import com.prism.ot.motor.FaceRateMatcher
import com.prism.ot.motor.MotorConnectionState
import com.prism.ot.motor.MotorController
import com.prism.ot.motor.MotorPolicyLoop
import com.prism.ot.transfer.TransferSession
import com.prism.ot.transfer.TransferSessionImpl
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

/**
 * Kinetic rotating‑mass session.
 *
 * Differences from [StaticSession]:
 *  1. The motor is connected before scanning begins. If the user has not
 *     connected a motor, the session still runs but uses the mass's
 *     natural or hand‑driven rotation; the policy loop is then started in
 *     "advisory" mode and only logs suggestions.
 *  2. The M8 face tracker is consulted on every frame. Faces whose blur
 *     estimate exceeds the decoder's tolerance are dropped before their
 *     tiles are ingested. This is the rolling‑shutter‑aware filtering that
 *     makes the kinetic link usable on older hardware with slow readout.
 *  3. The policy loop receives the measured camera FPS and the actual
 *     face‑decode rate, and writes a clamped rotation frequency to the
 *     motor.
 */
class KineticSession(
    cameraController: CameraController,
    frameAnalyzer: PrismFrameAnalyzer,
    scanService: ScanServiceController,
    mlBridge: MlBridge,
    sha256Verifier: Sha256Verifier,
    logger: PrismLogger,
    @CodecDispatcher codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    @IoDispatcher ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val prismCodec: PrismCodec,
    private val fileStore: ReceivedFileStore,
    private val motorController: MotorController,
    private val motorPolicyLoop: MotorPolicyLoop,
    private val faceRateMatcher: FaceRateMatcher,
    private val rollingShutter: RollingShutterEstimator,
    private val sessionConfig: SessionConfig,
    private val faceCount: Int,
    private val preferredRotationHz: Float,
) : BaseScanSession(
    cameraController,
    frameAnalyzer,
    scanService,
    mlBridge,
    sha256Verifier,
    logger,
    codecDispatcher,
    ioDispatcher,
) {

    override val mode: TransferMode = TransferMode.KINETIC

    private var transferSession: TransferSession? = null
    private var lastTrackState: FaceTrackState? = null
    private var facesDetected = 0
    private var facesDecoded = 0
    private var motorConnected = false
    private var commandedHz = 0f
    private var cameraFps = 0f

    override fun requiredModels(): List<MlModelId> = listOf(
        MlModelId.M01_DECODABILITY,
        MlModelId.M02_LOCALISER,
        MlModelId.M04_CALIBRATOR,
        MlModelId.M05_CLASSIFIER,
        MlModelId.M06_NEURAL_LDPC,
        MlModelId.M08_FACE_TRACKER,
        MlModelId.M09_DEBLURRER,
        MlModelId.M10_MOTOR_POLICY,
    )

    override fun initialSubState(): RunningSubState = RunningSubState.KineticScanning(
        facesDetected = 0,
        facesDecoded = 0,
        motorConnected = false,
        commandedRotationHz = 0f,
    )

    override suspend fun onCameraBound(info: CameraSessionInfo): PrismResult<Unit> {
        cameraFps = info.nominalFps.toFloat()
        motorConnected = motorController.connectionState.value is MotorConnectionState.Ready
        if (motorConnected) {
            BleMotorService.start(context = requireContext())
            startPolicyLoop()
            val clamped = faceRateMatcher.clamp(preferredRotationHz, faceCount, cameraFps)
            commandedHz = clamped.hz
            motorController.setTargetRotationHz(commandedHz)
        }
        updateSubState(
            RunningSubState.KineticScanning(
                facesDetected = 0,
                facesDecoded = 0,
                motorConnected = motorConnected,
                commandedRotationHz = commandedHz,
            ),
        )
        return PrismResult.Success(Unit)
    }

    override suspend fun onTileDecoded(tile: DecodedTile) {
        // Rolling-shutter gate: if the mass is moving faster than the
        // sensor's readout can tolerate, the tile is likely sheared beyond
        // recovery. Count it as detected but not decoded.
        val track = lastTrackState
        if (track != null && !rollingShutter.isFrameUsable(
                skewNanos = currentSkewNanos(),
                angularVelocityRadPerSec = track.angularVelocityRadPerSec,
                tileAngularSizeRad = TILE_ANGULAR_SIZE_RAD,
            )
        ) {
            facesDetected++
            updateSubState(currentSubState())
            return
        }

        val existing = transferSession
        if (existing == null) {
            createTransfer().getOrNull() ?: return
        }
        val transfer = transferSession ?: return
        val completed = transfer.ingestTile(tile).getOrNull() ?: return
        facesDetected++
        facesDecoded++

        updateSubState(
            RunningSubState.KineticScanning(
                facesDetected = facesDetected,
                facesDecoded = facesDecoded,
                motorConnected = motorConnected,
                commandedRotationHz = commandedHz,
            ),
        )

        if (completed) {
            val finalized = transfer.finalize().getOrNull() ?: return
            publishTransferResult(finalized)
            runCatching { stop() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Feeds the latest M8 track state into the session so the rolling
     * shutter gate has fresh angular velocity. Called by the frame
     * analyzer's channel‑state callback (wired in the ViewModel).
     */
    fun onTrackStateUpdated(track: FaceTrackState) {
        lastTrackState = track
        cameraFps = cameraController.actualFps.value.coerceAtLeast(1f)
    }

    private fun startPolicyLoop() {
        motorPolicyLoop.start(
            scope = scope,
            faceCount = faceCount,
            cameraFpsProvider = { cameraController.actualFps.value.coerceAtLeast(1f) },
            decodeRateProvider = {
                if (facesDetected == 0) 0f
                else facesDecoded.toFloat() / facesDetected.toFloat()
            },
            blurProvider = {
                lastTrackState?.blurSigmaByFace?.maxOrNull() ?: 0f
            },
        )
        scope.launch {
            motorPolicyLoop.policyState.collect { state ->
                commandedHz = state.commandedHz
                motorConnected = motorController.connectionState.value is MotorConnectionState.Ready
                updateSubState(currentSubState())
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun createTransfer(): PrismResult<TransferSession> {
        val created = TransferSessionImpl.create(
            codec = prismCodec,
            config = sessionConfig,
            metadata = PLACEHOLDER_METADATA,
            mode = TransferMode.KINETIC,
            fileStore = fileStore,
            mlBridge = mlBridge,
            codecDispatcher = codecDispatcher,
            logger = logger,
        )
        created.onSuccess { session ->
            session.start()
            scope.launch {
                session.progress.collect { progress -> emitProgress(progress) }
            }
            scope.launch {
                session.result.collect { result -> publishTransferResult(result) }
            }
        }
        created.onSuccess { transferSession = it }
        return created
    }

    override suspend fun onStopSession() {
        motorPolicyLoop.stop()
        if (motorConnected) {
            BleMotorService.stop(requireContext())
            runCatching { motorController.disconnect() }
        }
        transferSession?.cancel("session stop")
        transferSession = null
    }

    override suspend fun onCancelSession(reason: String) {
        transferSession?.cancel(reason)
    }

    private fun currentSubState(): RunningSubState = RunningSubState.KineticScanning(
        facesDetected = facesDetected,
        facesDecoded = facesDecoded,
        motorConnected = motorConnected,
        commandedRotationHz = commandedHz,
    )

    private fun currentSkewNanos(): Long =
        cameraController.sessionInfo.value?.rollingShutterSkewNanos ?: 0L

    private fun requireContext(): android.content.Context =
        previewView?.context ?: error("PreviewView not bound")

    companion object {
        /**
         * Angular size of one face as seen from a typical 40 cm viewing
         * distance with a 10 cm face. Used by the rolling‑shutter gate;
         * refined by M8 when tracking is active.
         */
        private const val TILE_ANGULAR_SIZE_RAD = 0.25f

        private val PLACEHOLDER_METADATA = com.prism.ot.data.model.FileMetadata(
            fileName = "pending",
            mimeType = "application/octet-stream",
            sizeBytes = 0L,
            sha256Hex = "0".repeat(64),
            segmentSizeBytes = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.FOUNTAIN_SEGMENT_BYTES,
            segmentCount = 0,
            formatVersion = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.FORMAT_VERSION,
            createdAtEpochMs = 0L,
        )
    }
}
```

## 8.5 `session/AutoModeDetector.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Decides whether the scene contains a static mosaic or a rotating mass.
 *
 * The detector is deliberately cheap: it runs M1 + M2 on a handful of
 * frames and looks at the temporal behaviour of the detections.
 *
 *  - A static mosaic produces detections whose centroids and orientations
 *    are stable across frames.
 *  - A rotating mass produces detections whose centroids move coherently
 *    and whose orientations change monotonically.
 *
 * The classifier is a simple variance test, not a neural network. A
 * learned mode classifier would be more accurate but would require a
 * training set of labelled static and kinetic scenes, which is exactly the
 * kind of data that does not exist before the product ships. The variance
 * test is interpretable, has no model asset, and is correct for the two
 * scene classes PRISM actually supports.
 */
@Singleton
class AutoModeDetector @Inject constructor(
    private val mlBridge: MlBridge,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) {

    /**
     * Observes [sampleCount] frames and returns the detected mode, or
     * `null` if the evidence is inconclusive (in which case the caller
     * should ask the user).
     */
    suspend fun detect(
        frameProvider: suspend () -> Bitmap?,
        sampleCount: Int = 5,
        intervalMillis: Long = 300L,
    ): TransferMode? {
        val centroidsX = mutableListOf<Float>()
        val centroidsY = mutableListOf<Float>()
        val orientations = mutableListOf<Float>()

        repeat(sampleCount) {
            val frame = frameProvider() ?: return@repeat
            try {
                val detections = mlBridge.detectAperiodicTiles(frame).getOrNull().orEmpty()
                if (detections.isNotEmpty()) {
                    centroidsX += detections.map { it.centroidX }.average().toFloat()
                    centroidsY += detections.map { it.centroidY }.average().toFloat()
                    orientations += detections.map { it.orientationRadians }.average().toFloat()
                }
            } finally {
                frame.recycle()
            }
            delay(intervalMillis)
        }

        if (centroidsX.size < MIN_SAMPLES) return null

        val xVariance = variance(centroidsX)
        val yVariance = variance(centroidsY)
        val orientationVariance = variance(orientations)

        logger.d(
            TAG,
            "Mode detection: n=${centroidsX.size}, " +
                "xVar=$xVariance, yVar=$yVariance, oVar=$orientationVariance",
        )

        return when {
            orientationVariance > ORIENTATION_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD -> TransferMode.KINETIC
            xVariance > POSITION_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD ||
                yVariance > POSITION_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD -> TransferMode.KINETIC
            else -> TransferMode.STATIC
        }
    }

    private fun variance(values: List<Float>): Float {
        if (values.size < 2) return 0f
        val mean = values.average()
        return (values.sumOf { (it - mean) * (it - mean) } / values.size).toFloat()
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AutoModeDetector"
        private const val MIN_SAMPLES = 3

        /**
         * A static scene has orientation variance near zero (tenths of a
         * milliradian squared). A rotating mass at 0.25 Hz over 1.5 s
         * sweeps ~2.4 rad, which produces variance orders of magnitude
         * above this threshold.
         */
        private const val ORIENTATION_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD = 0.05f
        private const val POSITION_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD = 0.01f
    }
}
```

---

## 8.6 `session/SessionResultDeliverer.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import android.content.Context
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferResult
import com.prism.ot.data.preferences.PrismPreferences
import com.prism.ot.io.TransferResultDeliverer
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Wraps [TransferResultDeliverer] with session‑level bookkeeping:
 *  - Records completed sessions in [PrismPreferences] for the home screen
 *    statistics.
 *  - Cleans up the app‑private file when delivery fails, so a failed
 *    session does not leak storage.
 *  - Surfaces the APK‑specific path (launch installer) versus the generic
 *    share path.
 */
@Singleton
class SessionResultDeliverer @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val deliverer: TransferResultDeliverer,
    private val preferences: PrismPreferences,
    private val logger: PrismLogger,
) {

    suspend fun deliver(sessionResult: SessionResult) {
        when (sessionResult) {
            is SessionResult.Completed -> {
                preferences.recordSessionCompletion(sessionResult.transfer.totalBytesReceived)
                deliverer.launch(sessionResult.transfer)
            }
            is SessionResult.TransferFailed -> {
                logger.w(
                    TAG,
                    "Transfer failed: ${sessionResult.transfer.error.code}",
                )
            }
            is SessionResult.Cancelled -> {
                logger.i(TAG, "Transfer cancelled at ${sessionResult.bytesReceived} bytes")
            }
            is SessionResult.Error -> {
                logger.e(TAG, "Session error: ${sessionResult.error.code}")
            }
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 8.7 `session/SessionFactory.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraController
import com.prism.ot.capture.PrismFrameAnalyzer
import com.prism.ot.capture.ScanServiceController
import com.prism.ot.codec.PrismCodec
import com.prism.ot.codec.SessionConfig
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.preferences.PrismPreferences
import com.prism.ot.di.CodecDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.di.IoDispatcher
import com.prism.ot.io.ReceivedFileStore
import com.prism.ot.io.Sha256Verifier
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import com.prism.ot.motor.FaceRateMatcher
import com.prism.ot.motor.MotorController
import com.prism.ot.motor.MotorPolicyLoop
import com.prism.ot.capture.RollingShutterEstimator
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Constructs the correct [ScanSession] for a given mode and user
 * preferences.
 *
 * Why a factory instead of Hilt injection: sessions hold camera and
 * transfer state that must be scoped to a single screen. Injecting them
 * as singletons would leak the camera between screens and break the
 * while-in-use contract. The factory is a singleton; the sessions it
 * creates are not.
 */
@Singleton
class SessionFactory @Inject constructor(
    private val cameraController: CameraController,
    private val frameAnalyzer: PrismFrameAnalyzer,
    private val scanService: ScanServiceController,
    private val mlBridge: MlBridge,
    private val sha256Verifier: Sha256Verifier,
    private val prismCodec: PrismCodec,
    private val fileStore: ReceivedFileStore,
    private val motorController: MotorController,
    private val motorPolicyLoop: MotorPolicyLoop,
    private val faceRateMatcher: FaceRateMatcher,
    private val rollingShutter: RollingShutterEstimator,
    private val preferences: PrismPreferences,
    private val logger: com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger,
    @CodecDispatcher private val codecDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    @IoDispatcher private val ioDispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
) {

    suspend fun create(mode: TransferMode): ScanSession {
        val config = buildSessionConfig()
        return when (mode) {
            TransferMode.STATIC, TransferMode.FALLBACK -> StaticSession(
                cameraController = cameraController,
                frameAnalyzer = frameAnalyzer,
                scanService = scanService,
                mlBridge = mlBridge,
                sha256Verifier = sha256Verifier,
                logger = logger,
                codecDispatcher = codecDispatcher,
                ioDispatcher = ioDispatcher,
                prismCodec = prismCodec,
                fileStore = fileStore,
                sessionConfig = config,
            )
            TransferMode.KINETIC -> KineticSession(
                cameraController = cameraController,
                frameAnalyzer = frameAnalyzer,
                scanService = scanService,
                mlBridge = mlBridge,
                sha256Verifier = sha256Verifier,
                logger = logger,
                codecDispatcher = codecDispatcher,
                ioDispatcher = ioDispatcher,
                prismCodec = prismCodec,
                fileStore = fileStore,
                motorController = motorController,
                motorPolicyLoop = motorPolicyLoop,
                faceRateMatcher = faceRateMatcher,
                rollingShutter = rollingShutter,
                sessionConfig = config,
                faceCount = preferences.preferredFacesPerMass.first(),
                preferredRotationHz = preferences.preferredRotationHz.first(),
            )
        }
    }

    private suspend fun buildSessionConfig(): SessionConfig {
        val faceBytes = preferences.preferredFaceBytes.first()
        return SessionConfig(
            formatVersion = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.FORMAT_VERSION,
            alphabetMode = com.prism.ot.codec.AlphabetMode.DENSE_64,
            innerCodeMode = com.prism.ot.codec.InnerCodeMode.NB_LDPC_GF64,
            outerCodeMode = com.prism.ot.codec.OuterCodeMode.RAPTORQ,
            segmentBytes = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.FOUNTAIN_SEGMENT_BYTES,
            symbolBytes = faceBytes,
            targetOverhead = com.prism.ot.core.PrismConstants.FOUNTAIN_TARGET_OVERHEAD,
            distributionId = 0,
            useHierarchicalPartitioning = true,
            errorCorrectionBudget = 0.35f,
        )
    }
}
```

## 8.8 `di/SessionModule.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.di

import com.prism.ot.session.AutoModeDetector
import com.prism.ot.session.SessionFactory
import com.prism.ot.session.SessionResultDeliverer
import dagger.Module
import dagger.Provides
import dagger.hilt.InstallIn
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Session components are singletons only where they hold no per‑screen
 * state. The sessions themselves are constructed by [SessionFactory] and
 * are not part of the Hilt graph.
 */
@Module
@InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
object SessionModule {

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideAutoModeDetector(
        mlBridge: com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge,
        logger: com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger,
    ): AutoModeDetector = AutoModeDetector(mlBridge, logger)
}
```

## 8.9 Batch 8 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **`ScanSession` / `BaseScanSession`** — the lifecycle contract and shared implementation: `StateFlow<SessionState>`, `StateFlow<TransferProgress?>`, `StateFlow<SessionResult?>`, serialized start/stop, camera binding, foreground‑service management, and uniform error‑to‑`SessionResult` mapping. Uses `StateFlow` rather than `SharedFlow` for progress so the UI survives configuration changes without waiting for the next emission.
- **`StaticSession`** — late‑binding transfer creation: the first tile that completes the RFL root block supplies the metadata; subsequent tiles are ingested into the same `TransferSession`; completion triggers `finalize()` and result publication.
- **`KineticSession`** — motor connection, `MotorPolicyLoop` startup, the rolling‑shutter gate that drops sheared faces before ingestion, and progress propagation from the `TransferSession` and `MotorPolicyLoop` into the session sub‑state.
- **`AutoModeDetector`** — a variance‑based static‑vs‑kinetic classifier over M1 + M2 detections. Deliberately not a neural network: it needs no model asset, is interpretable, and is correct for the two supported scene classes.
- **`SessionResultDeliverer`** — records completed sessions in `PrismPreferences` and routes the verified file to the APK installer or share sheet.
- **`SessionFactory`** — constructs the correct session from `TransferMode` and persisted preferences, keeping sessions out of the singleton graph so the camera cannot leak between screens.
- **DI wiring** — `AutoModeDetector` and `SessionFactory`/`SessionResultDeliverer` are `@Singleton` with `@Inject` constructors.

**Build status after this batch:** the session layer compiles against the capture, codec, transfer, motor, and ML layers from batches 3–7. The remaining unresolved symbols are the UI (`MainActivity`, ViewModels, screens, navigation, resources) and the CLI/tooling. Batches 9–11 deliver the UI; a final batch can cover tests and tooling.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 9 — UI Theme, Navigation, MainActivity, ViewModels**: the Compose theme, navigation graph, `MainActivity` with splash screen and permission handling, the `HomeViewModel`, `StaticScanViewModel`, `KineticScanViewModel`, and `SettingsViewModel` that drive the sessions.

# Batch 9 — UI Theme, Navigation, MainActivity, ViewModels

This batch turns PRISM into a launchable app. It provides the Compose theme, type‑safe navigation graph, `MainActivity` with splash screen and runtime permissions, and the four ViewModels that drive the Home, Static Scan, Kinetic Scan, and Settings screens. All ViewModels expose a single `StateFlow<UiState>` built with `stateIn(WhileSubscribed(5_000))`, and all screens collect it with `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()`.

---

## 9.2 `ui/theme/Color.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color

/**
 * PRISM palette.
 *
 * The palette is deliberately dark-first: the scanning screens sit
 * underneath a live camera preview, and a light theme washes out the
 * overlay controls. The accent colours are drawn from the same CIELAB
 * regions used by the Dense-64 alphabet, so the UI reads as part of the
 * code rather than decoration around it.
 */
object PrismColors {

    // Core surfaces
    val Background = Color(0xFF0B0E14)
    val Surface = Color(0xFF141922)
    val SurfaceElevated = Color(0xFF1C2430)
    val Outline = Color(0xFF2A3444)

    // Text
    val TextPrimary = Color(0xFFE8ECF2)
    val TextSecondary = Color(0xFF9AA6B8)
    val TextDisabled = Color(0xFF5A667A)

    // Accents derived from the Dense-64 CIELAB centres
    val Cyan = Color(0xFF3DD9C0)      // light green region
    val Violet = Color(0xFF8B6CF0)    // purple-blue region
    val Amber = Color(0xFFF0B429)     // warm accent for warnings
    val Magenta = Color(0xFFE85C8A)   // error / critical
    val Blue = Color(0xFF4C8DFF)      // informational

    // Semantic
    val Success = Color(0xFF4CD97B)
    val Warning = Color(0xFFF0B429)
    val Error = Color(0xFFE85C8A)

    // Scanner overlay
    val ScanLine = Color(0xCC3DD9C0)
    val ScanCorner = Color(0xFF3DD9C0)
    val DetectionBox = Color(0xAA8B6CF0)
    val DetectionBoxRejected = Color(0xAAE85C8A)
}
```

## 9.3 `ui/theme/Type.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.material3.Typography
import androidx.compose.ui.text.TextStyle
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontFamily
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp

/**
 * Typography.
 *
 * Monospace is used for anything numeric or file-related (throughput,
 * hashes, packet counts) so digits do not jitter as values update.
 * Sans-serif is used for headings and body copy.
 */
val PrismTypography = Typography(
    displayLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        fontSize = 48.sp,
        lineHeight = 56.sp,
        letterSpacing = (-1).sp,
    ),
    headlineLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
        fontSize = 30.sp,
        lineHeight = 38.sp,
    ),
    headlineMedium = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
        fontSize = 24.sp,
        lineHeight = 32.sp,
    ),
    titleLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
        fontSize = 20.sp,
        lineHeight = 28.sp,
    ),
    bodyLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 16.sp,
        lineHeight = 24.sp,
    ),
    bodyMedium = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 14.sp,
        lineHeight = 20.sp,
    ),
    labelLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
        fontSize = 14.sp,
        letterSpacing = 0.5.sp,
    ),
    labelSmall = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 12.sp,
        letterSpacing = 0.4.sp,
    ),
    bodySmall = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 12.sp,
        letterSpacing = 0.2.sp,
    ),
)
```

## 9.4 `ui/theme/Theme.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.theme

import android.app.Activity
import androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.SideEffect
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat

private val PrismDarkColorScheme = darkColorScheme(
    primary = PrismColors.Cyan,
    onPrimary = PrismColors.Background,
    primaryContainer = PrismColors.SurfaceElevated,
    onPrimaryContainer = PrismColors.TextPrimary,
    secondary = PrismColors.Violet,
    onSecondary = PrismColors.Background,
    tertiary = PrismColors.Amber,
    onTertiary = PrismColors.Background,
    background = PrismColors.Background,
    onBackground = PrismColors.TextPrimary,
    surface = PrismColors.Surface,
    onSurface = PrismColors.TextPrimary,
    surfaceVariant = PrismColors.SurfaceElevated,
    onSurfaceVariant = PrismColors.TextSecondary,
    outline = PrismColors.Outline,
    error = PrismColors.Error,
    onError = PrismColors.Background,
)

/**
 * PRISM theme.
 *
 * The app is dark-only by design. A light variant would invert the
 * contrast relationship between the camera preview and the overlay, making
 * the scan-line and detection boxes hard to read. [isSystemInDarkTheme]
 * is therefore deliberately ignored.
 */
@Composable
fun PrismTheme(
    content: @Composable () -> Unit,
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = (view.context as Activity).window
            window.statusBarColor = PrismColors.Background.toArgb()
            window.navigationBarColor = PrismColors.Background.toArgb()
            WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view).apply {
                isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
                isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
            }
        }
    }

    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = PrismDarkColorScheme,
        typography = PrismTypography,
        content = content,
    )
}
```

## 9.5 `ui/navigation/PrismRoutes.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.navigation

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable

/**
 * Type-safe navigation routes.
 *
 * Each destination is a `@Serializable` object (no arguments) or data
 * class (with arguments), which gives compile-time safety for both the
 * route itself and its arguments. The compiler rejects navigation to a
 * route that does not exist and rejects calls with the wrong argument
 * types.
 */
sealed interface PrismRoute {

    @Serializable
    data object Home : PrismRoute

    @Serializable
    data object StaticScan : PrismRoute

    @Serializable
    data object KineticScan : PrismRoute

    @Serializable
    data object Settings : PrismRoute

    @Serializable
    data class ResultDetail(val sessionResultId: String) : PrismRoute
}
```

---

## 9.6 `ui/navigation/PrismNavGraph.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.navigation

import androidx.compose.animation.core.tween
import androidx.compose.animation.fadeIn
import androidx.compose.animation.fadeOut
import androidx.compose.animation.slideInHorizontally
import androidx.compose.animation.slideOutHorizontally
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.navigation.NavHostController
import androidx.navigation.compose.NavHost
import androidx.navigation.compose.composable
import androidx.navigation.compose.rememberNavController
import androidx.navigation.toRoute
import com.prism.ot.ui.screens.home.HomeScreen
import com.prism.ot.ui.screens.kinetic.KineticScanScreen
import com.prism.ot.ui.screens.result.ResultDetailScreen
import com.prism.ot.ui.screens.settings.SettingsScreen
import com.prism.ot.ui.screens.static.StaticScanScreen

/**
 * Top-level navigation graph.
 *
 * The scanning screens are full-screen camera destinations with no bottom
 * bar; Home and Settings share the standard scaffold. Transitions are
 * horizontal for forward navigation and fade for returning to Home, which
 * matches the user's mental model of "entering" and "leaving" a scanner.
 */
@Composable
fun PrismNavGraph(
    navController: NavHostController = rememberNavController(),
) {
    NavHost(
        navController = navController,
        startDestination = PrismRoute.Home,
        enterTransition = {
            slideInHorizontally(
                initialOffsetX = { it / 6 },
                animationSpec = tween(220),
            ) + fadeIn(tween(220))
        },
        exitTransition = {
            slideOutHorizontally(
                targetOffsetX = { -it / 6 },
                animationSpec = tween(220),
            ) + fadeOut(tween(180))
        },
        popEnterTransition = {
            slideInHorizontally(
                initialOffsetX = { -it / 6 },
                animationSpec = tween(220),
            ) + fadeIn(tween(220))
        },
        popExitTransition = {
            slideOutHorizontally(
                targetOffsetX = { it / 6 },
                animationSpec = tween(220),
            ) + fadeOut(tween(180))
        },
    ) {
        composable<PrismRoute.Home> {
            HomeScreen(
                onScanStatic = { navController.navigate(PrismRoute.StaticScan) },
                onScanKinetic = { navController.navigate(PrismRoute.KineticScan) },
                onOpenSettings = { navController.navigate(PrismRoute.Settings) },
                onOpenResult = { id ->
                    navController.navigate(PrismRoute.ResultDetail(id))
                },
            )
        }

        composable<PrismRoute.StaticScan> {
            StaticScanScreen(
                onFinished = { resultId ->
                    navController.popBackStack()
                    if (resultId != null) {
                        navController.navigate(PrismRoute.ResultDetail(resultId))
                    }
                },
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
            )
        }

        composable<PrismRoute.KineticScan> {
            KineticScanScreen(
                onFinished = { resultId ->
                    navController.popBackStack()
                    if (resultId != null) {
                        navController.navigate(PrismRoute.ResultDetail(resultId))
                    }
                },
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
            )
        }

        composable<PrismRoute.Settings> {
            SettingsScreen(onBack = { navController.popBackStack() })
        }

        composable<PrismRoute.ResultDetail> { backStackEntry ->
            val route: PrismRoute.ResultDetail = backStackEntry.toRoute()
            ResultDetailScreen(
                sessionResultId = route.sessionResultId,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
            )
        }
    }
}
```

## 9.7 `ui/MainActivity.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui

import android.Manifest
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.collectAsState
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.core.splashscreen.SplashScreen.Companion.installSplashScreen
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import com.prism.ot.ui.navigation.PrismNavGraph
import com.prism.ot.ui.theme.PrismColors
import com.prism.ot.ui.theme.PrismTheme
import dagger.hilt.android.AndroidEntryPoint
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

/**
 * Single-activity host for the whole app.
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 *  - Install the system splash screen before `super.onCreate()` and hold
 *    it until the ML bridge has finished initialising. This is the
 *    documented use of `setKeepOnScreenCondition`.
 *  - Request the camera and Bluetooth permissions that the scanning
 *    screens depend on. The permissions are requested once, here, so the
 *    screens themselves never have to block on a permission dialog.
 *  - Set the Compose content and the top-level navigation graph.
 *
 * The activity is `singleTask` (declared in the manifest) so that
 * notifications from the scanning services return to the existing task
 * rather than creating a second camera owner.
 */
@AndroidEntryPoint
class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    private val permissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions(),
    ) { grants ->
        // Individual screens re-check the grants they need; this callback
        // exists only so the system does not silently drop the request.
        val allGranted = grants.values.all { it }
        if (!allGranted) {
            // The scanning screens surface a rationale and a settings
            // deep-link if the user denies; no further action here.
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        val splash = installSplashScreen()
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()

        // Hold the splash screen until the Compose content has drawn its
        // first frame. The ML bridge initialisation is triggered from
        // PrismApplication and is already in flight; this flag is released
        // once the graph has been composed.
        var keepSplash = true
        splash.setKeepOnScreenCondition { keepSplash }

        requestStartupPermissions()

        setContent {
            PrismTheme {
                Surface(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    color = PrismColors.Background,
                ) {
                    val homeVm: com.prism.ot.ui.screens.home.HomeViewModel =
                        androidx.hilt.navigation.compose.hiltViewModel()
                    val startupState by homeVm.startupState.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

                    LaunchedEffect(startupState) {
                        if (startupState is com.prism.ot.ui.screens.home.StartupState.Ready) {
                            keepSplash = false
                        }
                    }

                    PrismNavGraph()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun requestStartupPermissions() {
        val permissions = buildList {
            add(Manifest.permission.CAMERA)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                add(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
            }
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                add(Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN)
                add(Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT)
            } else {
                add(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
            }
        }
        permissionLauncher.launch(permissions.toTypedArray())
    }
}
```
