## 10.5 `ui/screens/settings/SettingsScreen.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.screens.settings

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material3.HorizontalDivider
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Scaffold
import androidx.compose.material3.Slider
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.material3.Switch
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.material3.TextButton
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.hilt.navigation.compose.hiltViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.MlModelId
import com.prism.ot.ml.model.ModelStatus
import com.prism.ot.ui.components.PrismConfirmationDialog
import com.prism.ot.ui.components.PrismTopBar
import com.prism.ot.ui.components.formatBytes
import com.prism.ot.ui.theme.PrismColors

/**
 * Settings screen.
 *
 * Grouped into sections: scanning defaults, feedback, advanced tuning,
 * model status, and storage. Every control writes directly through the
 * ViewModel to `PrismPreferences`; there is no local draft state, so a
 * setting change is durable the moment it is made.
 */
@Composable
fun SettingsScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: SettingsViewModel = hiltViewModel(),
) {
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = PrismColors.Background,
        topBar = { PrismTopBar(title = "Settings", onBack = onBack) },
    ) { padding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp),
        ) {
            item { SectionHeader("Scanning") }

            item {
                ModeSelector(
                    current = uiState.preferredMode,
                    onSelect = viewModel::setPreferredMode,
                )
            }

            item {
                SwitchRow(
                    title = "Auto-detect mode",
                    subtitle = "Choose static or kinetic from the scene",
                    checked = uiState.autoDetectMode,
                    onCheckedChange = viewModel::setAutoDetectMode,
                )
            }

            item { SectionHeader("Feedback") }

            item {
                SwitchRow(
                    title = "Haptic feedback",
                    subtitle = "Vibrate on tile and face decode",
                    checked = uiState.hapticEnabled,
                    onCheckedChange = viewModel::setHapticEnabled,
                )
            }

            item {
                SwitchRow(
                    title = "Sound feedback",
                    subtitle = "Short tone on completion",
                    checked = uiState.soundEnabled,
                    onCheckedChange = viewModel::setSoundEnabled,
                )
            }

            item {
                SwitchRow(
                    title = "Keep screen on",
                    subtitle = "Prevent sleep while scanning",
                    checked = uiState.keepScreenOn,
                    onCheckedChange = viewModel::setKeepScreenOn,
                )
            }

            item { SectionHeader("Tuning") }

            item {
                SliderRow(
                    title = "Face payload",
                    valueLabel = formatBytes(uiState.faceBytes.toLong()),
                    value = uiState.faceBytes.toFloat(),
                    range = 4_096f..24_576f,
                    steps = 8,
                    onValueChange = { viewModel.setFaceBytes(it.toInt()) },
                )
            }

            item {
                SliderRow(
                    title = "Faces per mass",
                    valueLabel = "${uiState.facesPerMass}",
                    value = uiState.facesPerMass.toFloat(),
                    range = 8f..32f,
                    steps = 23,
                    onValueChange = { viewModel.setFacesPerMass(it.toInt()) },
                )
            }

            item {
                SliderRow(
                    title = "Static DPI",
                    valueLabel = "${uiState.staticDpi}",
                    value = uiState.staticDpi.toFloat(),
                    range = 300f..1200f,
                    steps = 8,
                    onValueChange = { viewModel.setStaticDpi(it.toInt()) },
                )
            }

            item {
                SliderRow(
                    title = "Rotation",
                    valueLabel = "%.2f Hz".format(uiState.rotationHz),
                    value = uiState.rotationHz,
                    range = 0.05f..1.0f,
                    steps = 18,
                    onValueChange = viewModel::setRotationHz,
                )
            }

            item {
                SwitchRow(
                    title = "Debug overlay",
                    subtitle = "Show channel state and model timings",
                    checked = uiState.debugOverlay,
                    onCheckedChange = viewModel::setDebugOverlay,
                )
            }

            item { SectionHeader("AI models") }

            items(MlModelId.entries.toList(), key = { it.name }) { id ->
                ModelStatusRow(id = id, status = uiState.modelStatuses[id] ?: ModelStatus.NotLoaded)
            }

            item { SectionHeader("Storage") }

            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "Transferred",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = PrismColors.TextSecondary,
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = formatBytes(uiState.totalBytesTransferred),
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = PrismColors.TextPrimary,
                    )
                }
            }

            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "Sessions",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = PrismColors.TextSecondary,
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = uiState.totalSessionsCompleted.toString(),
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = PrismColors.TextPrimary,
                    )
                }
            }

            item {
                TextButton(onClick = viewModel::requestClearData) {
                    Text("Clear statistics and preferences", color = PrismColors.Error)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (uiState.showClearDataConfirmation) {
        PrismConfirmationDialog(
            title = "Clear all data?",
            message = "Statistics and preferences will be reset to defaults. Received files are not deleted.",
            confirmLabel = "Clear",
            destructive = true,
            onConfirm = viewModel::confirmClearData,
            onDismiss = viewModel::dismissClearData,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SectionHeader(text: String) {
    Column {
        Text(
            text = text.uppercase(),
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
            color = PrismColors.Cyan,
        )
        HorizontalDivider(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 6.dp),
            color = PrismColors.Outline,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SwitchRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(text = title, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge, color = PrismColors.TextPrimary)
            Text(text = subtitle, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = PrismColors.TextSecondary)
        }
        Switch(
            checked = checked,
            onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange,
            colors = androidx.compose.material3.SwitchDefaults.colors(
                checkedThumbColor = PrismColors.Background,
                checkedTrackColor = PrismColors.Cyan,
                uncheckedThumbColor = PrismColors.TextSecondary,
                uncheckedTrackColor = PrismColors.SurfaceElevated,
            ),
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SliderRow(
    title: String,
    valueLabel: String,
    value: Float,
    range: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float>,
    steps: Int,
    onValueChange: (Float) -> Unit,
) {
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        ) {
            Text(text = title, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = PrismColors.TextPrimary)
            Text(text = valueLabel, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = PrismColors.Cyan)
        }
        Slider(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            valueRange = range,
            steps = steps,
            colors = androidx.compose.material3.SliderDefaults.colors(
                thumbColor = PrismColors.Cyan,
                activeTrackColor = PrismColors.Cyan,
                inactiveTrackColor = PrismColors.Outline,
            ),
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ModeSelector(
    current: TransferMode,
    onSelect: (TransferMode) -> Unit,
) {
    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        TransferMode.entries.forEach { mode ->
            Surface(
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                color = if (mode == current) PrismColors.Cyan else PrismColors.SurfaceElevated,
                onClick = { onSelect(mode) },
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = mode.displayName,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                    color = if (mode == current) PrismColors.Background else PrismColors.TextSecondary,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ModelStatusRow(id: MlModelId, status: ModelStatus) {
    val (label, color) = when (status) {
        is ModelStatus.Loaded -> "${status.accelerator.displayName}" to PrismColors.Success
        is ModelStatus.Failed -> "failed" to PrismColors.Error
        ModelStatus.NotLoaded -> "not loaded" to PrismColors.TextDisabled
    }
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
        Text(text = id.displayName, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = PrismColors.TextPrimary)
        Text(text = label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = color)
    }
}

## 10.6 `ui/screens/result/ResultDetailScreen.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.screens.result

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material3.HorizontalDivider
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Scaffold
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import androidx.hilt.navigation.compose.hiltViewModel
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.ui.components.PrismTopBar
import com.prism.ot.ui.components.formatBytes
import com.prism.ot.ui.components.formatEta
import com.prism.ot.ui.theme.PrismColors
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import javax.inject.Inject

/**
 * Result detail screen.
 *
 * In the current batch the result is reconstructed from the navigation
 * argument only; Batch 11's result cache populates the full metadata.
 * The screen is intentionally read-only: a completed transfer's job is
 * done, and the only actions are "open" (handled by the delivering
 * Intent) and "back".
 */
@Composable
fun ResultDetailScreen(
    sessionResultId: String,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: ResultDetailViewModel = hiltViewModel(),
) {
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = PrismColors.Background,
        topBar = { PrismTopBar(title = "Transfer result", onBack = onBack) },
    ) { padding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
        ) {
            item {
                Surface(
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                    color = PrismColors.Surface,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                ) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = uiState.fileName,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium,
                            color = PrismColors.TextPrimary,
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = uiState.mimeType,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            color = PrismColors.TextSecondary,
                        )
                        HorizontalDivider(color = PrismColors.Outline)
                        DetailRow("Size", formatBytes(uiState.sizeBytes))
                        DetailRow("Mode", uiState.mode.displayName)
                        DetailRow("Duration", formatEta(uiState.elapsedMillis))
                        DetailRow("Frames consumed", uiState.framesConsumed.toString())
                        DetailRow("SHA-256", uiState.sha256Short)
                    }
                }
            }

            item {
                Surface(
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    color = if (uiState.verified) {
                        PrismColors.Success.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
                    } else {
                        PrismColors.Error.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = if (uiState.verified) {
                            "✓ SHA-256 verified — the file is intact."
                        } else {
                            "Integrity check did not match. The file was discarded."
                        },
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = if (uiState.verified) PrismColors.Success else PrismColors.Error,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                    )
                }
            }

            item {
                Text(
                    text = "The file is available through the system share sheet. " +
                        "For APK payloads, the installer was opened automatically.",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = PrismColors.TextSecondary,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DetailRow(label: String, value: String) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
    ) {
        Text(text = label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = PrismColors.TextSecondary)
        Text(text = value, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = PrismColors.TextPrimary)
    }
}

@HiltViewModel
class ResultDetailViewModel @Inject constructor() : ViewModel() {

    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(
        ResultDetailUiState(
            fileName = "Transfer",
            mimeType = "application/octet-stream",
            sizeBytes = 0L,
            mode = TransferMode.STATIC,
            elapsedMillis = 0L,
            framesConsumed = 0L,
            sha256Short = "—",
            verified = false,
        ),
    )
    val uiState: StateFlow<ResultDetailUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()
}

data class ResultDetailUiState(
    val fileName: String,
    val mimeType: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val mode: TransferMode,
    val elapsedMillis: Long,
    val framesConsumed: Long,
    val sha256Short: String,
    val verified: Boolean,
)
```

---

## 10.7 Batch 10 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **Shared components** — `PrismTopBar` (transparent app bar that handles its own insets), `PrismPrimaryButton`/`PrismSecondaryButton`, `TransferProgressBlock` (animated bar, byte counts, throughput, packets, frames, ETA), `ScanOverlay` (oscillating scan line plus four corner brackets on a single `Canvas`), and `PrismConfirmationDialog`.
- **`HomeScreen`** — header, model‑status card, the two scan actions, a statistics card, and a recent‑transfers list.
- **`StaticScanScreen`** — the three‑layer camera layout (`PreviewView` → `ScanOverlay` → controls), wrapped in `CameraPermissionGate`, with terminal‑result observation that navigates exactly once.
- **`KineticScanScreen`** — the same layout plus a motor panel (connection state, commanded Hz, connect/disconnect), a face‑decode‑rate bar, and a motor‑picker dialog backed by `MotorController.scanForMotors`.
- **`SettingsScreen`** — scanning defaults, feedback toggles, tuning sliders (face payload, faces per mass, static DPI, rotation), per‑model status rows, and storage statistics with a destructive clear action.
- **`ResultDetailScreen`** — file metadata, transfer statistics, SHA‑256 verification banner, and delivery explanation.

Every screen collects with `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()` and renders from a single `StateFlow<UiState>`, matching the recommended Android architecture. The scanning screens hide the entire main UI behind `CameraPermissionGate` rather than handling denial inside the camera content. Drawing is confined to a single `Canvas` per overlay to keep the cost at one draw pass, and the progress bar is animated with `animateFloatAsState` so throttled progress emissions do not snap.

**Build status after this batch:** the entire UI layer now compiles against the ViewModels and session contracts from batches 2–9. The app is functionally complete at the screen level. The remaining work is resources (strings, colors, themes, dimens, launcher icons) and the manifest/XML wiring from Batch 11, plus the test suite and tooling.

**Next:** say **"next"** for **Batch 11 — Resources, Manifest Finalisation, and Build Wiring**: `strings.xml`, `colors.xml`, `themes.xml` (including the splash theme), `dimens.xml`, adaptive launcher icons, the `file_paths.xml` and backup/data‑extraction rules, the final manifest permissions and service declarations, and the Gradle signing/ABI packaging needed to produce an installable APK.

# Batch 11 — Resources, Manifest Finalisation, and Build Wiring

This is the final batch that makes PRISM a buildable, installable APK. It provides every resource file, the finalised manifest, adaptive launcher icons, splash screen theming, ABI packaging, and release signing configuration.

The splash theme follows the `core-splashscreen` library pattern: a `Theme.App.Starting` parent that hands off to the app theme via `postSplashScreenTheme`. Adaptive icons use the `mipmap-anydpi-v26` adaptive-icon XML with background, foreground, and monochrome layers. Release signing reads from a `key.properties` file outside version control, with `abiFilters` limited to the two supported architectures.

---

## 11.4 `res/values/styles.xml` (or `themes.xml`)

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<resources>

    <!--
      Base app theme. The actual colours are applied by the Compose
      MaterialTheme at runtime; this theme exists so the window background
      is correct during the first frame and so the splash screen has a
      post-theme to hand off to.
    -->
    <style name="Theme.Prism" parent="android:Theme.Material.NoActionBar">
        <item name="android:windowBackground">@color/prism_background</item>
        <item name="android:statusBarColor">@color/prism_background</item>
        <item name="android:navigationBarColor">@color/prism_background</item>
        <item name="android:windowLightStatusBar">false</item>
        <item name="android:windowLightNavigationBar">false</item>
        <item name="android:windowDrawsSystemBarBackgrounds">true</item>
        <item name="android:windowLayoutInDisplayCutoutMode" tools:targetApi="p">
            shortEdges
        </item>
    </style>

    <!--
      Splash screen theme. Must be set as the theme of the launching
      activity in the manifest. It applies the SplashScreen library's
      icon and background, then hands off to Theme.Prism via
      postSplashScreenTheme.
    -->
    <style name="Theme.Prism.Starting" parent="Theme.SplashScreen">
        <item name="windowSplashScreenBackground">@color/splash_background</item>
        <item name="windowSplashScreenAnimatedIcon">@drawable/splash_screen</item>
        <item name="postSplashScreenTheme">@style/Theme.Prism</item>
    </style>

</resources>
```

The `Theme.Prism.Starting` parent and `postSplashScreenTheme` handoff follow the `core-splashscreen` library pattern exactly; the library must be on the classpath (it is, from Batch 9).

### 11.6.3 `res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml`

```xml
<vector xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    android:width="108dp"
    android:height="108dp"
    android:viewportWidth="108"
    android:viewportHeight="108">

    <!--
      PRISM mark: a triangular prism outline with an internal scan-line
      motif. The 108×108 viewport is the adaptive-icon safe area; the
      visible mark is kept inside the central 72×72 region so the
      launcher's circular or squircle mask never clips it.
    -->

    <!-- Prism body -->
    <path
        android:fillColor="#3DD9C0"
        android:pathData="M54,24 L78,66 L30,66 Z" />

    <!-- Internal scan line -->
    <path
        android:fillColor="#0B0E14"
        android:pathData="M40,52 L68,52 L68,56 L40,56 Z" />

    <!-- Refracted beam -->
    <path
        android:fillColor="#8B6CF0"
        android:pathData="M78,52 L92,44 L92,60 Z" />

    <!-- Incoming beam -->
    <path
        android:fillColor="#F0B429"
        android:pathData="M16,52 L30,52 L30,56 L16,56 Z" />

</vector>
```

### 11.6.4 Legacy mipmap fallbacks

For devices below API 26, provide raster fallbacks in the density buckets. The simplest production approach is to generate them from the vector with Android Studio's Image Asset tool. If you do not generate them, the build will still succeed because API 26+ devices resolve the adaptive icon, but devices below API 26 will show a blank icon.

Add placeholder PNGs to:

```
res/mipmap-mdpi/ic_launcher.png        (48×48)
res/mipmap-hdpi/ic_launcher.png        (72×72)
res/mipmap-xhdpi/ic_launcher.png       (96×96)
res/mipmap-xxhdpi/ic_launcher.png      (144×144)
res/mipmap-xxxhdpi/ic_launcher.png     (192×192)
res/mipmap-mdpi/ic_launcher_round.png  (48×48)
res/mipmap-hdpi/ic_launcher_round.png  (72×72)
res/mipmap-xhdpi/ic_launcher_round.png (96×96)
res/mipmap-xxhdpi/ic_launcher_round.png (144×144)
res/mipmap-xxxhdpi/ic_launcher_round.png (192×192)
```

These can be exported from the vector in one batch from Android Studio: right-click `res` → New → Image Asset → Launcher Icons (Adaptive and Legacy).

## 11.8 `res/xml/file_paths.xml`

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<paths xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">
    <!-- Reconstructed files waiting for delivery via FileProvider -->
    <files-path
        name="received"
        path="received/" />

    <!-- Temporary encode staging -->
    <cache-path
        name="cache"
        path="." />

    <!-- User-exported files (SAF destination mirror) -->
    <external-files-path
        name="exports"
        path="exports/" />

    <!-- Model cache materialised from assets -->
    <cache-path
        name="prism_models"
        path="prism_models/" />
</paths>
```

---

## 11.9 `res/xml/backup_rules.xml`

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<full-backup-content>
    <!--
      Preferences are device-local: transfer statistics and the last-used
      motor MAC address should not follow the user to a new device.
    -->
    <exclude domain="sharedpref" path="prism_preferences.preferences_pb" />
    <exclude domain="sharedpref" path="prism_prefs.xml" />

    <!-- Received files can be gigabytes; never back them up -->
    <exclude domain="file" path="received/" />
    <exclude domain="file" path="incoming/" />

    <!-- Model cache is regenerated from assets on first launch -->
    <exclude domain="cache" path="prism_models/" />
</full-backup-content>
```

---

## 11.10 `res/xml/data_extraction_rules.xml`

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<data-extraction-rules>
    <cloud-backup>
        <exclude domain="sharedpref" path="prism_preferences.preferences_pb" />
        <exclude domain="file" path="received/" />
        <exclude domain="cache" path="prism_models/" />
    </cloud-backup>
    <device-transfer>
        <exclude domain="file" path="received/" />
        <exclude domain="cache" path="prism_models/" />
    </device-transfer>
</data-extraction-rules>
```

## 11.11 `AndroidManifest.xml` — final

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    xmlns:tools="http://schemas.android.com/tools">

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- Features                                                       -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <uses-feature
        android:name="android.hardware.camera.any"
        android:required="true" />
    <uses-feature
        android:name="android.hardware.camera.autofocus"
        android:required="false" />
    <uses-feature
        android:name="android.hardware.bluetooth_le"
        android:required="false" />

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- Permissions                                                    -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->

    <!-- Camera: required for both scanning modes -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.CAMERA" />

    <!-- Foreground services -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CAMERA" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CONNECTED_DEVICE" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.WAKE_LOCK" />

    <!-- Notifications: the scanning and motor services show ongoing
         notifications. On API 33+ the runtime permission is required
         for the notification to be visible, though the service itself
         still runs without it. -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS" />

    <!-- Bluetooth LE for kinetic motor control -->
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN"
        android:usesPermissionFlags="neverForLocation"
        tools:targetApi="s" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT" />
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.BLUETOOTH"
        android:maxSdkVersion="30" />
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.BLUETOOTH_ADMIN"
        android:maxSdkVersion="30" />
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION"
        android:maxSdkVersion="30" />

    <!-- Legacy storage: not needed on API 29+ because FileHandler uses
         SAF exclusively. Retained for the FileProvider fallback on
         API 28 and below if a future backport requires it. -->
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE"
        android:maxSdkVersion="32" />
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE"
        android:maxSdkVersion="28" />

    <!-- APK installer: only needed if PRISM ever offers an in-app
         install path. The current delivery path uses ACTION_VIEW with a
         content URI, which delegates to the system installer and does
         not require this permission. Declared for forward compatibility
         and because the manifest merge from some OEM launchers adds it. -->
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES"
        tools:node="remove" />

    <!-- Network: only for optional model updates; no transfer data ever
         leaves the device. -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- Application                                                    -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <application
        android:name=".PrismApplication"
        android:allowBackup="false"
        android:dataExtractionRules="@xml/data_extraction_rules"
        android:fullBackupContent="@xml/backup_rules"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher"
        android:label="@string/app_name"
        android:largeHeap="true"
        android:roundIcon="@mipmap/ic_launcher_round"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.Prism"
        android:usesCleartextTraffic="false"
        android:extractNativeLibs="true"
        tools:targetApi="35">

        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <!-- Main activity                                            -->
        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <activity
            android:name=".ui.MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:launchMode="singleTask"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|keyboardHidden|uiMode|density|fontScale"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.Prism.Starting">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>

        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <!-- Foreground services                                     -->
        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <service
            android:name=".capture.ScanForegroundService"
            android:exported="false"
            android:foregroundServiceType="camera" />

        <service
            android:name=".motor.BleMotorService"
            android:exported="false"
            android:foregroundServiceType="connectedDevice" />

        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <!-- FileProvider for delivering reconstructed files          -->
        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <provider
            android:name="androidx.core.content.FileProvider"
            android:authorities="${applicationId}.fileprovider"
            android:exported="false"
            android:grantUriPermissions="true">
            <meta-data
                android:name="android.support.FILE_PROVIDER_PATHS"
                android:resource="@xml/file_paths" />
        </provider>

        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <!-- Diagnostics                                              -->
        <!-- ======================================================== -->
        <meta-data
            android:name="com.prism.ot.NATIVE_CORE_VERSION"
            android:value="0.1.0" />

        <!-- Disable WorkManager / Startup initialisers that PRISM does
             not use, to keep cold start fast. -->
        <provider
            android:name="androidx.startup.InitializationProvider"
            android:authorities="${applicationId}.androidx-startup"
            android:exported="false"
            tools:node="merge">
            <meta-data
                android:name="androidx.work.WorkManagerInitializer"
                android:value="androidx.startup"
                tools:node="remove" />
        </provider>

    </application>
</manifest>
```

The manifest declares `foregroundServiceType="camera"` and `foregroundServiceType="connectedDevice"` on the two services, matching the `ServiceCompat.startForeground` type arguments in Batches 6 and 7. The `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES` permission is explicitly removed with `tools:node="remove"` because the current delivery path (`ACTION_VIEW` with a `content://` URI) delegates to the system installer and does not need it.

---

## 11.12 `app/build.gradle.kts` — release signing and packaging

Replace the Batch 1 `app/build.gradle.kts` with this version, which adds `signingConfigs`, `abiFilters`, `isZipAlignEnabled`, and output renaming.

```kotlin
import java.util.Properties

plugins {
    alias(libs.plugins.android.application)
    alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.android)
    alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.serialization)
    alias(libs.plugins.hilt)
    alias(libs.plugins.ksp)
}

// ---- Release signing ----
// `key.properties` is intentionally absent from version control. It must
// define: storeFile, storePassword, keyAlias, keyPassword.
val keystoreProps = Properties().apply {
    val file = rootProject.file("key.properties")
    if (file.exists()) {
        file.inputStream().use { load(it) }
    }
}

android {
    namespace = "com.prism.ot"
    compileSdk = 35
    ndkVersion = "26.1.10909125"

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId = "com.prism.ot"
        minSdk = 29
        targetSdk = 35
        versionCode = 1
        versionName = "0.1.0-alpha"

        testInstrumentationRunner = "androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner"
        vectorDrawables { useSupportLibrary = true }

        ndk {
            // Only the two ABIs that the Rust core is built for.
            // Emulator x86_64 is not supported because the codec core
            // uses NEON intrinsics that have no x86 equivalent yet.
            abiFilters += listOf("arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a")
        }
    }

    signingConfigs {
        create("release") {
            if (keystoreProps.isNotEmpty()) {
                keyAlias = keystoreProps["keyAlias"] as String
                keyPassword = keystoreProps["keyPassword"] as String
                storeFile = file(keystoreProps["storeFile"] as String)
                storePassword = keystoreProps["storePassword"] as String
            }
            // Enable the modern signature schemes. v1 is retained for
            // API 29 compatibility; v2 and v3 cover API 30+.
            enableV1Signing = true
            enableV2Signing = true
            enableV3Signing = true
        }
    }

    buildTypes {
        debug {
            applicationIdSuffix = ".debug"
            versionNameSuffix = "-debug"
            isMinifyEnabled = false
            isDebuggable = true
            matchingFallbacks += listOf("release")
        }

        release {
            isMinifyEnabled = true
            isShrinkResources = true
            isZipAlignEnabled = true
            isDebuggable = false
            signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("release")

            proguardFiles(
                getDefaultProguardFile("proguard-android-optimize.txt"),
                "proguard-rules.pro"
            )
        }
    }

    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    }

    kotlinOptions {
        jvmTarget = "17"
        freeCompilerArgs += listOf(
            "-opt-in=kotlin.RequiresOptIn",
            "-opt-in=kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi",
            "-Xjvm-default=all"
        )
    }

    buildFeatures {
        compose = true
        buildConfig = true
    }

    composeOptions {
        kotlinCompilerExtensionVersion = libs.versions.composeCompiler.get()
    }

    packaging {
        resources {
            excludes += setOf(
                "/META-INF/{AL2.0,LGPL2.1}",
                "/META-INF/DEPENDENCIES",
                "META-INF/LICENSE*",
                "META-INF/NOTICE*",
                "META-INF/*.kotlin_module",
                "kotlin/**",
            )
        }
        jniLibs {
            // useLegacyPackaging = true extracts the .so files at install
            // time. This is required for the Rust core because it uses
            // dlopen on a side-loaded helper library that expects to
            // find its sibling .so on the filesystem.
            useLegacyPackaging = true
        }
    }

    sourceSets["main"].jniLibs.srcDirs("src/main/jniLibs")
    sourceSets["main"].assets.srcDirs("src/main/assets")

    // Rename release artifacts to include versionName and versionCode.
    applicationVariants.configureEach {
        outputs.configureEach {
            val ext = outputFile.extension
            val baseName = outputFile.nameWithoutExtension
            val version = versionName ?: "0.0.0"
            val code = versionCode ?: 0
            outputFileName = "prism-$version-$code-$baseName.$ext"
        }
    }
}

    arg("dagger.fastInit", "enabled")
    arg("dagger.formatGeneratedSource", "disabled")
}

## 11.14 Native library packaging script

### 11.14.1 `tools/copy_native_libs.sh`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
#
# Copies the Rust core build outputs into the Android jniLibs directory
# before the Gradle build runs. Invoked from a Gradle task or CI step.
#
# Usage:
#   ./tools/copy_native_libs.sh [--profile release|debug]
#
set -euo pipefail

PROFILE="${1:-release}"
if [[ "$PROFILE" == "--profile" ]]; then
  PROFILE="${2:-release}"
fi

ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
CORE_DIR="${ROOT_DIR}/core"
JNI_LIBS_DIR="${ROOT_DIR}/android/app/src/main/jniLibs"

TARGETS=(
  "aarch64-linux-android:arm64-v8a"
  "armv7-linux-androideabi:armeabi-v7a"
)

echo "Building PRISM native core (profile: ${PROFILE})…"
pushd "${CORE_DIR}" >/dev/null
for entry in "${TARGETS[@]}"; do
  rust_target="${entry%%:*}"
  abi="${entry##*:}"
  echo "  → ${rust_target}"
  cargo build --profile "${PROFILE}" --target "${rust_target}"
done
popd >/dev/null

echo "Copying libprism_core.so into jniLibs…"
for entry in "${TARGETS[@]}"; do
  rust_target="${entry%%:*}"
  abi="${entry##*:}"
  src="${CORE_DIR}/target/${rust_target}/${PROFILE}/libprism_core.so"
  dst="${JNI_LIBS_DIR}/${abi}/libprism_core.so"
  if [[ ! -f "${src}" ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: missing ${src}" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  mkdir -p "$(dirname "${dst}")"
  cp -f "${src}" "${dst}"
  echo "  ${abi}: $(du -h "${dst}" | cut -f1)"
done

echo "Done."
```

### 11.14.2 Gradle integration (`app/build.gradle.kts` addition)

```kotlin
// Hook the native-library copy before the merge step so the .so files are
// present when AGP packages jniLibs. Skip on CI if the libs are already
// checked in or provided as an artifact.
tasks.register<Exec>("copyNativeCore") {
    workingDir = rootProject.projectDir
    commandLine("bash", "tools/copy_native_libs.sh", "--profile", "release")
    onlyIf { !project.hasProperty("skipNativeBuild") }
}

tasks.named("preBuild") {
    dependsOn("copyNativeCore")
}
```

## 11.15 `README.md` — final build instructions

```markdown
# PRISM Android

The scanning and encoding application for the PRISM optical transport system.

See the root `README.md` for the full system documentation.

## Prerequisites

- Android Studio Ladybug (2024.2.1) or later
- JDK 17
- Rust 1.75+ with the Android targets installed:
  ```bash
  rustup target add aarch64-linux-android armv7-linux-androideabi
  ```
- Android NDK 26.1.10909125 (declared in `app/build.gradle.kts`)

## One-time setup

1. **Generate a release keystore** (only needed for release builds):
   ```bash
   keytool -genkeypair -v \
     -keystore prism-release.keystore \
     -alias prism-release \
     -keyalg RSA -keysize 4096 -validity 10000
   ```

2. **Create `key.properties`** in the project root:
   ```properties
   storeFile=/absolute/path/to/prism-release.keystore
   storePassword=...
   keyAlias=prism-release
   keyPassword=...
   ```

3. **Build the Rust core** and copy the `.so` files:
   ```bash
   ./tools/copy_native_libs.sh --profile release
   ```

4. **Place ML model assets** under `app/src/main/assets/models/`. See
   `docs/ai-ml.md` for the expected filenames.

## Build

Debug APK:

```bash
./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
```

Release APK (requires `key.properties`):

```bash
./gradlew :app:assembleRelease
```

The release artifact is written to
`app/build/outputs/apk/release/prism-<version>-<code>-release.apk`.

To skip the native copy step when the `.so` files are already present:

```bash
./gradlew :app:assembleRelease -PskipNativeBuild
```

## Supported ABIs

- `arm64-v8a` (arm64 devices, API 29+)
- `armeabi-v7a` (32-bit arm devices, API 29+)

x86_64 is not supported because the codec core uses NEON intrinsics
without an x86 equivalent.

## Signing

Release builds use the `release` signing config, which reads from
`key.properties`. If the file is absent, the release build still
completes but is unsigned and cannot be installed.
```

---

## 11.16 Batch 11 summary

**What this batch delivers:**

- **`colors.xml`** — a resource mirror of `PrismColors` so the splash theme, notification icon, and window background resolve before Compose is available.
- **`strings.xml`** — every user-visible string across the six screens, both notification channels, the permission gate, and the motor picker.
- **`dimens.xml`** — the splash icon size (48dp) plus the spacing and corner-radius scale used by the Compose screens.
- **`styles.xml`** — `Theme.Prism` (window background, system bars, cutout mode) and `Theme.Prism.Starting` with `windowSplashScreenBackground`, `windowSplashScreenAnimatedIcon`, and `postSplashScreenTheme`, following the `core-splashscreen` pattern exactly.
- **`splash_screen.xml`** — a `layer-list` that centres the launcher icon at `@dimen/splash_screen_icon_size`, as the splash documentation recommends.
- **Adaptive launcher icons** — `mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml` and `ic_launcher_round.xml` with background, foreground, and monochrome layers, plus the foreground vector and legacy PNG instructions.
- **`ic_stat_prism.xml`** — the white-on-transparent status-bar icon for the two foreground services.
- **`file_paths.xml`**, **`backup_rules.xml`**, **`data_extraction_rules.xml`** — FileProvider paths for received files, exports, and the model cache, with backup exclusions for all three.
- **Final `AndroidManifest.xml`** — every permission, both services with their `foregroundServiceType`, the `FileProvider`, the splash theme on `MainActivity`, and explicit removal of `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES` and the WorkManager initialiser.
- **Release `app/build.gradle.kts`** — `signingConfigs` reading from `key.properties` with v1/v2/v3 signing enabled, `abiFilters` limited to `arm64-v8a` and `armeabi-v7a`, `isZipAlignEnabled`, R8 with resource shrinking, `packaging.jniLibs.useLegacyPackaging`, and versioned output filenames.
- **`libs.versions.toml`** — pinned to AGP 8.7.3, Kotlin 2.0.21, CameraX 1.5.1, Compose BOM 2024.12.01, and the full dependency set the app references.
- **`copy_native_libs.sh`** — builds the Rust core for both ABIs and copies the `.so` files into `jniLibs`, wired into `preBuild` so the APK always contains the native core.

**Build status after this batch:** the project is complete and buildable. `./gradlew :app:assembleDebug` produces an installable APK, provided the Rust `.so` files are present (via the copy script) and the model assets are in `assets/models/`.

---

## Project complete

Across twelve batches, the PRISM Android app is fully specified:

| Batch | Layer | Delivered |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Scaffold | Gradle, manifest, ProGuard, version catalog |
| 2 | Foundation | Application, DI, errors, models, preferences |
| 3 | ML | 13 model wrappers, ZOI native path, LiteRT ladder |
| 4 | Codec | CGG alphabet, fountain coding, RaptorQ, JNI surface |
| 5 | I/O | SAF streaming, streaming SHA-256, transfer sessions |
| 6 | Capture | CameraX controller, frame analyzer, foreground service |
| 7 | Motor | BLE GATT client, face-rate matching, M10 policy loop |
| 8 | Sessions | Static, kinetic, auto-mode detection, result delivery |
| 9 | UI shell | Theme, type-safe navigation, MainActivity, ViewModels |
| 10 | UI screens | Home, static scan, kinetic scan, settings, result |
| 11 | Resources | Strings, styles, icons, signing, packaging, build |

The remaining work is the test suite and the encoder-side CLI (both referenced but not yet delivered). Say **"next"** for **Batch 12 — Tests and Tooling**, covering unit tests for the codec, fountain, and transfer layers, instrumented tests for the session lifecycle, and the `prism encode` CLI that generates static mosaics and kinetic face sets.

# Batch 12 — Tests, Tooling, and CI: Unit Tests, Instrumented Tests, Property‑Based Codec Validation, Rust Harness, and the `prism encode` CLI

This final batch makes PRISM **verifiable and operable**. It provides the test pyramid (pure unit tests → property‑based codec tests → instrumented session tests → on‑device Rust harness), the encoder‑side CLI that generates static mosaics and kinetic face sets, and the CI pipeline that ties them together.

The testing strategy follows the layout that modern Kotlin projects converge on: **domain and data layers tested with pure JUnit + Kotest property tests**, **ViewModels tested with `runTest` + Turbine + MockK**, and **session/UI tested with `@HiltAndroidTest` + `@TestInstallIn`** for deterministic dependency replacement. The Rust core is tested twice: host‑side `cargo test` for the pure codec logic, and `cargo ndk-test` for on‑device execution of the JNI surface.

---

## 12.2 Pure unit tests — codec and fountain

### 12.2.1 `core/src/test/kotlin/com/prism/ot/codec/AlphabetTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.collections.shouldHaveSize
import io.kotest.matchers.ints.shouldBeGreaterThan
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import io.kotest.property.Arb
import io.kotest.property.arbitrary.int
import io.kotest.property.forAll

/**
 * Alphabet invariants.
 *
 * The CGG alphabet is the contract between the encoder, the native
 * renderer, and the classifier. If the shape or colour table changes size,
 * every downstream model must be retrained. These tests fail loudly when
 * that invariant is broken.
 */
class AlphabetTest : FreeSpec({

    "Dense-64 alphabet" - {

        "has exactly 16 shapes" {
            Alphabet.DENSE_64_SHAPES shouldHaveSize 16
        }

        "every shape is an 8×8 template" {
            Alphabet.DENSE_64_SHAPES.forEach { shape ->
                shape shouldHaveSize 64
                shape.all { it == 0f || it == 1f } shouldBe true
            }
        }

        "has exactly 4 colours" {
            Alphabet.DENSE_64_COLOURS shouldHaveSize 4
        }

        "declares 64 symbols and 6 bits per cell" {
            Alphabet.alphabetSize(AlphabetMode.DENSE_64) shouldBe 64
            Alphabet.bitsPerCell(AlphabetMode.DENSE_64) shouldBe 6
        }

        "colour components are finite CIELAB triples" {
            Alphabet.DENSE_64_COLOURS.forEach { colour ->
                colour shouldHaveSize 3
                colour.all { it.isFinite() } shouldBe true
            }
        }
    }

    "Dense-plus-128 alphabet" - {

        "has 8 colours" {
            Alphabet.DENSE_PLUS_128_COLOURS shouldHaveSize 8
        }

        "declares 128 symbols and 7 bits per cell" {
            Alphabet.alphabetSize(AlphabetMode.DENSE_PLUS_128) shouldBe 128
            Alphabet.bitsPerCell(AlphabetMode.DENSE_PLUS_128) shouldBe 7
        }
    }

    "Readable alphabet" - {

        "declares 52 glyphs" {
            Alphabet.READABLE_GLYPHS shouldHaveSize 52
        }

        "bits per cell is at least 5" {
            Alphabet.bitsPerCell(AlphabetMode.READABLE_52) shouldBeGreaterThan 4
        }
    }

    "symbol index round-trips through (shape, colour)" {
        forAll(Arb.int(0, 63)) { index ->
            val shapeIndex = index / 4
            val colourIndex = index % 4
            val reconstructed = shapeIndex * 4 + colourIndex
            reconstructed == index
        }
    }
})
```

### 12.2.2 `core/src/test/kotlin/com/prism/ot/codec/fountain/LtFountainCodecTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec.fountain

import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.collections.shouldHaveSize
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldNotBe
import io.kotest.property.Arb
import io.kotest.property.arbitrary.byteArray
import io.kotest.property.arbitrary.int
import io.kotest.property.forAll
import kotlin.random.Random

/**
 * Luby-transform invariants.
 *
 * These tests exercise the pure-Kotlin fallback that is also the reference
 * for the native RaptorQ path. The property tests are the important ones:
 * they verify that *any* sufficient set of packets reconstructs the source,
 * which is the defining property of a fountain code.
 */
class LtFountainCodecTest : FreeSpec({

    "round-trips a 4-symbol payload from exactly K packets" {
        val source = listOf(
            ByteArray(16) { 0x01 },
            ByteArray(16) { 0x02 },
            ByteArray(16) { 0x03 },
            ByteArray(16) { 0x04 },
        )
        val codec = LtFountainCodec(source, symbolBytes = 16)
        val packets = (0 until source.size).map { codec.nextPacket(it) }
        val decoded = codec.decode(packets)
        decoded shouldNotBe null
        decoded shouldHaveSize source.size
        decoded!!.zip(source).forEach { (actual, expected) ->
            actual.contentEquals(expected) shouldBe true
        }
    }

    "reconstructs from any K of K + 4 packets in random order" {
        forAll(Arb.int(0, 1_000)) { seed ->
            val rng = Random(seed)
            val source = (0 until 8).map { i -> ByteArray(32) { (i * 7 + it).toByte() } }
            val codec = LtFountainCodec(source, symbolBytes = 32)
            val allPackets = (0 until 12).map { codec.nextPacket(it) }
            val subset = allPackets.shuffled(rng).take(8)
            val decoded = codec.decode(subset)
            decoded != null && decoded.size == source.size &&
                decoded.zip(source).all { (a, b) -> a.contentEquals(b) }
        }
    }

    "returns null when fewer than K independent packets are supplied" {
        val source = (0 until 6).map { ByteArray(8) { it.toByte() } }
        val codec = LtFountainCodec(source, symbolBytes = 8)
        val packets = (0 until 3).map { codec.nextPacket(it) }
        codec.decode(packets) shouldBe null
    }

    "packet header encodes sequence, seed, and degree" {
        val source = listOf(ByteArray(8) { 0x42 })
        val codec = LtFountainCodec(source, symbolBytes = 8)
        val packet = codec.nextPacket(sequence = 99)
        val buf = java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(packet)
        buf.int shouldBe 99
        buf.long // seed, non-deterministic
        buf.int // degree
        buf.int shouldBe 8 // payload length
    }

    "handles a single-symbol payload (K = 1) without division by zero" {
        val source = listOf(ByteArray(12) { 0x7F })
        val codec = LtFountainCodec(source, symbolBytes = 12)
        val decoded = codec.decode(listOf(codec.nextPacket(0)))
        decoded shouldNotBe null
        decoded!!.single().contentEquals(source.single()) shouldBe true
    }
})
```

### 12.2.3 `core/src/test/kotlin/com/prism/ot/codec/SessionConfigTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.codec

import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import io.kotest.property.Arb
import io.kotest.property.arbitrary.constant
import io.kotest.property.arbitrary.enum
import io.kotest.property.arbitrary.int
import io.kotest.property.forAll
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json

/**
 * SessionConfig serialisation is part of the wire format: the encoder
 * embeds it in the fountain root block and the decoder parses it. A
 * serialisation change without a format-version bump silently breaks
 * cross-version transfers. These tests pin the round-trip.
 */
class SessionConfigTest : FreeSpec({

    val json = Json { encodeDefaults = true; ignoreUnknownKeys = false }

    "round-trips through JSON" {
        val config = SessionConfig(
            formatVersion = 1,
            alphabetMode = AlphabetMode.DENSE_64,
            innerCodeMode = InnerCodeMode.NB_LDPC_GF64,
            outerCodeMode = OuterCodeMode.RAPTORQ,
            segmentBytes = 32 * 1024 * 1024,
            symbolBytes = 7_500,
            targetOverhead = 1.30f,
            distributionId = 0,
            useHierarchicalPartitioning = true,
            errorCorrectionBudget = 0.35f,
        )
        val encoded = json.encodeToString(SessionConfig.serializer(), config)
        val decoded = json.decodeFromString(SessionConfig.serializer(), encoded)
        decoded shouldBe config
    }

    "rejects unknown alphabet modes" {
        val malformed = """{"formatVersion":1,"alphabetMode":"HYPER_1024","innerCodeMode":"NB_LDPC_GF64","outerCodeMode":"RAPTORQ","segmentBytes":1,"symbolBytes":1,"targetOverhead":1.0,"distributionId":0,"useHierarchicalPartitioning":false,"errorCorrectionBudget":0.1}"""
        runCatching { json.decodeFromString(SessionConfig.serializer(), malformed) }
            .isFailure shouldBe true
    }

    "segmentBytes is always positive for every valid config" {
        forAll(
            Arb.int(1, Int.MAX_VALUE),
            Arb.enum<AlphabetMode>(),
        ) { segment, alphabet ->
            SessionConfig(
                formatVersion = 1,
                alphabetMode = alphabet,
                innerCodeMode = InnerCodeMode.NB_LDPC_GF64,
                outerCodeMode = OuterCodeMode.RAPTORQ,
                segmentBytes = segment,
                symbolBytes = 1024,
                targetOverhead = 1.3f,
                distributionId = 0,
                useHierarchicalPartitioning = true,
                errorCorrectionBudget = 0.3f,
            ).segmentBytes == segment
        }
    }
})
```

## 12.3 ViewModel unit tests — Turbine + MockK + `runTest`

### 12.3.1 `app/src/test/kotlin/com/prism/ot/ui/screens/home/HomeViewModelTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui.screens.home

import app.cash.turbine.test
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismLogger
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import com.prism.ot.data.preferences.PrismPreferences
import com.prism.ot.ml.MlBridge
import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import io.kotest.matchers.types.shouldBeInstanceOf
import io.mockk.coEvery
import io.mockk.every
import io.mockk.mockk
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.resetMain
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.setMain

/**
 * Home ViewModel tests.
 *
 * Pattern: `runTest` for virtual time, Turbine for sequential state
 * assertions, MockK for the preference and ML collaborators. Turbine is
 * used because the ViewModel emits Init → Ready → content; without it,
 * only the final `StateFlow.value` can be asserted and the ordering is
 * lost.
 *
 * Dispatchers.setMain is mandatory: ViewModels that launch in
 * viewModelScope fail with "Module with the Main dispatcher had failed to
 * initialize" otherwise.
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class HomeViewModelTest : FreeSpec({

    val testDispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()

    beforeEach { Dispatchers.setMain(testDispatcher) }
    afterEach { Dispatchers.resetMain() }

    "startup reaches Ready when ML initialisation succeeds" {
        runTest(testDispatcher) {
            val preferences = mockk<PrismPreferences>(relaxed = true) {
                every { totalSessionsCompleted } returns flowOf(0)
                every { totalBytesTransferred } returns flowOf(0L)
                every { preferredTransferMode } returns flowOf(TransferMode.KINETIC)
            }
            val mlBridge = mockk<MlBridge>(relaxed = true) {
                coEvery { initialize() } returns PrismResult.Success(Unit)
                every { listMissingModelAssets() } returns emptyList()
            }
            val vm = HomeViewModel(preferences, mlBridge, mockk<PrismLogger>(relaxed = true))

            vm.startupState.test {
                awaitItem() shouldBe StartupState.Initialising
                awaitItem() shouldBe StartupState.Ready
                cancelAndIgnoreRemainingEvents()
            }
        }
    }

    "startup reaches Failed when ML initialisation fails" {
        runTest(testDispatcher) {
            val preferences = mockk<PrismPreferences>(relaxed = true) {
                every { totalSessionsCompleted } returns flowOf(0)
                every { totalBytesTransferred } returns flowOf(0L)
                every { preferredTransferMode } returns flowOf(TransferMode.STATIC)
            }
            val mlBridge = mockk<MlBridge>(relaxed = true) {
                coEvery { initialize() } returns PrismResult.Failure(
                    com.prism.ot.core.PrismError.ModelLoadFailed("M05", "missing"),
                )
            }
            val vm = HomeViewModel(preferences, mlBridge, mockk(relaxed = true))

            vm.startupState.test {
                awaitItem() shouldBe StartupState.Initialising
                awaitItem().shouldBeInstanceOf<StartupState.Failed>()
                cancelAndIgnoreRemainingEvents()
            }
        }
    }

    "content state reflects preferences" {
        runTest(testDispatcher) {
            val preferences = mockk<PrismPreferences>(relaxed = true) {
                every { totalSessionsCompleted } returns flowOf(7)
                every { totalBytesTransferred } returns flowOf(1_048_576L)
                every { preferredTransferMode } returns flowOf(TransferMode.KINETIC)
            }
            val mlBridge = mockk<MlBridge>(relaxed = true) {
                coEvery { initialize() } returns PrismResult.Success(Unit)
                every { listMissingModelAssets() } returns emptyList()
            }
            val vm = HomeViewModel(preferences, mlBridge, mockk(relaxed = true))

            vm.uiState.test {
                // First emission is the combine() result once flows
                // produce their initial values.
                val state = awaitItem()
                state.contentState.totalSessionsCompleted shouldBe 7
                state.contentState.totalBytesTransferred shouldBe 1_048_576L
                state.contentState.preferredMode shouldBe TransferMode.KINETIC
                cancelAndIgnoreRemainingEvents()
            }
        }
    }
})
```

### 12.3.2 `app/src/test/kotlin/com/prism/ot/data/preferences/PrismPreferencesTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.data.preferences

import androidx.datastore.core.DataStoreFactory
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.PreferenceDataStoreFactory
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.emptyPreferences
import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import java.io.File
import java.nio.file.Files

/**
 * DataStore tests using a real file in a temp directory.
 *
 * DataStore is best tested against a real store rather than a mock: the
 * interesting behaviour (atomic writes, flow re-emission, clamping) lives
 * in the library, and a mock would test only the call forwarding.
 */
class PrismPreferencesTest : FreeSpec({

    lateinit var scope: CoroutineScope
    lateinit var tempDir: File
    lateinit var store: androidx.datastore.core.DataStore<androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences>
    lateinit var preferences: PrismPreferences

    beforeEach {
        scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)
        tempDir = Files.createTempDirectory("prism-prefs").toFile()
        store = PreferenceDataStoreFactory.create(
            scope = scope,
            produceFile = { File(tempDir, "test.preferences_pb") },
        )
        preferences = PrismPreferences(
            context = mockkContextWithStore(store),
        )
    }

    afterEach {
        scope.cancel()
        tempDir.deleteRecursively()
    }

    "defaults are sensible" {
        runTest {
            preferences.preferredFacesPerMass.first() shouldBe 16
            preferences.preferredFaceBytes.first() shouldBe 7_500
            preferences.autoDetectMode.first() shouldBe true
        }
    }

    "face bytes are clamped to the valid range" {
        runTest {
            preferences.setPreferredFaceBytes(1)
            preferences.preferredFaceBytes.first() shouldBe 4_096
            preferences.setPreferredFaceBytes(99_999)
            preferences.preferredFaceBytes.first() shouldBe 24_576
        }
    }

    "session completion accumulates bytes and count" {
        runTest {
            preferences.recordSessionCompletion(1_000)
            preferences.recordSessionCompletion(2_000)
            preferences.totalBytesTransferred.first() shouldBe 3_000L
            preferences.totalSessionsCompleted.first() shouldBe 2
        }
    }
})

/**
 * Helper that wraps a pre-built DataStore in a minimal Context mock.
 * `PrismPreferences` takes a Context and resolves the store via the
 * `prismDataStore` delegate; in tests we bypass the delegate by
 * providing the store directly through a test seam.
 */
private fun mockkContextWithStore(
    store: androidx.datastore.core.DataStore<androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences>,
): android.content.Context = io.mockk.mockk {
    io.mockk.every {
        // The production class reads the store through a Context extension;
        // the test seam exposes it via a package-private field.
        // See PrismPreferencesTestSeam below.
    }
}

/**
 * To make the above test compilable without reflection, `PrismPreferences`
 * exposes a `@VisibleForTesting internal constructor(context, store)`.
 * This seam is the standard way to test DataStore-backed classes without
 * Robolectric.
 */
```

> **Implementation note:** add this secondary constructor to `PrismPreferences`:
> ```kotlin
> @VisibleForTesting
> internal constructor(
>     context: Context,
>     private val testStore: DataStore<Preferences>?,
> ) : this(context) { /* store resolution prefers testStore */ }
> ```
> The test above then constructs `PrismPreferences(mockk(), store)` directly instead of the helper shown.

## 12.4 Instrumented tests — Hilt replacement and session lifecycle

### 12.4.1 `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/com/prism/ot/HiltTestRunner.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner
import dagger.hilt.android.testing.HiltTestApplication

/**
 * Custom instrumented test runner.
 *
 * Hilt generates `HiltTestApplication` for instrumented tests. Without
 * this runner, the production `PrismApplication` is used and the test
 * graph is not installed, which produces "Hilt was not initialized"
 * failures. The runner is declared in `android.defaultConfig` as
 * `testInstrumentationRunner`.
 */
class HiltTestRunner : AndroidJUnitRunner() {
    override fun newApplication(
        cl: ClassLoader,
        className: String,
        context: Context,
    ): Application = super.newApplication(cl, HiltTestApplication::class.java.name, context)
}
```

### 12.4.2 `app/build.gradle.kts` — runner declaration

```kotlin
defaultConfig {
    testInstrumentationRunner = "com.prism.ot.HiltTestRunner"
}
```

### 12.4.3 `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/com/prism/ot/di/FakeCameraModule.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.di

import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraController
import com.prism.ot.capture.CameraSessionInfo
import com.prism.ot.core.PrismResult
import com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode
import dagger.Module
import dagger.Provides
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import dagger.hilt.testing.TestInstallIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import javax.inject.Singleton

/**
 * Replaces the real CameraX controller with a fake in instrumented tests.
 *
 * `@TestInstallIn` is preferred over `@UninstallModules` because it is
 * declarative and applies to every test in the module, which removes the
 * per-test teardown burden. The replacement module is compiled
 * only into `androidTest`, so production builds are unaffected.
 */
@Module
@TestInstallIn(
    components = [SingletonComponent::class],
    replaces = [CameraModule::class],
)
object FakeCameraModule {

    @Provides @Singleton
    fun provideFakeCameraController(): CameraController = FakeCameraController()
}

class FakeCameraController : CameraController {
    private val _sessionInfo = MutableStateFlow<CameraSessionInfo?>(null)
    private val _fps = MutableStateFlow(30f)

    override val sessionInfo = _sessionInfo
    override val actualFps = _fps

    override suspend fun bind(
        lifecycleOwner: androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner,
        previewView: androidx.camera.view.PreviewView,
        analyzer: com.prism.ot.capture.FrameAnalyzer,
        mode: TransferMode,
    ): PrismResult<CameraSessionInfo> {
        val info = CameraSessionInfo(
            selectedFpsRange = 30..30,
            supportedFpsRanges = listOf(30..30),
            sensorOrientationDegrees = 90,
            rollingShutterSkewNanos = 16_000_000L,
            isRgbaOutput = true,
        )
        _sessionInfo.value = info
        return PrismResult.Success(info)
    }

    override fun unbind() { _sessionInfo.value = null }
    override fun setTorch(enabled: Boolean) {}
    override fun setZoom(ratio: Float) {}
    override suspend fun focusAt(normalizedX: Float, normalizedY: Float) =
        PrismResult.Success(Unit)
}
```

### 12.4.4 `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/com/prism/ot/session/StaticSessionTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.session

import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
import dagger.hilt.android.testing.HiltAndroidRule
import dagger.hilt.android.testing.HiltAndroidTest
import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import io.kotest.matchers.types.shouldBeInstanceOf
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import javax.inject.Inject

/**
 * Instrumented session lifecycle test.
 *
 * Verifies that a StaticSession can be created, started, and cancelled
 * without a real camera. The FakeCameraModule supplies a deterministic
 * camera controller; the fake does not produce tiles, so the test asserts
 * lifecycle transitions only, not decoding.
 *
 * `@HiltAndroidTest` is mandatory on any test that uses Hilt injection;
 * `HiltAndroidRule` must be ordered before any rule that triggers
 * injection.
 */
@HiltAndroidTest
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class StaticSessionTest : FreeSpec({

    @get:Rule(order = 0)
    val hiltRule = HiltAndroidRule(this)

    @Inject lateinit var sessionFactory: SessionFactory

    beforeSpec { hiltRule.inject() }

    "session reaches Running and then Finished on cancel" {
        runBlocking {
            val session = sessionFactory.create(com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode.STATIC)

            withTimeout(10_000) {
                session.state.first { it is SessionState.Running }
            }

            session.cancel("test")

            withTimeout(10_000) {
                session.state.first { it == SessionState.Finished }
            }
            session.result.value.shouldBeInstanceOf<SessionResult.Cancelled>()
        }
    }

    "session can be stopped without a cancel" {
        runBlocking {
            val session = sessionFactory.create(com.prism.ot.data.model.TransferMode.STATIC)
            withTimeout(10_000) { session.state.first { it is SessionState.Running } }
            session.stop()
            withTimeout(10_000) { session.state.first { it == SessionState.Finished } }
        }
    }
})
```

### 12.4.5 `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/com/prism/ot/ui/HomeScreenTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.prism.ot.ui

import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsDisplayed
import androidx.compose.ui.test.junit4.createAndroidComposeRule
import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithText
import androidx.compose.ui.test.performClick
import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
import dagger.hilt.android.testing.HiltAndroidRule
import dagger.hilt.android.testing.HiltAndroidTest
import io.kotest.core.spec.style.FreeSpec
import io.kotest.matchers.shouldBe
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.runner.RunWith

/**
 * Compose UI test for the home screen.
 *
 * Uses `createAndroidComposeRule<HiltTestActivity>` rather than a plain
 * activity rule so Hilt can inject into the composition. The rule order
 * matters: `HiltAndroidRule` must run before the compose rule.
 */
@HiltAndroidTest
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class HomeScreenTest : FreeSpec({

    @get:Rule(order = 0)
    val hiltRule = HiltAndroidRule(this)

    @get:Rule(order = 1)
    val composeRule = createAndroidComposeRule<HiltTestActivity>()

    beforeSpec { hiltRule.inject() }

    "shows both scan actions" {
        composeRule.setContent {
            com.prism.ot.ui.theme.PrismTheme {
                com.prism.ot.ui.screens.home.HomeScreen(
                    onScanStatic = {},
                    onScanKinetic = {},
                    onOpenSettings = {},
                    onOpenResult = {},
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.onNodeWithText("Static scan").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText("Kinetic scan").assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    "static action invokes the callback" {
        var clicked = false
        composeRule.setContent {
            com.prism.ot.ui.theme.PrismTheme {
                com.prism.ot.ui.screens.home.HomeScreen(
                    onScanStatic = { clicked = true },
                    onScanKinetic = {},
                    onOpenSettings = {},
                    onOpenResult = {},
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.onNodeWithText("Static scan").performClick()
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        clicked shouldBe true
    }
})
